# Project Manual Testing Documentation
## Scoreboad — QA Manual + Technical Reference

> **Generated by static analysis of the codebase on 2026-08-20.**
> Everything below was read out of the source. Where a feature is *not* built,
> that is stated explicitly rather than assumed. No application code was changed
> to produce this document.
>
> **Build health at time of writing** (verified by running the commands):

> - `npm run lint` → **clean, zero warnings**
> - `npm run typecheck` → **clean, zero errors**

# 1. Project Overview

## 1.1 What this project does

**Scoreboad** is a multi-tenant, AI-assisted recruitment operating system —
an applicant tracking system (ATS) that carries a candidate from first resume to
signed onboarding documents, with AI assistance layered on top of (never instead
of) the manual workflow.

It covers the entire hiring lifecycle:

```
Job requisition
   → Candidate intake (manual, single resume upload, or bulk drop)
   → Resume text extraction + AI parsing + human review
   → Application created (candidate × job)
   → AI match scoring (deterministic + optional semantic)
   → AI voice screening call (Bolna) + AI screening report + human correction
   → Structured evaluations (phone / video / written assessment / director round)
   → Interviews, scheduling, calendar invite, structured feedback
   → Client submission + client feedback clock (agency mode only)
   → Hired
   → Onboarding document checklist → verified → Completed
```

Running alongside all of that:

- an **automation engine** (trigger → conditions → actions, with an approval queue,
  a daily cap, and an organization-wide kill switch),
- an **append-only activity/audit log**,
- **internal notifications** (in-app + optional email) and **candidate
  communications** (email + WhatsApp templates with a mandatory unsubscribe),
- **analytics** with CSV export and an "Ask Analytics AI" explainer,
- **settings & integrations** (Bolna, Google Calendar, Email, WhatsApp, LLM, n8n).

## 1.2 What problem it solves

Recruitment teams lose time on three things this product attacks directly:

| Problem | How the product answers it |
| --- | --- |
| Resumes arrive as unstructured files; someone re-types them | Text extraction + AI parsing into a reviewable structured diff |
| Early screening calls are repetitive and eat recruiter hours | Automated outbound voice screening with a consent disclosure, transcript, and AI report |
| "Where is everybody?" is answered by memory and spreadsheets | A stage pipeline with SLA aging, an attention queue, and an audit trail |
| Candidates go dark because nobody remembered to email them | Event-triggered templated messages with an opt-out that cannot be removed |
| Managers cannot see what is actually happening | Analytics with a funnel, time-to-hire, bottleneck detection, and honest "not enough data" states |

## 1.3 Who the users are

Four roles, defined as a PostgreSQL enum `public.org_role` and enforced in
**both** the API layer (`requireRole()`) and in Row-Level Security policies:

| Role | Who they are | Broad capability |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **owner** | Created the workspace | Everything. Only an Owner may grant/revoke Owner. At least one active Owner must always exist. |
| **admin** | Ops / hiring lead | Everything except changing Owner access and deleting the org. Manages settings, integrations, automations, templates. |
| **recruiter** | Day-to-day recruiter | Creates and works jobs, candidates, applications, interviews, clients. **Scoped to their own work** on Dashboard, Pipeline, Applications and Analytics. Cannot manage settings, automations, integrations, invites, or the audit log. |
| **viewer** | Read-only stakeholder | Reads almost everything. Cannot create, edit, send, upload, trigger AI writes, or export analytics. |

There is a **fifth party who is not a user**: the **candidate**. Candidates have
no login. They touch the product in exactly two places — they receive templated
emails/WhatsApp messages, and they can open the signed `/unsubscribe` link. That
page is the only public authenticated-by-token surface in the product.

## 1.4 Technologies used

| Layer | Technology |
| --- | --- |
| Framework | **Next.js 16.3.0** (App Router), React 19.2.8, TypeScript 5 |
| Edge auth | `proxy.ts` — Next 16's replacement for `middleware.ts` |
| Styling | **Bulma 1.0.4** + `app/globals.scss` (3,513 lines of design tokens & components), Sass |
| Icons | `lucide-react` |
| Fonts | `next/font` — Inter (body) + Bricolage Grotesque (display), self-hosted |
| Database | **Supabase / PostgreSQL** with Row-Level Security on every table |
| Auth | Supabase Auth (email+password, Google OAuth, magic/recovery links) |
| File storage | Supabase Storage — two **private** buckets: `resumes`, `onboarding-documents` |
| Server data access | `@supabase/ssr` server client (RLS-bound) + a narrowly-scoped service-role client |
| Document parsing | `unpdf` (PDF), `mammoth` (.docx), `word-extractor` (.doc) |
| LLM | OpenAI-compatible `/chat/completions` via `fetch` — no vendor SDK |
| Voice | **Bolna AI** (outbound screening calls) + signed webhook |
| Calendar | **Google Calendar** OAuth |
| Workflow handoff | **n8n** webhook action |
| Scheduling | **Vercel Cron** — `vercel.json` runs `GET /api/automations/sweep` hourly |
| Tests | **Vitest** — 50 files / 1114 unit tests over pure logic |

## 1.5 How the layers communicate

```
                       ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────┐
   Browser             │ React Server Components (default)            │
   ───────             │   - read data directly via lib/*/queries.ts  │
                       │   - RLS-bound Supabase server client         │
                       │ Client Components ("use client")             │
                       │   - forms, modals, filters, boards           │
                       │   - fetch() → /api/* → router.refresh()      │
                       │   - a few call Supabase Auth directly        │
                       └───────────────┬──────────────────────────────┘
                                       │
                         ┌─────────────▼─────────────┐
     proxy.ts  ────────► │ Session refresh + deny-by- │
   (every request)       │ default auth at the edge   │
                         └─────────────┬─────────────┘
                                       │
   ┌───────────────────────────────────▼───────────────────────────────┐
   │ app/api/**/route.ts  (83 route files)                             │
   │   requireMembership() / requireRole() / getCurrentOrganizationId()│
   │   → lib/tenant.ts resolves the tenant FROM THE SESSION ONLY       │
   │   → handleRouteError() normalises every error shape               │
   └───────────┬───────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────┘
               │                           │
     ┌─────────▼─────────┐       ┌─────────▼──────────────────────────┐
     │ lib/ai/*          │       │ lib/integrations/{bolna,calendar,  │
     │ ONE provider      │       │ email,llm,n8n,whatsapp}            │
     │ boundary          │       │ connect / test / getStatus /       │
     │ (provider.ts)     │       │ disconnect — AES-GCM credentials   │
     └─────────┬─────────┘       └─────────┬──────────────────────────┘
               │                           │
               ▼                           ▼
        OpenAI-compatible          Bolna / Google / Resend-compatible /
        /chat/completions          Meta WhatsApp Cloud / n8n
               │
   ┌───────────▼───────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
   │ Supabase PostgreSQL — 42 tables, every one carries                │
   │ organization_id + RLS. Triggers enforce cross-row invariants.     │
   │ Storage: resumes (private), onboarding-documents (private)        │
   └───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                       ▲
                       │  POST /api/webhooks/bolna  (HMAC-signed, no session)
                       └─ Bolna call outcome / transcript / recording
```

**Crucial architectural fact for testers:** the browser holds an *authenticated
PostgREST client*. Any signed-in user can, in principle, write directly to the
database and skip an API route entirely. This is why every meaningful rule is
enforced **twice** — once in the route handler (for a friendly error message) and
once in an RLS policy or database trigger (for the actual security boundary).
When you test permissions, a passing UI test is only half the test.

## 1.6 The complete application flow

```
1.  Visitor lands on /            → redirect /dashboard
2.  proxy.ts sees no session      → redirect /login?next=/dashboard
3.  /signup → Supabase signUp     → email confirmation (if enabled)
4.  /auth/callback?code=…         → exchangeCodeForSession → /onboarding
5.  /onboarding                   → create_organization_and_owner() RPC
                                     → org + owner membership in one transaction
                                  → optional AI setup recommendations (Owner/Admin)
                                  → agency_mode chosen here (clients on/off)
6.  /dashboard                    → KPI tiles, attention queue, daily AI brief
7.  /jobs/new                     → paste JD → AI extract → review → save
8.  /jobs/[id]                    → Hiring Stages config, Add candidates (bulk intake)
9.  /candidates/new  OR bulk drop → dedupe check → candidate record
10. /candidates/[id]/resume       → upload → extract → AI parse → review diff → apply
11. /applications/new             → candidate × job → application at stage "applied"
12. /applications/[id]            → match score, stage moves, notes, timeline
13. …/screening-call              → start Bolna call (consent disclosure is structural)
    ← POST /api/webhooks/bolna    → transcript + status + consent recorded
14. …/screening-report            → AI summary → human corrects → ai_* columns frozen
15. …/match, …/interview-brief    → AI reading aids, nothing saved
16. Evaluations logged per stage  → verdict + next action
17. /interviews                   → schedule (+ optional Google Calendar invite)
                                  → structured feedback → trigger completes interview
18. …/submission (agency mode)    → draft → SEND → client feedback clock opens
19. Stage → hired                 → DB trigger creates onboarding_record + checklist
20. /hires/[id]                   → upload / verify / reject documents
                                  → all required verified → Mark complete
21. Throughout: automations fire, notifications land, activity_events append,
    candidate messages send with an unmovable unsubscribe footer,
    analytics recompute from the same query layer the screen uses.
```

# 2. Architecture Overview

## 2.1 Tenant isolation — the single most important rule

`lib/tenant.ts` is the contract every module inherits:

| Function | Used by | On failure |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `getCurrentUser()` | anything | returns `null`; **self-heals** a valid session with no `public.users` row by calling the `ensure_current_user_profile()` RPC (prevents an infinite redirect loop) |
| `requireCurrentUser()` | API routes | throws `TenantError(401)` |
| `requireMembership()` | API routes | throws `TenantError(403)` if the user belongs to no org |
| `requireRole([...])` | API routes | throws `TenantError(403)` naming the required role and the caller's role |
| `requireMembershipOrRedirect()` | **pages** | `redirect("/login")` or `redirect("/onboarding")` — never throws |
| `getCurrentOrganizationId()` | API routes | the server-resolved tenant id |

The active organization comes from a cookie `active_organization_id`, but that
cookie is **a hint only** — it is validated against a real `organization_members`
row every time. A forged cookie yields the user's default org, never someone
else's tenant.

**Known trap (documented in `AGENTS.md`, worth re-testing):**
`organization_members`' SELECT policy deliberately exposes *every* member row of
an org you belong to (the team list needs it). Any query asking "what is *my*
membership?" **must** filter `user_id` explicitly or it picks up a teammate's
role. `getUserMemberships()` does filter correctly.

## 2.2 Layer-by-layer

**Edge (`proxy.ts` → `lib/supabase/session.ts`)**
Deny-by-default. Public paths are exactly:
`/login`, `/signup`, `/forgot-password`, `/reset-password`, `/invite`, `/unsubscribe`.
`/api/*` and `/auth/*` are passed through so they can return JSON 401s rather than
HTML redirects. A signed-in user hitting `/login` or `/signup` is bounced to
`/dashboard`. The `?next=` parameter is preserved and is validated as a
same-origin relative path (`/^\/(?!\/)/`) before use in both `LoginForm` and
`/auth/callback` — so it cannot become an open redirect.

**Pages** — Server Components by default, `export const dynamic = "force-dynamic"`
on every data-bearing page so "today" never goes stale. Data loads through
`lib/<module>/queries.ts`, never through client-side fetch effects. After a
mutation, client components call `router.refresh()`.

**API routes** — 83 `route.ts` files. Every one wraps its body in
`try { … } catch (error) { return handleRouteError(error) }`. `handleRouteError`
turns a `TenantError` into its own status and everything else into a **generic
500** so internals and raw provider/database errors never reach the browser.

**Database** — 42 tables, all with `organization_id` and RLS. 59 triggers enforce
invariants that a route handler cannot (see §Database Documentation). Helper
functions `is_org_member()` / `has_org_role()` are `SECURITY DEFINER` with a
pinned `search_path`, which is what lets them run inside another table's policy
without recursing.

**Service-role client (`lib/supabase/admin.ts`)** — bypasses RLS. It exists for
exactly two reasons: integration credentials are protected by a **column-level
REVOKE** so even an Owner's session cannot read the ciphertext, and the Bolna
webhook / cron sweep have no session at all. Every query made with it must filter
`organization_id` explicitly. It returns `null` when unconfigured, so features
degrade to "not configured" rather than crashing.

## 2.3 The AI safety model

```
Raw Data → AI → Structured Output → Validation → Human Review → Business Action
```

- `lib/ai/provider.ts` is the **only** file that talks to an LLM. There is
  deliberately **no generic `askAI()`** — each feature is a named function.
- Every AI function returns `AiResult<T>` = `{ok:true,data}` | `{ok:false,code,message}`
  with `code` ∈ `not_configured` | `provider_error` | `invalid_output`.
  AI failure is an **expected state**; the manual workflow must always remain usable.
- Output is JSON-mode, schema-validated by a `validate()` callback before it is
  ever rendered. Invalid output is rejected, not shown.
- 30-second abort budget. AI must never hang a request.
- **AI never writes to a trusted table directly.** `ai-action` endpoints return
  `{ data, saved: false }`.
- The screening report keeps `ai_*` columns frozen forever (a trigger refuses to
  update them) so "what did the AI actually say?" always has an answer.
- `lib/ai/numericGuard.ts` enforces that the Daily Brief never states a number
  that contradicts the KPI tiles — and that enforcement is unit-tested.

## 2.4 Fail-closed rules (safety-critical, test these hard)

| Rule | Where enforced |
| --- | --- |
| Integration credentials are AES-GCM encrypted; **no `INTEGRATION_ENCRYPTION_KEY` → adapters refuse to connect** | `lib/integrations/crypto.ts` |
| Bolna webhook: **no `BOLNA_WEBHOOK_SECRET` → 503, every request rejected**; otherwise constant-time HMAC over raw bytes | `app/api/webhooks/bolna/route.ts` |
| Cron sweep: **no `CRON_SECRET` → 503**, never open | `app/api/automations/sweep/route.ts` |
| Screening call consent disclosure cannot be disabled; `consent_confirmed` is stamped by a trigger; Module 9 refuses a transcript without it | `trg_screening_calls_consent`, `lib/screening/script.ts` |
| Screening retry cap: default 3, hard max 5, min delay 15 min | `lib/screening/retry.ts` |
| `callback_requested` is **never** auto-retried | `TERMINAL_STATUSES` |
| Unsubscribe footer is appended by the send pipeline *after* rendering — a template cannot delete it | `lib/communications/optout.ts` |
| No signing key → footer degrades to "reply and ask to be removed" rather than emitting a dead link | same |
| Message templates ship **inactive** — a product update never starts emailing candidates | `message_templates.active default false` |
| Automations always create as `draft`; `status` is not read from the create body at all | `POST /api/automations` |
| Organization-wide automation kill switch checked **before a single rule is loaded** | `lib/automations/engine.ts` |

**Not built (do not test, do not report as bugs):**
- Privacy & Compliance retrofit — organization deletion, full data export,
  individual erasure. `/settings/security` says so in plain words and shows no
  button that pretends otherwise.
- AI & Calling Cost tracking retrofit — no spend meter, no budget cap UI.
- Payments / billing — **does not exist anywhere in this codebase.**
- Panel-interview aggregated scoring, custom RBAC beyond the four roles,
  drag-and-drop on the pipeline board (buttons/dropdowns by design).
- Candidate-facing portal or login.

# 4. MODULE M01 — Authentication & Session

## 4.1 Purpose

Gets a real human into a real workspace, and keeps everyone else out. It is the
foundation every other module inherits: no page in the product renders without a
session, and no API route acts without one.

**Who uses it:** everybody, once, then invisibly on every request.
**Problem solved:** a multi-tenant product where the wrong person seeing the wrong
candidate's phone number is a legal problem, not just a bug.

## 4.2 UI Components

### `/login`
| UI Component | Purpose | User Action | Expected Result |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Logo (full lockup, 56px) | Brand anchor | — | Renders above the card, no layout shift |
| "Sign in" title + subtitle | Orientation | — | Reads "Welcome back to your recruitment workspace." |
| `FormError` banner | Shows auth failures | — | Hidden until an error exists |
| Work email input (`type=email`, `required`, `autoComplete=email`) | Identity | Type an address | Browser validates the shape |
| Password input (`type=password`, `required`, `autoComplete=current-password`) | Secret | Type | Masked |
| "Forgot your password?" link | Recovery | Click | → `/forgot-password` |
| **Sign in** button (primary, full width) | Submit | Click | Shows `is-loading` spinner, disabled while busy |
| **Continue with Google** button | OAuth | Click | Redirects to Google consent → `/auth/callback` |
| "Create one" link | Signup | Click | → `/signup` |
| `Suspense` + `SkeletonRows rows={3}` | Loading | — | Skeleton while the form (which reads `useSearchParams`) hydrates |

### `/signup`
| UI Component | Purpose | User Action | Expected Result |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Your name (`required`) | Profile name | Type | Stored in `raw_user_meta_data.name` |
| Work email (`required`) | Identity | Type | — |
| Password (`required`, `minLength=8`) | Secret | Type | Help text: "At least 8 characters." |
| **Create account** button | Submit | Click | Loading state |
| Confirmation panel (`.ai-panel`) | Post-signup | — | Replaces the form: "Check **{email}** for a confirmation link." |
| "Sign in" link | — | Click | → `/login` |

### `/forgot-password`
| UI Component | Purpose | User Action | Expected Result |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Work email input | Identity | Type | — |
| **Send reset link** button | Submit | Click | **Always** shows success — never confirms whether the address exists |
| Sent panel | Confirmation | — | "If an account exists for **{email}**, a reset link is on its way." |

### `/reset-password`
| UI Component | Purpose | User Action | Expected Result |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| New password (`minLength=8`) | Secret | Type | — |
| Confirm new password | Match check | Type | Mismatch → "Those passwords don't match." |
| **Save new password** button | Submit | Click | → `/dashboard` on success |

### Sign out
`components/SignOutButton.tsx`, reachable from the account menu (avatar, top-right).

## 4.3 Complete User Flow — Sign up

```
Step 1  User opens /signup
Step 2  User types name, email, password (≥8 chars)
Step 3  User clicks "Create account"
Step 4  FRONTEND validates password length locally (before any network call)
Step 5  supabase.auth.signUp() is called DIRECTLY from the browser
        (this is not an API route of ours)
Step 6  Supabase creates auth.users row
Step 7  DB TRIGGER on_auth_user_created → handle_new_auth_user()
        inserts public.users (auth_id, name, email, avatar_url)
        (skips silently if the identity has no email — phone-only auth)
Step 8  If email confirmation is ON: no session returned →
        UI swaps to "Check {email} for a confirmation link"
        If confirmation is OFF: session returned → router.push("/onboarding")
Step 9  User clicks the emailed link → /auth/callback?code=…&next=/onboarding
Step 10 exchangeCodeForSession(code) → session cookie set
Step 11 `next` is validated as same-origin relative → redirect /onboarding
```

**USER ACTION** → **FRONTEND** (local length check) → **SUPABASE AUTH** (no API
route of ours) → **DB TRIGGER** (`public.users` row) → **EMAIL** → **/auth/callback**
(code exchange) → **UI RESULT** (/onboarding).

## 4.4 Complete User Flow — Sign in

```
Step 1  User opens a protected page, e.g. /candidates
Step 2  proxy.ts runs, calls supabase.auth.getUser(), finds no user
Step 3  proxy.ts redirects to /login?next=/candidates
Step 4  LoginForm reads ?next, validates it matches /^\/(?!\/)/
Step 5  User enters credentials, clicks Sign in
Step 6  supabase.auth.signInWithPassword()
Step 7  On failure: generic message — "That email and password combination
        didn't work." (deliberately does NOT reveal whether the account exists)
Step 8  On success: router.push(next); router.refresh()
Step 9  proxy.ts now sees a session, allows the page
Step 10 Page calls requireMembershipOrRedirect()
        - no public.users row → self-heals via ensure_current_user_profile() RPC
        - no organization    → redirect /onboarding
        - otherwise          → render
```

## 4.5 Technical Flow

- **Session storage:** Supabase auth cookies, refreshed on *every* matched request
  by `updateSession()`. The matcher excludes `_next/static`, `_next/image`,
  `favicon.ico` and image extensions.
- **Self-healing profile:** if a session exists with no `public.users` row (signed
  up before migration 0001, or a window where the trigger was missing),
  `getCurrentUser()` calls the `ensure_current_user_profile()` RPC, which reads
  `auth.uid()` only and cannot create anyone else's profile. Without this the
  symptom was `ERR_TOO_MANY_REDIRECTS`.
- **Open-redirect defence:** `next` is regex-validated in *two* places —
  `LoginForm` and `app/auth/callback/route.ts`.

## 4.6 API Flow

| API | Method | Auth | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `/auth/callback` | GET | none (code is the credential) | `?code` + `?next`. Missing code → `/login?error=missing_code`. Exchange failure → `/login?error=auth_failed`. |

Login, signup, password reset and sign-out do **not** go through our API — they
call Supabase Auth directly from the browser.

## 4.7 Database Flow

```
auth.users  (Supabase-managed)
   │ INSERT triggers on_auth_user_created
   ▼
public.users (id, auth_id UNIQUE FK→auth.users ON DELETE CASCADE,
              name, email, avatar_url, created_at)
   │
   ▼ referenced by organization_members.user_id
```

## 4.8 Test Cases

| Test Case ID | Feature | Test Scenario | Steps to Test | Expected Result | Actual | Status | Priority |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| TC-AUTH-001 | Signup | Create a new workspace account | 1. Open `/signup` 2. Name "QA Tester" 3. New email 4. Password `Password123` 5. Click Create account | Either "Check {email} for a confirmation link" panel, or redirect to `/onboarding` | | | Critical |
| TC-AUTH-002 | Signup | Password below minimum | Same, password `abc` | Inline error "Use at least 8 characters for your password." No network call made | | | High |
| TC-AUTH-003 | Signup | Duplicate email | Sign up with an address that already has an account | Supabase's own error message is shown in the `FormError` banner (this one is **not** genericised) | | | High |
| TC-AUTH-004 | Signup | Empty required fields | Submit with all fields blank | Browser `required` validation blocks submit | | | Medium |
| TC-AUTH-005 | Signup | Invalid email shape | Enter `notanemail` | Browser `type=email` validation blocks submit | | | Medium |
| TC-AUTH-006 | Login | Valid credentials | 1. `/login` 2. Valid email + password 3. Sign in | Redirect to `/dashboard` (or `?next` target); top nav renders with avatar | | | Critical |
| TC-AUTH-007 | Login | Wrong password | Valid email, wrong password | "That email and password combination didn't work." Still on `/login`. **Verify it does NOT say "no such user"** | | | Critical |
| TC-AUTH-008 | Login | Non-existent email | Unknown address + any password | **Identical** generic message to TC-AUTH-007 (account-existence must not leak) | | | Critical |
| TC-AUTH-009 | Login | Loading state | Click Sign in on a slow connection | Button shows `is-loading` and is `disabled`; a second click does nothing | | | High |
| TC-AUTH-010 | Login | Deep-link preservation | 1. Signed out 2. Open `/candidates/…` 3. Get bounced to login 4. Sign in | Lands on `/candidates/…`, not `/dashboard` | | | High |
| TC-AUTH-011 | Login | Open-redirect attempt | Open `/login?next=https://evil.example.com` then sign in | Lands on `/dashboard`. **Never** navigates off-origin | | | Critical |
| TC-AUTH-012 | Login | Protocol-relative redirect | `/login?next=//evil.example.com` | Same — `/dashboard` | | | Critical |
| TC-AUTH-013 | Login | Already signed in | Signed in, navigate to `/login` | Immediately redirected to `/dashboard` by proxy.ts | | | Medium |
| TC-AUTH-014 | Login | Error banner from callback | Open `/login?error=auth_failed` | Banner: "We couldn't complete that sign-in. Please try again." | | | Low |
| TC-AUTH-015 | Google OAuth | Happy path | Click Continue with Google, approve | Returns via `/auth/callback`, lands on `/dashboard` or `/onboarding` | | | High |
| TC-AUTH-016 | Google OAuth | Provider not enabled in Supabase | Click Continue with Google | "Google sign-in isn't available right now. Use your email and password instead." | | | Medium |
| TC-AUTH-017 | Callback | Missing code | Open `/auth/callback` with no params | Redirect `/login?error=missing_code`, banner shown | | | Medium |
| TC-AUTH-018 | Callback | Reused / expired code | Open a callback URL twice | Second attempt → `/login?error=auth_failed` | | | High |
| TC-AUTH-019 | Forgot password | Existing account | Enter a real address, Send reset link | "If an account exists for {email}, a reset link is on its way." Email arrives | | | High |
| TC-AUTH-020 | Forgot password | Non-existent account | Enter a fake address | **Exactly the same** success panel. No email. (enumeration defence) | | | Critical |
| TC-AUTH-021 | Reset password | Valid link | Open the emailed link → `/reset-password`, enter matching passwords ≥8 | Redirect to `/dashboard`, signed in with the new password | | | Critical |
| TC-AUTH-022 | Reset password | Mismatched confirmation | Enter two different passwords | "Those passwords don't match." No network call | | | High |
| TC-AUTH-023 | Reset password | Too short | Enter `abc` twice | "Use at least 8 characters." | | | Medium |
| TC-AUTH-024 | Reset password | Expired link | Wait past expiry, then submit | "That reset link has expired. Request a new one from the sign-in page." | | | High |
| TC-AUTH-025 | Reset password | Opened without a recovery session | Open `/reset-password` directly with no session | Page renders (it is a public path) but submit fails with the expired-link message | | | Medium |
| TC-AUTH-026 | Route protection | Every protected path | Signed out, try `/dashboard` `/jobs` `/candidates` `/applications` `/pipeline` `/interviews` `/clients` `/automations` `/analytics` `/hires` `/notifications` `/audit-log` `/settings` `/team/invite` `/screening-calls` | **Every one** redirects to `/login?next=…` | | | Critical |
| TC-AUTH-027 | Route protection | Public paths reachable | Signed out, open `/login` `/signup` `/forgot-password` `/reset-password` `/invite/anything` `/unsubscribe` | All render, no redirect | | | High |
| TC-AUTH-028 | Route protection | API returns JSON not HTML | Signed out: `curl -i http://localhost:3000/api/jobs` | HTTP 401 with a JSON body `{"error":"Not authenticated"}` — **not** a 307 to `/login` | | | Critical |
| TC-AUTH-029 | Sign out | Happy path | Avatar menu → Sign out | Redirected to `/login`; pressing Back does not restore the app | | | Critical |
| TC-AUTH-030 | Session | Refresh persistence | Sign in, hard-refresh (Cmd+R) five times | Stays signed in, no flicker to `/login` | | | High |
| TC-AUTH-031 | Session | Expiry mid-session | Delete the `sb-*` auth cookies in DevTools, then click any nav link | Redirected to `/login?next=<that page>` | | | High |
| TC-AUTH-032 | Session | Multiple tabs | Sign out in tab A, click a link in tab B | Tab B redirects to `/login` on the next navigation | | | Medium |
| TC-AUTH-033 | Session | Two accounts, two browsers | Sign in as A in Chrome, B in Firefox, act in both | No cross-contamination of data or identity | | | High |
| TC-AUTH-034 | Session | Profile self-heal | Delete the user's `public.users` row in SQL, then load `/dashboard` | Row is recreated by `ensure_current_user_profile()`; user lands on `/onboarding`. **No redirect loop** | | | High |
| TC-AUTH-035 | Config | Supabase unconfigured | Blank `NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL`, restart, open `/login` | Actionable error naming the exact env vars — **not** an opaque network failure | | | Medium |
| TC-AUTH-036 | Config | Placeholder value | Set URL to `https://placeholder.supabase.co` | Same actionable error (placeholder is detected, not just emptiness) | | | Medium |

## 4.9 UI/UX Checklist — M01

- [ ] `/login` `/signup` `/forgot-password` `/reset-password` all render centred with the full logo lockup
- [ ] No horizontal scrollbar at 375px, 768px, 1024px, 1440px
- [ ] Tab order: email → password → forgot link → Sign in → Continue with Google
- [ ] Enter in any field submits the form
- [ ] Labels are `<label for>`-associated (screen-reader clickable)
- [ ] Password fields never reveal characters
- [ ] Error banner appears **above** the fields and is announced
- [ ] Submit buttons show `is-loading` and are `disabled` while busy (double-click safe)
- [ ] Fonts load with no layout shift (Inter + Bricolage via `next/font`)
- [ ] Browser back after sign-in does not show a cached authenticated page
- [ ] Autofill from the password manager populates both fields correctly
- [ ] Theme colour `#004CF5` shows in the Android Chrome address bar

## 4.10 Edge Cases — M01

| ID | Edge case | Expected |
| --- | --- | --- |
| EC-AUTH-01 | Email with `+tag` (`qa+1@x.com`) | Accepted end to end |
| EC-AUTH-02 | 320-character email | Accepted or cleanly rejected — never a 500 |
| EC-AUTH-03 | Unicode name "測試 用戶" | Stored and rendered correctly in the avatar initials |
| EC-AUTH-04 | Leading/trailing spaces in email | Trimmed or rejected — never creates a second account |
| EC-AUTH-05 | 200-character password | Accepted |
| EC-AUTH-06 | Rapid double-click on Sign in | One request; button disabled |
| EC-AUTH-07 | Network offline mid-submit | Error banner, form stays filled, retry works |
| EC-AUTH-08 | Browser back from `/onboarding` to `/signup` | Redirected to `/dashboard` (already has a session) |
| EC-AUTH-09 | Sign in with an unconfirmed email (confirmation enabled) | Supabase's "Email not confirmed" surfaces; user not signed in |
| EC-AUTH-10 | Same recovery link opened twice | Second use rejected with the expired-link message |

## 4.11 Known Risks — M01

1. **Signup errors are not genericised.** `SignupForm` renders
   `signUpError.message` verbatim. If Supabase returns "User already registered",
   `/signup` becomes an account-enumeration oracle even though `/login` and
   `/forgot-password` were carefully hardened against exactly that.
2. **No client-side rate limiting** on login attempts. You are relying entirely
   on Supabase's built-in throttling.
3. **Password policy is length-only** (≥8). No complexity, no breach-list check.
4. **No "sign out everywhere"** / active-session list.

# 5. MODULE M02 — Organization & Workspace Onboarding

## 5.1 Purpose

Turns a bare authenticated user into a tenant. Creates the organization, makes
the creator its Owner, captures the facts every later module reads (timezone,
industry, size, **agency mode**), and optionally asks the AI for setup
recommendations that are shown but never auto-applied.

**Who uses it:** whoever creates the workspace (becomes Owner); Owners/Admins
later, from `/settings/organization`.
**Problem solved:** a product where "today" means different things to different
people, and where an in-house team is shown a Clients module that is empty by
definition.

## 5.2 UI Components

### `/onboarding` — a 3-step wizard (`OnboardingFlow.tsx`)

| Step | UI Component | Purpose | User Action | Expected Result |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| — | "Step N of 3" indicator | Progress | — | Reads 1, 2 or 3 |
| 1 | `<h1>` "Name your workspace" | — | — | — |
| 1 | Organization name input (`required`) | Tenant name | Type | — |
| 1 | Country input | Metadata | Type | Optional |
| 1 | Timezone select | **Drives every date calculation** | Choose | Defaults `Asia/Kolkata` |
| 1 | **Continue** button (primary) | Creates the org | Click | Calls `POST /api/organizations`, advances to step 2 |
| 2 | `<h1>` "Tell us about your hiring" | — | — | — |
| 2 | Industry input | placeholder "e.g. IT services, BFSI, Manufacturing" | Type | Optional |
| 2 | Size select | Headcount band | Choose | Optional |
| 2 | **"Who do you hire for?"** radio pair | **Agency mode** | Choose | "We recruit for client companies" (shows Clients) / "We hire for ourselves" (hides Clients) |
| 2 | Hiring focus textarea | AI input | Type | placeholder "e.g. IT recruitment, mostly Java, React, DevOps" |
| 2 | **Get AI suggestions** button | AI | Click | Loading, then step 3 with an `.ai-panel` |
| 2 | **Skip** button | Bypass AI | Click | Straight to step 3, no AI call |
| 3 | `<h1>` "Review and finish" | — | — | — |
| 3 | AI panel with 5 `SuggestionList`s | Pipeline stages / Screening questions / Interview rounds / Candidate fields / Automation ideas | Read | "These are suggestions only — nothing is saved until you finish." |
| 3 | AI error + **Retry AI suggestions** | Failure recovery | Click | Re-runs the AI call |
| 3 | **Finish setup** button | Commit | Click | `POST /api/organizations/[id]/onboarding` → `/dashboard` |
| 3 | **Back** button | — | Click | Returns to step 2 |

### `/organizations/switch` (`OrganizationSwitcher.tsx`)
Lists every active membership. Shown in the account menu only when
`memberships.length > 1`. A "Create a workspace" primary button links to `/onboarding`.

### `/settings/organization` (`OrganizationForm.tsx`)
Owner/Admin only. Name, industry, size, country, timezone, agency mode, logo URL,
brand colour (`placeholder="#004CF5"`, validated `^#[0-9A-Fa-f]{6}$`). Explicit **Save**.

## 5.3 Complete User Flow — first workspace

```
Step 1  Confirmed user lands on /onboarding
        (page first checks: already onboarded? → redirect /dashboard)
Step 2  Types workspace name, picks country + timezone
Step 3  Clicks Continue
Step 4  FRONTEND: POST /api/organizations {name, country, timezone}
Step 5  BACKEND: requireCurrentUser(); validates name 1–120 chars
Step 6  DATABASE: create_organization_and_owner() RPC (SECURITY DEFINER)
          a. defensive upsert into public.users (in case the trigger lagged)
          b. INSERT organizations
          c. INSERT organization_members (role='owner', status='active')
        Both writes happen inside ONE function — organization_members has no
        client-facing INSERT policy, so this RPC is the only way in.
Step 7  RESPONSE 201 {data: organization}
Step 8  UI advances to step 2
Step 9  User answers industry / size / who-do-you-hire-for / focus
Step 10 Clicks "Get AI suggestions"
Step 11 POST /api/organizations/[id]/ai-action  (Owner/Admin only)
        → lib/ai/generateOnboardingRecommendations()
        → returns {data, saved:false}. NOTHING is written.
Step 12 Step 3 renders the suggestions read-only
Step 13 Clicks "Finish setup"
Step 14 POST /api/organizations/[id]/onboarding
        → validates :id === session-resolved org id (else 404)
        → parseAgencyMode() distinguishes absent from false
        → UPDATE organizations SET industry, size, agency_mode,
             onboarding_answer, onboarding_completed_at = now()
Step 15 Redirect /dashboard; nav now shows (or hides) Clients per agency_mode
```

## 5.4 Technical / API Flow

| API | Method | Auth | Request | Response | Errors |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `/api/organizations` | GET | signed-in | — | user's memberships | 401 |
| `/api/organizations` | POST | signed-in | `{name, industry?, size?, country?, timezone?}` | 201 `{data}` | 400 name empty / >120 chars / invalid JSON |
| `/api/organizations/[id]` | GET | member | — | `{data}` | 404 if `:id` ≠ session org |
| `/api/organizations/[id]` | PATCH | **owner/admin** | partial org fields | `{data}` | 400 empty field / bad `agency_mode`; 403 role; 404 wrong id |
| `/api/organizations/[id]` | DELETE | — | — | **Always refuses** — deletion belongs to the unbuilt Privacy retrofit | — |
| `/api/organizations/[id]/onboarding` | POST | **owner/admin** | `{industry, size, agency_mode, onboarding_answer}` | `{data}` | 400/403/404 |
| `/api/organizations/[id]/ai-action` | POST | **owner/admin** | `{answer}` | `{data, saved:false}` | 400 empty answer; AI codes mapped to status |
| `/api/organizations/switch` | POST | signed-in | `{organization_id}` | sets `active_organization_id` cookie | 400 missing id; 404 not a membership |

## 5.5 Database Flow

```
USER ACTION  "Continue"
 → API       POST /api/organizations
 → BACKEND   requireCurrentUser() + name validation
 → DATABASE  rpc create_organization_and_owner()
               INSERT public.users (upsert, defensive)
               INSERT public.organizations
               INSERT public.organization_members (owner, active)
 → RESPONSE  201 {data: organization}
 → UI        wizard step 2
```

`organizations` columns: `id, name, industry, size, country, timezone (NOT NULL
DEFAULT 'Asia/Kolkata'), agency_mode (NOT NULL DEFAULT true), automations_enabled,
onboarding_answer, onboarding_completed_at, created_at`.

**Agency-mode read rule:** always through `isAgencyMode()`, which returns
`organization?.agency_mode !== false`. An unmigrated/absent value reads as
**agency**, because that is what every org was before the flag existed. Reading it
the other way would silently blank the Clients module out of a live agency.

## 5.6 Test Cases

| Test Case ID | Feature | Test Scenario | Steps | Expected Result | Actual | Status | Priority |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| TC-ORG-001 | Onboarding | Create first workspace | Sign up → `/onboarding` → name "QA Agency" → India / Asia/Kolkata → Continue | Step 2 appears; org + owner membership rows exist | | | Critical |
| TC-ORG-002 | Onboarding | Blank workspace name | Leave name empty, Continue | Blocked (`required`) or 400 "Organization name is required." | | | High |
| TC-ORG-003 | Onboarding | 121-character name | Paste a 121-char name | 400 "Organization name is too long." | | | Medium |
| TC-ORG-004 | Onboarding | Whitespace-only name | Enter `"   "` | Rejected — the DB also has `check (length(btrim(name)) > 0)` | | | High |
| TC-ORG-005 | Onboarding | Timezone is respected | Choose `America/New_York`, finish, open `/dashboard` | Dashboard header shows the New York date and the timezone name | | | Critical |
| TC-ORG-006 | Onboarding | Skip AI | Step 2 → Skip | Step 3 with **no** AI panel, Finish still works | | | High |
| TC-ORG-007 | Onboarding | AI suggestions happy path | Fill focus, Get AI suggestions | `.ai-panel` with 5 lists + a rationale; "nothing is saved until you finish" | | | High |
| TC-ORG-008 | Onboarding | AI not configured | Unset `OPENAI_API_KEY`, restart, Get AI suggestions | Friendly `not_configured` message + **Retry AI suggestions** button. Wizard still completable | | | Critical |
| TC-ORG-009 | Onboarding | AI provider down | Point `AI_BASE_URL` at a dead host | "The AI service is temporarily unavailable. You can continue manually and retry later." | | | High |
| TC-ORG-010 | Onboarding | Empty AI answer | Click Get AI suggestions with focus blank | 400 "Tell us what kind of hiring you do so we can suggest defaults." | | | Medium |
| TC-ORG-011 | Onboarding | Agency mode ON | Choose "We recruit for client companies", finish | **Clients** appears in the nav; `/clients` loads | | | Critical |
| TC-ORG-012 | Onboarding | Agency mode OFF | Choose "We hire for ourselves", finish | **Clients is absent** from the nav and from the Analytics client filter | | | Critical |
| TC-ORG-013 | Onboarding | Already onboarded | Onboarded user opens `/onboarding` | Redirected to `/dashboard` | | | High |
| TC-ORG-014 | Onboarding | Back button on step 3 | Click Back | Returns to step 2 with values intact | | | Medium |
| TC-ORG-015 | Onboarding | Refresh mid-wizard | Complete step 1, refresh | Wizard resumes at step 2 (the org already exists) | | | High |
| TC-ORG-016 | Settings | Edit organization | `/settings/organization` → change name → Save | Success; name updates in the account menu after refresh | | | High |
| TC-ORG-017 | Settings | Recruiter blocked | Sign in as recruiter, open `/settings/organization` | Section is **hidden** from the settings sidebar, not greyed out; direct URL shows a restricted panel | | | Critical |
| TC-ORG-018 | Settings | Viewer blocked | Same as a viewer | Same | | | Critical |
| TC-ORG-019 | Settings | Recruiter via API | `curl -X PATCH /api/organizations/{id} -d '{"name":"hax"}'` as a recruiter | **403** | | | Critical |
| TC-ORG-020 | Settings | Invalid brand colour | Enter `blue` | Rejected — DB check is `^#[0-9A-Fa-f]{6}$` | | | Medium |
| TC-ORG-021 | Settings | Empty field on PATCH | Send `{"name":""}` | 400 "name cannot be empty." | | | Medium |
| TC-ORG-022 | Settings | agency_mode wrong type | Send `{"agency_mode":"yes"}` | 400 "agency_mode must be true or false." | | | Medium |
| TC-ORG-023 | Settings | agency_mode absent ≠ false | PATCH only `{"name":"X"}` | `agency_mode` is **unchanged**, not set to false | | | High |
| TC-ORG-024 | Tenant | Cross-tenant id | As org A, `GET /api/organizations/{orgB-id}` | **404** "Organization not found." — never 403 (would confirm existence) | | | Critical |
| TC-ORG-025 | Switch | Two workspaces | Belong to two orgs → account menu → Organizations | Switcher lists both; the current one is marked | | | High |
| TC-ORG-026 | Switch | Actually switches | Pick the other org | Cookie set; dashboard, jobs and candidates all show org B's data only | | | Critical |
| TC-ORG-027 | Switch | Single membership | Belong to one org | The switcher entry is **not shown** in the account menu | | | Medium |
| TC-ORG-028 | Switch | Forged cookie | Edit `active_organization_id` in DevTools to another org's UUID, reload | Falls back to your default org. **No data from the forged org appears** | | | Critical |
| TC-ORG-029 | Switch | Non-member org | `POST /api/organizations/switch {"organization_id":"<foreign uuid>"}` | 404 "Organization not found." | | | Critical |
| TC-ORG-030 | Delete | Organization deletion | `DELETE /api/organizations/{id}` | Refused with an explanatory message — deletion is part of the unbuilt Privacy retrofit | | | Medium |

## 5.7 UI/UX Checklist — M02

- [ ] Step indicator reads 1 → 2 → 3 and never skips
- [ ] Continue / Get AI suggestions / Finish all show `is-loading` and disable
- [ ] AI panel is visually distinct (`.ai-panel`) so suggestions are not mistaken for saved data
- [ ] "These are suggestions only" copy is present whenever suggestions render
- [ ] AI error uses `role="alert"` and offers Retry
- [ ] The timezone select is searchable / long enough to find a zone
- [ ] Wizard is usable at 375px width
- [ ] `/settings/organization` shows an explicit **Save**, never auto-saves
- [ ] Settings sections a role cannot open are **absent** from the sidebar
- [ ] Brand colour field shows a live swatch (if implemented) and rejects bad hex

## 5.8 Edge Cases — M02

| ID | Edge case | Expected |
| --- | --- | --- |
| EC-ORG-01 | Emoji in workspace name | Stored and rendered |
| EC-ORG-02 | Two workspaces with the same name (different owners) | Allowed — no global uniqueness on `organizations.name` |
| EC-ORG-03 | Timezone changed after data exists | Dashboard/analytics "today" shifts; historical timestamps do not move |
| EC-ORG-04 | Wizard abandoned after step 1 | Org exists with `onboarding_completed_at = NULL`; user is sent back to `/onboarding` next visit |
| EC-ORG-05 | AI returns malformed JSON | `invalid_output` → "The AI response was incomplete or in an unexpected format." |
| EC-ORG-06 | AI takes >30s | Aborted → "took too long to respond" |
| EC-ORG-07 | Switch org while a form is half-filled in another tab | The other tab writes to whichever org its own request resolves — verify no cross-tenant write |

## 5.9 Known Risks — M02

1. **`onboarding_completed_at` is only set at "Finish setup".** A user who creates
   the org and closes the tab has a real, usable organization that the wizard will
   keep prompting for. Verify this is not a loop.
2. **Timezone is a free select with no validation of IANA validity server-side** —
   check what happens if an invalid zone is POSTed directly.
3. `agency_mode` reads as *agency* when the column is missing. On a database where
   migration 0028 has not run, an in-house team sees Clients.

# 6. MODULE M03 — Team, Invites & Permissions

## 6.1 Purpose

Gets colleagues into the workspace at the right level of access, and keeps the
workspace from being orphaned.

**Who uses it:** Owners and Admins (invite/remove/change roles); everyone else
can see the member list.

## 6.2 UI Components

### `/team/invite` (`TeamManager.tsx`)
| UI Component | Purpose | User Action | Expected Result |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `<h2>` "Invite a teammate" | — | — | — |
| Email input (`#invite-email`) | Invitee | Type | — |
| Role select (`#invite-role`) | Access level | Choose | owner / admin / recruiter / viewer |
| **Send invite** button | Create | Click | `POST /api/invites` → row appears under Pending invites |
| `<h2>` "Pending invites" table | Track | — | email, role, expiry, Revoke |
| **Revoke** button | Cancel | Click | `DELETE /api/invites/:id` → status `revoked` |
| `<h2>` "Members" table | Roster | — | name/email, role select, Remove |
| Role select per member | Change role | Choose | `PATCH /api/members/:id` |
| **Remove** button | Soft-remove | Click | `DELETE /api/members/:id` → status `removed` |

### `/invite/[token]` — **public path**
Signed out → "Sign in" (with `?next=/invite/{token}`) and "Create account".
Signed in → "Accept as {email}" + **Accept invite** button → `POST /api/invites/accept`.

### `/settings/users`
The same roster inside the settings shell, visible to **every** role (it has no
`roles` restriction in `SettingsShell`).

## 6.3 Complete User Flow — invite → accept

```
USER ACTION   Owner types colleague@x.com, picks "recruiter", clicks Send invite
→ FRONTEND    POST /api/invites {email, role}
→ BACKEND     requireRole(["owner","admin"])
              validate email shape → 400 "Enter a valid email address."
              validate role       → 400 "Select a valid role."
              if role==="owner" and caller is admin → 403
                "Only an Owner can invite another Owner."
              if a pending invite already exists → 409
                "An invite for this email is already pending."
→ DATABASE    INSERT invites (token uuid default gen_random_uuid(),
              expires_at default now()+7 days, status 'pending')
→ RESPONSE    201 {data: invite}
→ UI          Row appears in Pending invites

USER ACTION   Colleague opens /invite/{token}
→ proxy.ts    /invite is a PUBLIC path — page renders signed out
→ FRONTEND    If signed out: Sign in (?next preserved) / Create account
              If signed in: "Accept as {email}" + Accept invite
→ API         POST /api/invites/accept {token}
→ BACKEND     requireCurrentUser() only — the TOKEN is the credential
→ DATABASE    rpc accept_invite(p_token) — SECURITY DEFINER, does ALL validation:
                token exists? still pending? not expired?
                upserts public.users, INSERTs organization_members,
                marks the invite 'accepted'
              organization_members has NO client-facing INSERT policy, so this
              RPC is the only path in.
→ RESPONSE    {data:{organization_id}} and the active-org cookie is set
→ UI          router.push("/dashboard") — the new member is in
```

## 6.4 API Flow

| API | Method | Auth | Errors |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `/api/members` | GET | any member | 400 invalid `status`/`role` filter |
| `/api/members/[id]` | PATCH `{role}` | **owner/admin** | 400 invalid role; 403 non-Owner touching Owner access; 404 not found/inactive; **409 last-Owner**; `{changed:false}` when the role is unchanged |
| `/api/members/[id]` | DELETE | **owner/admin** | 403 non-Owner removing an Owner; **409 last-Owner**; 404 |
| `/api/invites` | GET | **owner/admin** | 400 invalid status filter |
| `/api/invites` | POST `{email, role}` | **owner/admin** | 400 email/role; 403 admin inviting an Owner; 409 duplicate pending |
| `/api/invites/[id]` | DELETE | **owner/admin** | 404 "Pending invite not found." |
| `/api/invites/accept` | POST `{token}` | signed-in | 400 "Invite token is required."; 400 "This invite link is no longer valid. Ask for a new invite." |

## 6.5 Database Flow

`invites (id, organization_id, email CHECK position('@' in email) > 1, role,
token uuid UNIQUE, invited_by FK→users ON DELETE SET NULL, expires_at DEFAULT
now()+7 days, status CHECK IN ('pending','accepted','revoked','expired'), created_at)`

`organization_members (id, organization_id, user_id, role org_role, status
CHECK IN ('active','removed'), invited_by, joined_at, UNIQUE(organization_id,user_id))`

**Last-Owner protection is a database constraint trigger** (`enforce_owner_remains()`),
not just an API check — because a check-then-write across two statements is a
TOCTOU race. The route also counts remaining Owners for a friendly message and
translates the trigger's violation back into the same 409.

## 6.6 Test Cases

| Test Case ID | Feature | Test Scenario | Steps | Expected Result | Actual | Status | Priority |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| TC-TEAM-001 | Invite | Owner invites a recruiter | `/team/invite` → email → role recruiter → Send | 201; row in Pending invites with a 7-day expiry | | | Critical |
| TC-TEAM-002 | Invite | Admin invites an admin | As admin, invite role=admin | Allowed | | | High |
| TC-TEAM-003 | Invite | Admin invites an **Owner** | As admin, role=owner | **403** "Only an Owner can invite another Owner." | | | Critical |
| TC-TEAM-004 | Invite | Owner invites an Owner | As owner, role=owner | Allowed | | | High |
| TC-TEAM-005 | Invite | Recruiter tries to invite | Recruiter opens `/team/invite` | Form hidden/blocked; `POST /api/invites` → 403 | | | Critical |
| TC-TEAM-006 | Invite | Viewer tries to invite | Same as viewer | 403 | | | Critical |
| TC-TEAM-007 | Invite | Malformed email | `not-an-email` | 400 "Enter a valid email address." | | | High |
| TC-TEAM-008 | Invite | Invalid role | `POST /api/invites {"role":"superuser"}` | 400 "Select a valid role." | | | High |
| TC-TEAM-009 | Invite | Duplicate pending invite | Invite the same address twice | **409** "An invite for this email is already pending." | | | High |
| TC-TEAM-010 | Invite | Re-invite after revoke | Revoke, then invite the same address | Allowed (the old row is `revoked`, not `pending`) | | | Medium |
| TC-TEAM-011 | Invite | Revoke | Click Revoke on a pending row | Status → `revoked`; the link stops working | | | High |
| TC-TEAM-012 | Invite | Revoke an already-accepted invite | `DELETE /api/invites/{accepted-id}` | 404 "Pending invite not found." | | | Medium |
| TC-TEAM-013 | Accept | Signed-out invitee | Open `/invite/{token}` signed out | Page renders (public path) with Sign in / Create account | | | Critical |
| TC-TEAM-014 | Accept | Sign in preserves the invite | Click Sign in from the invite page, authenticate | Returns to `/invite/{token}` | | | High |
| TC-TEAM-015 | Accept | Happy path | Signed in → Accept invite | Joins the org at the invited role; lands on `/dashboard`; appears in Members | | | Critical |
| TC-TEAM-016 | Accept | Expired token | Set `expires_at` to the past in SQL, then accept | 400 "This invite link is no longer valid. Ask for a new invite." | | | Critical |
| TC-TEAM-017 | Accept | Revoked token | Revoke, then accept | Same 400 | | | Critical |
| TC-TEAM-018 | Accept | Reused token | Accept, then accept again | Second attempt rejected | | | High |
| TC-TEAM-019 | Accept | Garbage token | `/invite/00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000` → Accept | 400, no crash | | | High |
| TC-TEAM-020 | Accept | Non-UUID token | `/invite/hello` → Accept | 400, no 500 | | | High |
| TC-TEAM-021 | Accept | **Different email than invited** | Invite a@x.com, accept while signed in as b@x.com | Document the observed behaviour — the RPC keys on the token, and the UI only says "Use the email address the invite was sent to" | | | High |
| TC-TEAM-022 | Roles | Promote recruiter → admin | Members table → change the role select | Saved; the promoted user gains admin nav/settings after refresh | | | Critical |
| TC-TEAM-023 | Roles | Admin promotes someone to Owner | As admin, set role=owner | **403** "Only an Owner can change Owner access." | | | Critical |
| TC-TEAM-024 | Roles | Admin demotes an Owner | As admin, change an Owner's role | **403** | | | Critical |
| TC-TEAM-025 | Roles | No-op role change | Set the role it already has | `{changed:false}`, no audit noise | | | Low |
| TC-TEAM-026 | Roles | **Last Owner demotion** | Single Owner tries to become admin | **409** with the last-Owner message | | | Critical |
| TC-TEAM-027 | Roles | **Last Owner removal** | Single Owner tries to remove themselves | **409** | | | Critical |
| TC-TEAM-028 | Roles | Last Owner via direct PostgREST | From the browser console, `supabase.from('organization_members').update({role:'viewer'})` on the sole Owner | **Refused by the database trigger**, not just the route | | | Critical |
| TC-TEAM-029 | Members | Remove a member | Click Remove on a recruiter | Status `removed`; they lose access on their next navigation | | | Critical |
| TC-TEAM-030 | Members | Admin removes an Owner | As admin | **403** "Only an Owner can remove another Owner." | | | Critical |
| TC-TEAM-031 | Members | Removed member's data survives | Remove a recruiter who owns jobs | Jobs still exist; `owner_recruiter_id` handling is graceful | | | High |
| TC-TEAM-032 | Members | Removed member re-invited | Remove, then invite the same person | Works (unique constraint is on org+user, and status flips back) — verify | | | Medium |
| TC-TEAM-033 | Tenant | Cross-tenant member id | As org A, `PATCH /api/members/{orgB-member-id}` | 404 "Team member not found." | | | Critical |
| TC-TEAM-034 | Tenant | **Membership leak trap** | Sign in as a recruiter in an org with an Owner | The recruiter's own role resolves as `recruiter` — verify the account menu and permissions do **not** pick up the Owner's row | | | Critical |
| TC-TEAM-035 | Visibility | Members list for a viewer | Viewer opens `/settings/users` | Can read the roster; role selects and Remove are hidden/disabled | | | High |

## 6.7 UI/UX Checklist — M03

- [ ] Pending invites table shows the expiry date, not just "pending"
- [ ] Revoke asks for confirmation (or is clearly reversible by re-inviting)
- [ ] Role selects have an `aria-label` naming the member (they do: `Role for {email}`)
- [ ] Remove is visually distinguished as destructive
- [ ] Empty states: "no pending invites", "no members yet"
- [ ] Errors render inline near the action, not as a full-page state
- [ ] Table scrolls horizontally on mobile rather than overflowing the page

## 6.8 Known Risks — M03

1. **No invite email is sent.** `POST /api/invites` creates the row and returns
   `{data, invite_url}` — the link is handed to the Owner/Admin **once**, in the
   response, to share by hand. The code comment says Module 15 will email it
   later; it does not today. Confirm the UI actually surfaces `invite_url`,
   because if it does not, an invite is created that nobody can use.
2. **VERIFIED GAP — the invite token is not bound to the invited email address.**
   `accept_invite(p_token)` looks the invite up by token, checks only
   `status='pending' and expires_at > now()`, and then inserts a membership for
   **whoever is signed in** — it never compares `invites.email` with the caller's
   address. Anyone who obtains or guesses a token (it is a UUIDv4, so guessing is
   impractical, but forwarding is not) joins the organization at the invited
   role. The page text "Use the email address the invite was sent to" is advice,
   not enforcement. Test TC-TEAM-021 deliberately and file it.
3. **`ON CONFLICT … DO UPDATE SET role = excluded.role, status = 'active'`** in
   `accept_invite` means an existing member who accepts a *new* invite has their
   role **overwritten**, and a previously `removed` member is silently
   reactivated. An Admin holding a stale "viewer" invite link can demote
   themselves by accident; more importantly, a removed member who kept an old
   pending invite link can restore their own access. Worth testing directly.
4. Expired invites are never swept to `status='expired'`; expiry is only
   evaluated at accept time, so the Pending list can show links that no longer work.
