# 7. MODULE M04 — Dashboard

## 7.1 Purpose

The first screen after sign-in. Answers two questions in one glance: *what
happened today?* (six KPI tiles) and *what needs me right now?* (the attention
queue), with an optional AI-written Daily Operations Brief above them.

**Who uses it:** everyone. A **Recruiter sees only their own workload**
(`scopeForRole()` returns `"own"`); Owner/Admin/Viewer see the whole organization.

**Problem solved:** a dashboard that reports a fake `0` when its query failed is
worse than no dashboard — it is a false statement to a manager. This module's
whole design is about never doing that.

## 7.2 UI Components

| UI Component | Purpose | User Action | Expected Result |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Eyebrow "Dashboard" + `LayoutGrid` icon | Identity | — | Anchors the page |
| `<h1>` | Scope | — | Reads the **organization name** for owner/admin/viewer, **"Your workload"** for a recruiter |
| Meta line | Date + timezone | — | `formatDayInZone(timezone)` · timezone; recruiters also get "· showing only applications assigned to you" |
| **Invite your team** button (primary) | Growth | Click | → `/team/invite` |
| `DailyBrief` card | AI narrative | Click generate | `POST /api/dashboard/ai-action` |
| 6 KPI tiles (3×2 grid) | Metrics | — | See table below |
| `AttentionQueue` card | Work queue | Click a row | → the linked application |
| `QuickLinks` card ("Jump to") | Navigation | Click a row | Jobs / Candidates / Pipeline / Interviews |
| `DashboardSkeleton` | Loading | — | Six card skeletons + one list skeleton, streamed behind `<Suspense>` |

### The six KPI tiles (`TILE_ORDER`)

| Tile | Label | Icon | Source module | Row |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `newCandidates` | New candidates | `UserPlus` | M08 | 1 — work happening |
| `screeningsCompleted` | Screenings completed | `PhoneCall` | M12 | 1 |
| `interviewsToday` | Interviews today | `CalendarDays` | M16 | 1 |
| `overdueApplications` | Overdue applications | `Clock` | M10 | 2 — work going wrong |
| `failedCalls` | Failed screening calls | `PhoneOff` | M12 | 2 |
| `failedAutomations` | Failed automations | `ZapOff` | M18 | 2 |

**Tile colouring rule (test this):** `overdueApplications` gets an **amber** left
accent, `failedCalls` and `failedAutomations` get **red** — and **only when the
value is non-zero**. A permanently red tile teaches people to ignore red.

**Each tile has three possible states**, and they must look different:
`{status:"ok", value}` · `{status:"pending", module}` (the source table does not
exist) · `{status:"error"}` (the query genuinely failed).

## 7.3 Complete User Flow

```
Step 1  User signs in → /dashboard
Step 2  PAGE: requireMembershipOrRedirect() resolves the tenant + role
Step 3  resolveTimeZone(organization.timezone) — the ORG's zone, never the browser's
Step 4  Header paints immediately (org name, date, invite button)
Step 5  <Suspense> streams DashboardContent
Step 6  getDashboardData({organizationId, role, recruiterUserId, timezone})
          - dayRangeInZone() computes a HALF-OPEN [start, end) for "today"
          - six independent metric queries, each degrading on its own
          - the attention query, with its own status
Step 7  DailyBrief renders (with hasAnyData so it can refuse to narrate nothing)
Step 8  KpiTiles render, tinting only non-zero negative metrics
Step 9  AttentionQueue renders one of FOUR states: error / pending / empty / list
Step 10 User clicks an attention row → /applications/{id}
```

**USER ACTION** (open dashboard) → **SERVER COMPONENT** (no client fetch) →
**6 + 1 parallel Supabase queries** → **degradation logic per metric** →
**UI RESULT** (tiles + queue, each honestly labelled).

## 7.4 Technical Flow — the degradation rules (the heart of this module)

`isMissingRelation(error)` returns true **only** for `42P01` (undefined_table),
`PGRST205` (not in schema cache), `PGRST200` (missing embedded relationship), or
a message containing "does not exist" / "could not find the table" / "schema cache".

Anything else — an **RLS denial (42501)**, a statement timeout, a network fault —
is a real failure and surfaces as `status:"error"`. This narrowness is deliberate:
reporting a genuine bug as "not built yet" would hide it.

`OVERDUE_DAYS = 3` is the fallback threshold; `buildOverdueClause()` prefers the
per-stage SLA target from `pipeline_sla_config` via `targetDaysFor()`.

## 7.5 API Flow

| API | Method | Auth | Purpose | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `/api/dashboard` | GET | any member | KPI snapshot + attention queue as JSON | The page itself does **not** call this — it loads server-side. This exists for external/programmatic use. |
| `/api/dashboard/ai-action` | POST | any member | Generate the Daily Operations Brief | **Response-cached in-process**, keyed on the figures themselves, so the brief regenerates when a number moves. Cache is per-instance and does not survive a redeploy. |

The brief is derived **strictly** from the counts the dashboard already computed,
and `lib/ai/numericGuard.ts` enforces that it never states a number contradicting
a tile. That guard is unit-tested (`generateDailyBrief.test.ts`).

## 7.6 Database Flow

Reads only. Owns **no tables**:

```
candidates          → newCandidates      (created within today's [start,end))
screening_calls     → screeningsCompleted, failedCalls
interviews          → interviewsToday
applications (+ application_stage_history, pipeline_sla_config)
                    → overdueApplications, attention queue
automation_runs     → failedAutomations
```

## 7.7 Test Cases

| Test Case ID | Feature | Test Scenario | Steps | Expected Result | Actual | Status | Priority |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| TC-DASH-001 | Load | Dashboard renders | Sign in as owner | Header + 6 tiles + attention + quick links, no console errors | | | Critical |
| TC-DASH-002 | Loading | Skeleton appears | Throttle to Slow 3G, reload | Six card skeletons then content. **Never a full-page spinner** | | | High |
| TC-DASH-003 | Scope | Owner sees org-wide | As owner with data from several recruiters | `<h1>` shows the org name; counts include everyone | | | Critical |
| TC-DASH-004 | Scope | **Recruiter sees own only** | As a recruiter with 2 assigned + 8 unassigned/other applications | `<h1>` reads "Your workload"; meta line says "showing only applications assigned to you"; counts reflect only their work | | | Critical |
| TC-DASH-005 | Scope | Viewer sees org-wide, read-only | As viewer | Org-wide numbers; no action buttons that mutate | | | High |
| TC-DASH-006 | Timezone | "Today" uses the ORG timezone | Set org tz to `America/New_York`; create a candidate at 23:00 IST | The candidate counts on the **New York** day, not the browser's | | | Critical |
| TC-DASH-007 | Timezone | Half-open range | Create a candidate at exactly 00:00:00 org-time | Counted in the new day, not the old one | | | High |
| TC-DASH-008 | Timezone | DST boundary | Use a DST-observing zone across a transition | Day boundary is correct (covered by `lib/time.test.ts`) | | | Medium |
| TC-DASH-009 | Tiles | Zero state is not red | Fresh org, all metrics 0 | All six tiles show 0; **no red or amber accents** | | | High |
| TC-DASH-010 | Tiles | Overdue is amber | Create an application and age it past its stage SLA | `overdueApplications` shows a non-zero value with an **amber** left border | | | High |
| TC-DASH-011 | Tiles | Failures are red | Create a failed screening call and a failed automation run | Both tiles show a **red** left border | | | High |
| TC-DASH-012 | Tiles | Pending state | On a DB missing one source table, load the dashboard | That tile says the owning module isn't built. **It does NOT show 0** | | | Critical |
| TC-DASH-013 | Tiles | Error ≠ pending | Revoke SELECT on `candidates` (forces 42501), reload | Tile shows an **error** state, not "not built yet" | | | Critical |
| TC-DASH-014 | Attention | Empty queue | Fresh org | "Nothing needs you — You're all caught up…" with a `CheckCircle2` icon | | | High |
| TC-DASH-015 | Attention | Populated | Age several applications past target | Rows sorted most-urgent-first with a `{n}d` age tag coloured by severity | | | Critical |
| TC-DASH-016 | Attention | **Error ≠ empty** | Break the attention query | "Couldn't check what needs attention. This list may be incomplete — reload to try again." **Never** "nothing needs attention" | | | Critical |
| TC-DASH-017 | Attention | Row navigates | Click a row title | Opens the linked application | | | High |
| TC-DASH-018 | Brief | Generate | Click generate on the Daily Brief | A short narrative appears | | | High |
| TC-DASH-019 | Brief | **Numeric consistency** | Compare every number in the brief with the tiles | **No number contradicts a tile.** Any mismatch is a Critical bug | | | Critical |
| TC-DASH-020 | Brief | Empty organization | Fresh org, generate | Brief refuses to narrate nothing / says there is nothing yet — it must not invent activity | | | High |
| TC-DASH-021 | Brief | AI not configured | Unset `OPENAI_API_KEY` | Friendly unavailable message; **tiles and queue still render** | | | Critical |
| TC-DASH-022 | Brief | AI provider error | Dead `AI_BASE_URL` | "temporarily unavailable… continue manually" | | | High |
| TC-DASH-023 | Brief | Cache behaviour | Generate twice with no data change | Second response is served from cache (`cached: true`) | | | Medium |
| TC-DASH-024 | Brief | Cache invalidates | Generate, add a candidate, generate again | A **fresh** brief reflecting the new number | | | High |
| TC-DASH-025 | Freshness | No stale cache | Load, change data in another tab, reload | Numbers update — the page is `force-dynamic` | | | High |
| TC-DASH-026 | Nav | Quick links | Click each of the four rows | Jobs / Candidates / Pipeline / Interviews open | | | Medium |
| TC-DASH-027 | Nav | Invite button | Click "Invite your team" | → `/team/invite` (recruiter/viewer will be blocked there — verify the button either hides or the destination refuses gracefully) | | | Medium |
| TC-DASH-028 | API | `/api/dashboard` unauthenticated | `curl /api/dashboard` | 401 JSON | | | High |
| TC-DASH-029 | API | `/api/dashboard` as recruiter | Call as a recruiter | Own-scope data only | | | High |
| TC-DASH-030 | Perf | Large dataset | 1,000+ applications | Page renders in a reasonable time; no timeout | | | Medium |

## 7.8 UI/UX Checklist — M04

- [ ] Tiles form a clean **3×2** grid on desktop (never 4-across with two orphans)
- [ ] Tiles stack to 1 or 2 columns on mobile without clipping
- [ ] Each tile has a distinct icon (they are indistinguishable without one)
- [ ] Numbers use the display font and are the largest thing in the tile
- [ ] Attention rows show the title, a detail line, and an age tag
- [ ] Long candidate/job names truncate rather than breaking the row
- [ ] The bottom row (`dash-bottom`) stretches both cards to equal height
- [ ] Skeleton shape matches the eventual content
- [ ] No layout shift when the streamed content replaces the skeleton
- [ ] Date and timezone are both visible in the meta line

## 7.9 Edge Cases — M04

| ID | Edge case | Expected |
| --- | --- | --- |
| EC-DASH-01 | Brand-new organization, zero of everything | All tiles 0, empty queue, brief says so honestly |
| EC-DASH-02 | Exactly one attention item | Correct singular grammar ("1 application") |
| EC-DASH-03 | Attention item with no link | Renders as plain text, not a dead link |
| EC-DASH-04 | Metric that is both pending and would be 0 | Shows **pending**, never 0 |
| EC-DASH-05 | Recruiter with zero assigned applications | "Your workload" with honest zeros, not org-wide numbers |
| EC-DASH-06 | Two rapid clicks on Generate brief | One request; button disabled |
| EC-DASH-07 | Org timezone changed while the page is open | Reload reflects the new day boundary |
| EC-DASH-08 | Very large counts (10,000+) | Formatted readably, no overflow |

## 7.10 Known Risks — M04

1. The AI brief cache is **in-process** — on serverless it is per-instance and
   dies on redeploy. Expect inconsistent `cached` flags across requests.
2. The "Invite your team" button renders for **every** role, including Viewer,
   who cannot invite anyone. Verify the destination degrades gracefully.
3. `OVERDUE_DAYS = 3` is duplicated as a constant in both `lib/dashboard/metrics.ts`
   and the ai-action route (the route's own comment admits it "mirrors" it). Two
   copies of a threshold can drift.

---

# 8. MODULE M05 — Jobs Management

## 8.1 Purpose

Requisitions: what the organization is hiring for. A job is the anchor everything
else hangs off — applications, matching, screening scripts, pipeline columns,
analytics segments.

**Who uses it:** Owner/Admin/Recruiter create and edit; Owner/Admin archive;
Viewer reads.

## 8.2 UI Components

### `/jobs` — list
| UI Component | Purpose | User Action | Expected Result |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `PageHeader` + **Create job** | Entry point | Click | → `/jobs/new` (hidden for viewer) |
| Search input (`#job-search`) | Title search | Type + submit | `?q=` — PostgREST metacharacters `%_,()` are **stripped** server-side |
| Status select (`#job-status`) | Filter | Choose | All / Draft / Open / On hold / Closed |
| Recruiter select (`#job-recruiter`) | Filter | Choose | `?recruiter_id=` |
| Client select (`#job-client`) | Filter | Choose | **Agency mode only** |
| "Include archived" checkbox | Filter | Tick | `?archived=true` |
| Jobs table | Data | — | Title, Status, Health, … |
| `StatusBadge` | Draft/Open/On hold/Closed | — | — |
| `HealthBadge` | Healthy / Needs attention | Hover/click | Shows the reasons verbatim |
| `EmptyState` (two variants) | No data vs no matches | — | "No jobs yet" + Create job, or "No matching jobs" + Clear filters |
| `ErrorState` | Query failure | — | "Couldn't load jobs." |
| `SkeletonRows` | Loading | — | — |

### `/jobs/new` and `/jobs/[id]/edit` — `JobForm.tsx` (751 lines)
| Section | Field | Type | Validation (server-side, `lib/jobs/validation.ts`) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **Start from a job description** | Paste area | textarea | placeholder "Paste the full job description here…" |
| | **Extract with AI** button | button | `POST /api/jobs/ai-action` → returns proposals, **saves nothing** |
| | Diff table | Field / Yours (kept) / AI suggestion | Human decides field by field |
| **Job details** | Title (`#title`) | text | **Required**, ≤200 chars, trimmed |
| | Min experience (`#exp-min`) | number | ≥0, ≤60, numeric |
| | Max experience (`#exp-max`) | number | ≥0, ≤60; **min ≤ max** |
| | Min salary (`#salary-min`) | number | ≥0 |
| | Max salary (`#salary-max`) | number | ≥0; **min ≤ max** |
| | Location (`#location`) | text | ≤200 chars |
| | Work mode (`#work-mode`) | select | Not specified / On-site / Hybrid / Remote |
| | Status (`#status`) | select | draft / open / on_hold / closed |
| | Client (`#client`) | select | Agency mode only; "No client" default |
| | Owner recruiter (`#owner`) | select | "Unassigned" default; **must be an active member of this org** |
| | Description (`#description`) | textarea | ≤20,000 chars |
| | Required skills | `StringListEditor` | "Add a skill and press Enter"; ≤25 entries, ≤120 chars each, case-insensitive dedupe |
| | Preferred skills | `StringListEditor` | Same; **anything also in Required is stripped** (required wins) |
| | **Save / Cancel** | buttons | Explicit save, never auto-save |

### `/jobs/[id]` — detail
Tabs/cards: **Details**, **Health**, **Hiring stages** (see M06), **Pipeline**,
**Add candidates** (see M07), **Edit job**, **Screening summary**, `JobActions`
(archive).

## 8.3 Complete User Flow — create a job with AI assistance

```
Step 1  Recruiter opens /jobs → clicks Create job
Step 2  /jobs/new renders JobForm
Step 3  Recruiter pastes a full JD into "Start from a job description"
Step 4  Clicks Extract with AI
Step 5  FRONTEND: POST /api/jobs/ai-action {text}
Step 6  BACKEND: requireRole(["owner","admin","recruiter"])   ← Viewer denied
        400 if the text is empty: "Paste a job description to extract from."
Step 7  lib/ai/extractJobFromDescription() → completeJson() with a validator
Step 8  RESPONSE {data, saved: false}   ← NOTHING IS WRITTEN
Step 9  UI shows a diff table: Field | Yours (kept) | AI suggestion
Step 10 Recruiter accepts/rejects each field individually
Step 11 Recruiter fills anything remaining, clicks Save
Step 12 FRONTEND: POST /api/jobs {title, …, screening_questions: [...]}
Step 13 BACKEND: requireRole(...); parseJobPayload(body,"create")
        - title required, ≤200
        - min≤max on both ranges (mirrors the DB CHECK for a readable message)
        - skills deduped, capped, preferred∖required
        - owner_recruiter_id VERIFIED to be an active member (else 400)
Step 14 DATABASE: INSERT jobs (organization_id from the SESSION;
        owner_recruiter_id defaults to the creator so every job has an owner)
Step 15 replaceQuestions() delete-then-insert job_screening_questions
Step 16 logActivity("job.created")
Step 17 RESPONSE 201 {data: job}
Step 18 UI navigates to /jobs/[id]
```

## 8.4 Job Health rules (`lib/jobs/health.ts`) — testable, deterministic, **not AI**

`STALE_OPEN_JOB_DAYS = 30`. Only `open` and `on_hold` jobs are assessed.

| # | Condition | Reason text |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | status = `on_hold` | "On hold — needs a decision to resume or close" |
| 2 | open, 0 screening questions | "No screening questions — add them in the AI Screening Call stage to enable screening" |
| 3 | open, both experience bounds null | "No experience range — candidate matching will be less accurate" |
| 4 | open, both salary bounds null | "No salary band — candidate matching will be less accurate" |
| 5 | open, `required_skills` empty | "No required skills listed" |
| 6 | open, age > 30 days | "Open for {n} days without being filled" |
| 7 | open, applicationCount === 0 **and** age ≥ 7 days | "No candidates after {n} days" |

Status is `healthy` only when the reasons list is empty. Order is stable.

## 8.5 API Flow

| API | Method | Auth | Request | Response | Errors |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `/api/jobs` | GET | any member | `?status ?client_id ?recruiter_id ?q ?include_archived ?page ?per_page` | `{data, pagination, caller_role}` | 400 invalid status |
| `/api/jobs` | POST | owner/admin/**recruiter** | job fields + optional `screening_questions[]` | 201 `{data}` | 400 validation / bad recruiter; 403 viewer |
| `/api/jobs/[id]` | GET | any member | — | `{data: job + screening_questions, caller_role}` | 404 (cross-tenant reported as 404, never 403) |
| `/api/jobs/[id]` | PATCH | owner/admin/recruiter | partial | `{data}` | 400; **403 "You can only close jobs you own."**; 404 |
| `/api/jobs/[id]` | DELETE | **owner/admin** | — | `{data}` | 404 "Job not found, or already archived." |
| `/api/jobs/ai-action` | POST | owner/admin/recruiter | `{text}` | `{data, saved:false}` | 400 empty; AI codes |

**Note:** `DELETE` **archives** (`archived_at = now()`, `status = 'closed'`).
It is never a hard delete, because applications and analytics reference the row.

**`interview_questions` is no longer accepted** by POST/PATCH. Interview questions
now live in the Phone/Video Interview hiring-stage configs. Sending the old key is
silently ignored.

## 8.6 Database Flow

```
USER ACTION  Save job
 → API       POST /api/jobs
 → BACKEND   requireRole → parseJobPayload → member check for owner_recruiter_id
 → DATABASE  INSERT public.jobs
               CHECK jobs_experience_range_valid (min ≤ max)
               CHECK jobs_salary_range_valid     (min ≤ max)
               trigger trg_jobs_touch_updated_at
               trigger trg_jobs_client_tenant_integrity (client must be same org)
               trigger trg_jobs_mark_matches_stale (edits invalidate match scores)
               trigger trg_jobs_recruiter_closes_own (DB-level close rule)
             INSERT public.job_screening_questions (delete-then-insert)
             INSERT public.activity_events ('job.created')
 → RESPONSE  201
 → UI        /jobs/[id]
```

## 8.7 Test Cases

| Test Case ID | Feature | Test Scenario | Steps | Expected Result | Actual | Status | Priority |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| TC-JOB-001 | Create | Minimum valid job | `/jobs/new` → title "QA Engineer" → Save | 201, redirected to the detail page, job appears in `/jobs` | | | Critical |
| TC-JOB-002 | Create | Full job | Fill every field including 5 required + 3 preferred skills | All values persist exactly | | | Critical |
| TC-JOB-003 | Create | Missing title | Save with title blank | "A job title is required." | | | Critical |
| TC-JOB-004 | Create | 201-character title | Paste 201 chars | "Title must be 200 characters or fewer." | | | High |
| TC-JOB-005 | Create | Whitespace-only title | `"   "` | Rejected (trim-then-check) | | | High |
| TC-JOB-006 | Create | **min experience > max** | Min 5, Max 3 | "Minimum experience cannot be greater than maximum experience." | | | Critical |
| TC-JOB-007 | Create | **min salary > max** | Min 20, Max 10 | "Minimum salary cannot be greater than maximum salary." | | | Critical |
| TC-JOB-008 | Create | Negative experience | Min = −1 | "Minimum experience cannot be negative." | | | High |
| TC-JOB-009 | Create | Experience > 60 | Min = 99 | "Minimum experience is unrealistically large." | | | High |
| TC-JOB-010 | Create | Non-numeric experience | `POST` with `"experience_min":"abc"` | "Minimum experience must be a number." | | | High |

| TC-JOB-012 | Create | 121-character skill | Add one | "Each entry in Required skills must be 120 characters or fewer." | | | Medium |

| TC-JOB-014 | Create | **Skill in both lists** | "Java" in Required and Preferred | Stripped from Preferred; Required wins | | | High |

| TC-JOB-021 | Create | **`organization_id` from the body is ignored** | `POST {"title":"X","organization_id":"<org B>"}` | Job is created in **org A** | | | Critical |
| TC-JOB-022 | Create | Viewer denied | As viewer, `POST /api/jobs` | 403; the Create job button is hidden in the UI | | | Critical |
| TC-JOB-023 | Create | Invalid JSON | `curl -d 'not json'` | 400 "Invalid JSON body." | | | Medium |
| TC-JOB-024 | AI extract | Happy path | Paste a real JD → Extract with AI | Diff table with proposals; **nothing saved yet** | | | Critical |
| TC-JOB-025 | AI extract | Nothing is written | Extract, then navigate away without saving | No job row exists | | | Critical |
| TC-JOB-026 | AI extract | Field-by-field accept | Accept 2 of 6 suggestions | Only those 2 populate | | | High |
| TC-JOB-027 | AI extract | Empty text | Click Extract with a blank box | "Paste a job description to extract from." | | | Medium |
| TC-JOB-028 | AI extract | AI unavailable | Unset `OPENAI_API_KEY` | Friendly message; **manual entry still fully works** | | | Critical |

| TC-JOB-040 | Archive | Recruiter denied | As recruiter, `DELETE /api/jobs/{id}` | 403 | | | Critical |

| TC-JOB-042 | Archive | Include archived | Tick "Include archived" | Archived jobs appear | | | High |
| TC-JOB-043 | Archive | Applications survive | Archive a job with applications | Applications still exist and open | | | Critical |

## 8.8 UI/UX Checklist — M05

- [ ] The list header action and the empty-state action have the **same name** ("Create job")
- [ ] Health badge is scannable at a glance and reasons are readable
- [ ] Skill chips are removable and the input clears after Enter
- [ ] Number inputs use `type=number` and reject letters
- [ ] Long descriptions do not blow out the card
- [ ] Save is explicit; navigating away with unsaved changes is either warned about or clearly discarded
- [ ] Table scrolls horizontally (`.table-container`) on mobile
- [ ] Filters are reflected in the URL so a filtered view is shareable
- [ ] Client filter is completely absent (not disabled) for in-house orgs
- [ ] Loading skeleton on the list, error state on failure, both distinct from empty

## 8.9 Edge Cases — M05

| ID | Edge case | Expected |
| --- | --- | --- |
| EC-JOB-01 | Title of exactly 200 chars | Accepted |
| EC-JOB-02 | Experience min = max = 0 | Accepted |
| EC-JOB-03 | Salary min = max | Accepted |
| EC-JOB-04 | Only min experience, no max | Accepted (constraint only fires when both present) |
| EC-JOB-05 | **Partial update where the stored other half conflicts** | The route cannot see the stored half — the **DB CHECK** is the backstop; verify the error is readable, not a raw constraint name |
| EC-JOB-06 | Emoji / RTL text in title | Renders correctly |
| EC-JOB-07 | Skill with a leading/trailing space | Trimmed |
| EC-JOB-08 | Empty string in the skills array | Skipped, not stored |
| EC-JOB-09 | Two jobs with the same title | Allowed — no uniqueness constraint |
| EC-JOB-10 | Owner recruiter later removed from the org | FK is `ON DELETE SET NULL`; job survives |
| EC-JOB-11 | Rapid double Save | One job created, not two |
| EC-JOB-12 | Browser back after save | Does not re-submit |

## 8.10 Known Risks — M05

1. **No duplicate-title warning.** Two identical requisitions can be created
   silently, which splits a pipeline in two.
2. `parseJobPayload` returns "No valid fields provided." if a PATCH contains only
   unknown keys — verify that reads sensibly to a user.
3. `MAX_SKILLS` differs between jobs (25) and candidates (40). Not a bug, but a
   surprise if you test them together.
4. Search strips `%_,()` rather than escaping them, so a genuine search for a
   title containing a comma silently searches for a different string.

# 9. MODULE M06 — Job Hiring Stages

## 9.1 Purpose

Per-job configuration of which pipeline steps exist and what the AI says during
them. Four steps can be switched on or off per job; a fifth (resume scoring)
always runs and the toggle instead chooses between the platform default and this
job's own prompt.

**Who uses it:** Owner/Admin/Recruiter configure; **Viewer can see which stages
are enabled** (explicitly required) but cannot change them.

## 9.2 The five stages (`lib/hiring-stages/catalog.ts`)

| `stage_key` | Label | Kind | Execution | Engine |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `resume_score` | Resume Score | **scoring** | live | Module 7 matching |
| `ai_screening_call` | AI Screening Call | pipeline | **live** | Bolna (Module 8) |
| `phone_interview` | Phone Interview | pipeline | **configuration_only** | none |
| `video_interview` | Video Interview | pipeline | **configuration_only** | none |
| `written_assessment` | Written Assessment | pipeline | **configuration_only** | none |

**This is the most important honesty test in the module.** Only the AI Screening
Call is wired to a real engine. The other three save configuration and nothing
runs it. The UI must say `CONFIGURATION_ONLY_NOTE` = **"Not yet active —
configuration only"** on those rows. For `resume_score`, off/on read as
`SCORING_OFF_NOTE` = "Off — scored with the platform default" and `SCORING_ON_NOTE`
= "On — scored with this job's own prompt" — **never** as "no resume scoring",
which would be false and alarming.

`job_hiring_stages.stage_key` has a DB CHECK limited to the four *pipeline* keys;
`resume_score` is synced through `trg_job_hiring_stages_sync_resume_score` /
`sync_job_resume_passing_score()`.

## 9.3 UI Components

| UI Component | Purpose | User Action | Expected Result |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `HiringStages` card on `/jobs/[id]` | Overview | — | One row per stage with a toggle and an execution note |
| Per-stage toggle | Enable/disable | Click | Marks dirty; saved by an explicit Save |
| **Configure: {stage.label}** | Opens `StageConfigModal` | Click | Modal opens |
| Prompt template textarea | AI script | Type | placeholder e.g. "e.g. Confirm {{candidate.name}} is still interested in {{job.title}}…" — ≤8,000 chars |
| `PlaceholderEditor` | Token insertion | Click a token | Inserts `{{job.title}}` etc. **Tokens stay unresolved at save time** |
| Language input (AI call) | Call language | Type | — |
| Max call attempts | Retry cap | Number | **1–10** (`MIN_CALL_ATTEMPTS`/`MAX_CALL_ATTEMPTS`) |
| Suggested duration (minutes) | Interviews | Number | **5–480** |
| Time limit (minutes) | Written assessment | Number | 5–480 |
| "What to evaluate" list | Guidance | Add items | ≤25 items, ≤500 chars each |
| "What the score is made of" | Resume score weights | Adjust | `ScoringWeights` per component |
| Passing score | Gate | Number | `resume_score` max 100; round stages max 10 |
| **Save / Cancel** | Commit | Click | `PUT /api/jobs/[id]/hiring-stages` |

## 9.4 Complete User Flow

```
Step 1  Recruiter opens /jobs/{id}
Step 2  GET /api/jobs/{id}/hiring-stages returns ALL FIVE, configured or not
Step 3  Recruiter enables "AI Screening Call"
Step 4  Clicks Configure: AI Screening Call
Step 5  Types a prompt using {{candidate.name}} / {{job.title}} tokens
Step 6  Sets language and max attempts (1–10)
Step 7  Clicks Save
Step 8  FRONTEND: PUT /api/jobs/{id}/hiring-stages {stages:[…]}
Step 9  BACKEND: requireRole(["owner","admin","recruiter"])
        parseStagesPayload():
          - must be a list, ≤ STAGE_LIMIT
          - each entry an object with a known stage_key
          - EACH STAGE MAY APPEAR ONLY ONCE
          - normalizeStageConfig() clamps every number into range
          - normalizePromptTemplate() trims and truncates at 8000
        Job ownership is verified (404 if not this org's job)
Step 10 DATABASE: UPSERT job_hiring_stages on (job_id, stage_key)
        NEVER DELETE — a disabled stage keeps its row, script and settings
        trg_job_hiring_stages_tenant_integrity, _touch, _sync_resume_score fire
Step 11 RESPONSE {data: stages}
Step 12 UI reflects the saved state; tokens are still literal in the stored script
```

**Placeholder substitution happens at RUN time, never at save time.** A stored
script with a candidate's name baked in would be wrong for every other candidate
and would leak one candidate's details into another's call.

## 9.5 API Flow

| API | Method | Auth | Errors |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `/api/jobs/[id]/hiring-stages` | GET | **any member** (Viewer included) | 404 "Job not found." |
| `/api/jobs/[id]/hiring-stages` | PUT | owner/admin/recruiter | 400 "Invalid request body." / "Send a list of stages." / "Too many stages." / "Each stage must be an object." / "Unknown hiring stage." / "Each stage may appear only once."; 400 "Could not save the hiring stages."; 404 |

## 9.6 Test Cases

| Test Case ID | Feature | Test Scenario | Steps | Expected Result | Actual | Status | Priority |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| TC-STAGE-001 | Read | All five stages listed | Open a brand-new job's detail page | All five rows render, all disabled, none missing | | | Critical |
| TC-STAGE-002 | Honesty | **Configuration-only notes** | Look at Phone/Video/Written rows | Each shows "Not yet active — configuration only" | | | Critical |
| TC-STAGE-003 | Honesty | **Resume score wording** | Toggle resume_score off | Reads "Off — scored with the platform default", **never** "no resume scoring" | | | Critical |
| TC-STAGE-004 | Honesty | Resume score on | Toggle on | "On — scored with this job's own prompt" | | | High |
| TC-STAGE-005 | Enable | Enable AI screening call | Toggle + Save | Persists across reload | | | Critical |
| TC-STAGE-006 | Disable | **Config survives disabling** | Configure a prompt, disable the stage, Save, reload, re-enable | The prompt is **still there** (upsert, never delete) | | | Critical |
| TC-STAGE-007 | Prompt | Tokens stay literal | Save a prompt with `{{candidate.name}}`, reload | Stored and displayed **unresolved** | | | Critical |
| TC-STAGE-008 | Prompt | 8,001 characters | Paste | Truncated to 8,000, not rejected with a 500 | | | Medium |
| TC-STAGE-009 | Prompt | Empty prompt | Clear it, Save | Stored as `null`, no crash | | | Medium |
| TC-STAGE-010 | Numbers | Max attempts out of range | Set 0, then 11 | Clamped to 1 and 10 respectively | | | High |
| TC-STAGE-011 | Numbers | Duration out of range | Set 1, then 999 | Clamped to 5 and 480 | | | High |
| TC-STAGE-012 | Numbers | Non-numeric | `PUT` with `"maxAttempts":"lots"` | Falls back to a valid default, no 500 | | | High |
| TC-STAGE-013 | Payload | Duplicate stage key | `PUT` two `ai_screening_call` entries | 400 "Each stage may appear only once." | | | High |
| TC-STAGE-014 | Payload | Unknown stage key | `PUT {"stage_key":"drug_test"}` | 400 "Unknown hiring stage." | | | High |
| TC-STAGE-015 | Payload | Not a list | `PUT {"stages":{}}` | 400 "Send a list of stages." | | | Medium |
| TC-STAGE-016 | Payload | Too many stages | Send 50 | 400 "Too many stages." | | | Medium |
| TC-STAGE-017 | Perms | **Viewer can read** | As viewer, open a job detail | Stage list is **visible**, controls hidden/disabled | | | Critical |
| TC-STAGE-018 | Perms | Viewer cannot write | Viewer `PUT` | 403 | | | Critical |
| TC-STAGE-019 | Perms | Recruiter can write | As recruiter | Allowed | | | High |
| TC-STAGE-020 | Tenant | Cross-tenant job | `PUT /api/jobs/{orgB-job}/hiring-stages` | 404 | | | Critical |
| TC-STAGE-021 | Integration | Screening questions drive health | Enable AI Screening Call with 0 questions | Job Health rule 2 fires and names this stage | | | High |
| TC-STAGE-022 | Integration | Enabled stages drive the pipeline | Disable Written Assessment, open an application for that job | The stage-move dropdown does **not** offer Written Assessment | | | Critical |
| TC-STAGE-023 | Integration | Terminal exits always available | Same application | Rejected and Withdrawn are **always** offered regardless of configuration | | | Critical |
| TC-STAGE-024 | Resume score | Passing score sync | Set a resume passing score, Save | `jobs` row is synced by `trg_job_hiring_stages_sync_resume_score` | | | High |
| TC-STAGE-025 | Weights | Scoring weights | Adjust component weights, Save | Persist; reflected in a recalculated match | | | Medium |

## 9.7 UI/UX Checklist — M06

- [ ] Every row states its execution reality — a tester must never guess whether enabling something does anything
- [ ] Modal opens on Configure and closes on Cancel, Escape, and backdrop click
- [ ] Unsaved modal changes are either kept or explicitly discarded — never silently lost
- [ ] Placeholder editor inserts at the caret, not at the end
- [ ] Number inputs show their allowed range
- [ ] Toggle state and the Save button's dirty state are visually clear
- [ ] Viewer sees the stages read-only, with no disabled-looking dead controls

## 9.8 Known Risks — M06

1. Three of five stages are configuration-only. If the UI ever stops saying so,
   recruiters will enable them and wait for something that never happens.
2. Numeric config is **clamped, not rejected** — a user typing 999 gets 480 with
   no message. Confirm the UI reflects the clamped value after save.
3. Prompt truncation at 8,000 chars is silent.

# 10. MODULE M07 — Bulk Resume Intake

## 10.1 Purpose

Drop a folder of resumes onto a job and let the product do the work: extract text,
AI-parse it, decide **deterministically** who each file belongs to, create or match
the candidate, store the resume, queue any profile disagreements for review, and
create the application.

**Who uses it:** Owner/Admin/Recruiter, from the "Add candidates" modal on a job.
**Problem solved:** the single most repetitive task in agency recruitment.

## 10.2 The governing design rule

> **Every file resolves independently.** One corrupt PDF among ten must not cost
> the other nine.

This is structural, not a promise: **one file per HTTP request**. There is no
shared transaction, no shared array, and no way for one file's failure to reach a
sibling. A processing exception is caught and recorded as a `failed` **row with
HTTP 200**, so the batch keeps its shape and the file can be retried.

## 10.3 The eight intake statuses (`lib/intake/status.ts`)

| Status | Meaning |
| --- | --- |
| `queued` | Accepted, not yet processed |
| `processing` | In flight |
| `candidate_created` | New person created + application created |
| `candidate_matched` | Matched an existing candidate + application created |
| `already_applied` | Matched, but an application for this job already existed |
| `match_conflict` | **Ambiguous — nothing was created.** File parked at `<org>/_intake/…` so it can still be downloaded and read |
| `manually_connected` | A human resolved a conflict via the override |
| `failed` | Extraction, parsing, or storage failed; `error_message` says why |

## 10.4 The matching rule (`lib/intake/match.ts`) — deterministic, never AI

Collect every candidate id the **normalised email** matches and every id the
**normalised phone** matches, then take the **union**:

| Union size | Outcome |
| --- | --- |
| 0 | `create` |
| 1 | `match` (even if only one field matched) |
| 2+ | `conflict` — resolve nothing |

Conflict reasons: `email_phone_disagree` (email says one person, phone says
another), `email_ambiguous` (one address already on two records),
`phone_ambiguous`.

**Why the union and not a sequence:** checking email first and taking the hit
would silently pick the email's owner in exactly the shared-family-phone case
that must not be guessed at.

**Normalisation** (`lib/candidates/dedupe.ts`, kept in sync with the DB trigger
`normalize_candidate_contact()`):
- Email: lowercase + trim. Deliberately **no** gmail dot/plus stripping.
- Phone: digits only, compared on the **last 10** (`PHONE_MATCH_DIGITS`). So
  `+91 98765 43210`, `098765 43210` and `9876543210` are one person. Shorter
  strings are kept as-is.

## 10.5 What the AI is and is not allowed to do here

- The AI **parses**, and that is all.
- It **never** decides who the resume belongs to (that is `match.ts`, deterministic).
- It **never** overwrites a field on an existing candidate — the proposal is
  queued as an unreviewed `resume_parse_results` row and counted in
  `queued_conflict_count`, which is what lets the modal say "3 profile updates
  need your review".
- The one place parsed values reach `candidates` directly is when creating a
  **brand-new** record, where there is nothing to overwrite.

## 10.6 UI Components

| UI Component | Purpose | User Action | Expected Result |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **Add candidates** button on `/jobs/[id]` | Opens `IntakeModal` | Click | Modal opens |
| Drop zone / file picker | Select files | Drop or browse | `accept` = `RESUME_UPLOAD_ACCEPT` (.pdf,.doc,.docx + MIME types) |
| Per-file row | Progress + outcome | — | Filename, size, status chip toned by `INTAKE_TONE` |
| Summary line | `summaryLine(summarize(statuses))` | — | One sentence a recruiter actually reads |
| Conflict row → candidate picker | Manual override | Choose a candidate | `PATCH /api/jobs/[id]/intake/[itemId]` |
| Reopen the modal | Resume a batch | Reopen | `GET /api/jobs/[id]/intake?batch_id=` restores state |

## 10.7 Complete User Flow

```
Step 1  Recruiter opens /jobs/{id}, clicks Add candidates
Step 2  Drops 10 PDFs
Step 3  FRONTEND generates ONE batch_id (uuid), then POSTs each file separately
Step 4  For EACH file: POST /api/jobs/{id}/intake  (multipart)
        BACKEND:
          requireRole(["owner","admin","recruiter"])
          getJobDetail → 404 if not this org's job
          409 if the job is ARCHIVED ("Restore it before adding candidates.")
          400 if no file / empty file
          400 if size > 10 MB
          batch_id validated as a UUID, else a fresh one is generated
          isAllowedResumeUpload(name, type) → EXTENSION FIRST, MIME as fallback
            (browsers disagree about .doc's MIME type)
            → on rejection: a `failed` ROW with HTTP 200, so the batch continues
          hashFile(buffer) → SHA-256 (used to skip re-parsing unchanged files)
        processIntakeFile():
          extractResumeText()  → unpdf / mammoth / word-extractor
          parseResume()        → AI, schema-validated
          resolveCandidateMatch() → create | match | conflict
          create  → INSERT candidates (parsed values allowed: nothing to overwrite)
          match   → queue field conflicts as resume_parse_results (UNREVIEWED)
          conflict→ store the file at <org>/_intake/…, keep parsed_json, STOP
          store the resume in the private `resumes` bucket
          ensureApplicationForCandidate() → application at DEFAULT_APPLICATION_STAGE
          logActivity + logAiCall
          dispatch() automations (application_created trigger)
          trySendForEvent() candidate communication
Step 5  Each response returns one `resume_intake_items` row
Step 6  UI updates that row's chip; other files keep going
Step 7  Recruiter resolves any conflicts via the candidate picker
Step 8  PATCH /api/jobs/{id}/intake/{itemId} {candidate_id}
        → status becomes `manually_connected`
```

## 10.8 API Flow

| API | Method | Auth | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `/api/jobs/[id]/intake` | GET | any member | `?batch_id=` required (400 otherwise). Results are re-filtered by `job_id` in code |
| `/api/jobs/[id]/intake` | POST | owner/admin/recruiter | multipart, **one file**. 404 job / 409 archived / 400 no file / 400 >10 MB. **Per-file failures return 200 with a failed row.** |
| `/api/jobs/[id]/intake/[itemId]` | PATCH | owner/admin/recruiter | `{candidate_id}`. 400 invalid item id / missing candidate; 404 job or item; delegates status from `result.status` |

## 10.9 Database Flow

`resume_intake_items (id, organization_id, batch_id, job_id, file_name,
file_size_bytes, file_hash, storage_path, status, candidate_id, application_id,
resume_id, conflict_candidate_ids uuid[], parsed_json jsonb,
queued_conflict_count, error_message, created_by, created_at, processed_at)`

`conflict_candidate_ids` is deliberately **not** a foreign key — it is evidence
about a decision that was refused, and must survive one of those candidates being
archived.

## 10.10 Test Cases

| Test Case ID | Feature | Test Scenario | Steps | Expected Result | Actual | Status | Priority |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| TC-INTK-001 | Happy path | Single new candidate | Drop one PDF of an unknown person | `candidate_created`; candidate + resume + application all exist | | | Critical |
| TC-INTK-002 | Happy path | 10 files at once | Drop 10 valid PDFs | 10 rows, all resolved; summary line is accurate | | | Critical |
| TC-INTK-003 | **Independence** | One corrupt file among ten | Include a 0-byte or truncated PDF | That row = `failed` with a reason; **the other nine still succeed** | | | Critical |
| TC-INTK-004 | Independence | Unsupported type | Drop a `.txt` and a `.png` | Each becomes a `failed` row saying "Only PDF and Word documents (.doc, .docx) are supported." **HTTP 200, batch continues** | | | Critical |
| TC-INTK-005 | Size | 10 MB + 1 byte | Drop an 11 MB PDF | 400 "That file is larger than 10 MB." | | | High |
| TC-INTK-006 | Size | Empty file | Drop a 0-byte file | 400 "Choose a resume file to upload." | | | High |
| TC-INTK-007 | Matching | Existing candidate by email | Drop a resume for someone already on file | `candidate_matched`; **no duplicate candidate created** | | | Critical |
| TC-INTK-008 | Matching | Phone-format variance | Existing `9876543210`, resume says `+91 98765 43210` | Matched — last-10-digit comparison | | | Critical |
| TC-INTK-009 | Matching | Email case/space variance | `Rahul@Example.COM ` vs stored `rahul@example.com` | Matched | | | Critical |
| TC-INTK-010 | Matching | **Gmail dots are NOT stripped** | `r.ahul@gmail.com` vs `rahul@gmail.com` | Treated as **different** people (deliberate) | | | High |
| TC-INTK-011 | Conflict | **email_phone_disagree** | Resume's email belongs to A, phone belongs to B | `match_conflict`. **No candidate, no application created.** File is downloadable | | | Critical |
| TC-INTK-012 | Conflict | email_ambiguous | Two candidates already share one address | `match_conflict` | | | High |
| TC-INTK-013 | Conflict | phone_ambiguous | Two candidates share a phone | `match_conflict` | | | High |
| TC-INTK-014 | Conflict | Manual resolution | Pick a candidate on a conflict row | Status → `manually_connected`; candidate + application resolved | | | Critical |
| TC-INTK-015 | Conflict | Parse output reused | Resolve a conflict | The stored `parsed_json` is used — **no second AI call is paid for** | | | High |
| TC-INTK-016 | Duplicate app | Already applied | Drop the same resume for the same job twice | Second → `already_applied`, existing application referenced, no duplicate | | | Critical |
| TC-INTK-017 | Profile conflicts | Queued, not applied | Drop a resume with a different job title for a known candidate | Candidate's stored profile is **unchanged**; `queued_conflict_count` > 0; a review banner appears on the profile | | | Critical |
| TC-INTK-018 | Persistence | Close and reopen the modal | Start a batch, close, reopen | `GET …/intake?batch_id=` restores every row and status | | | High |
| TC-INTK-019 | Persistence | Missing batch_id | `GET /api/jobs/{id}/intake` with no param | 400 "A batch id is required." | | | Medium |
| TC-INTK-020 | Job state | Archived job | Archive a job, then try to add candidates | 409 "This job is archived. Restore it before adding candidates." | | | High |
| TC-INTK-021 | Tenant | Cross-tenant job | POST to org B's job id | 404 "Job not found." | | | Critical |
| TC-INTK-022 | Tenant | Cross-tenant candidate on override | PATCH with org B's candidate id | Refused | | | Critical |
| TC-INTK-023 | Perms | Viewer denied | As viewer | 403; button hidden | | | Critical |
| TC-INTK-024 | AI down | Parsing unavailable | Unset `OPENAI_API_KEY`, drop a file | Row fails with an honest reason. **The product must not claim a parse happened** | | | Critical |
| TC-INTK-025 | Extraction | Scanned image PDF | Drop a PDF with no text layer | `failed` with a reason about extractable text — **not** a message blaming the AI | | | High |
| TC-INTK-026 | Extraction | Old `.doc` | Drop a Word 97 `.doc` | Extracted via `word-extractor` | | | High |
| TC-INTK-027 | Extraction | `.docx` | Drop one | Extracted via `mammoth` | | | High |
| TC-INTK-028 | Extraction | Password-protected PDF | Drop one | Clean `failed` row, no 500 | | | High |
| TC-INTK-029 | Naming | 300-character filename | Drop one | Stored truncated to the last 200 chars, no error | | | Low |
| TC-INTK-030 | Naming | Filename with `/` or unicode | Drop one | Stored safely; storage path is not broken | | | Medium |
| TC-INTK-031 | Side-effect | Automations fire | Have an active `application_created` rule | It runs for each created application | | | High |
| TC-INTK-032 | Side-effect | Candidate email sent | Have an **active** `application_received` template | Message logged for each new application | | | High |
| TC-INTK-033 | Side-effect | Inactive template | Template exists but `active = false` | **No message is sent** | | | Critical |
| TC-INTK-034 | Resilience | Processing throws | Simulate a DB outage mid-file | `failed` row + HTTP 200, batch shape preserved | | | High |
| TC-INTK-035 | Resilience | Bookkeeping row insert fails | Simulate | The **real outcome** is still reported (a synthesised row), not a failure the recruiter would retry | | | Medium |
| TC-INTK-036 | Concurrency | Two recruiters, same file | Both drop the same resume simultaneously | One application (unique `candidate_id, job_id`); the other reports `already_applied` | | | High |

## 10.11 UI/UX Checklist — M07

- [ ] Per-file progress is visible; a slow file does not freeze the others
- [ ] Status chips are colour-toned per `INTAKE_TONE` and readable without colour
- [ ] The summary line is a real sentence, not a raw count dump
- [ ] Conflict rows offer a clear resolution path (a candidate picker), not just an error
- [ ] The conflicted file can be **downloaded and read** while deciding
- [ ] Closing the modal does not lose work
- [ ] The file picker's `accept` matches the server allowlist exactly
- [ ] "3 profile updates need your review" is discoverable from the candidate profile too
- [ ] Drag-and-drop and click-to-browse both work

## 10.12 Known Risks — M07

1. **Per-file failures return HTTP 200.** Correct for the batch, but anyone
   testing at the API level must read `data.status`, not the HTTP code.
2. `already_applied` and `candidate_matched` look similar in a summary; confirm
   the recruiter can tell them apart.
3. The batch has no server-side concurrency limit — 100 files means 100
   concurrent AI calls from the browser. Watch for rate limiting.
4. `conflict_candidate_ids` has no FK, so a conflict can reference an archived
   candidate. Verify the picker renders that gracefully.

# 11. MODULE M08 — Candidates Management

## 11.1 Purpose

The people. One record per human, reachable, deduplicated, and never destroyed —
archived instead, because applications, screening and analytics all reference it.

**Who uses it:** Owner/Admin/Recruiter create and edit; Owner/Admin archive;
Viewer reads (including natural-language search — results are read-only for
everyone).

## 11.2 UI Components

### `/candidates` — list + search
| UI Component | Purpose | User Action | Expected Result |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **Add candidate** button | Create | Click | → `/candidates/new` (hidden for viewer) |
| Natural-language search (`#nl-search`) | AI search | Type + submit | placeholder "e.g. Java candidates in Gurgaon with 4-7 years and less than 45 days notice" |
| Sparkle icon in the field | Signals AI | — | An icon rather than a label saying "this is AI" |
| Structured filters: Skills (`#f-skills`), Location (`#f-location`), Min/Max experience (`#f-exp-min`/`#f-exp-max`), Notice (`#f-notice`), Source (`#f-source`) | Filter | Set | Applied server-side |
| **Clear** button | Reset | Click | Filters cleared |
| `CandidateList` | Data | — | Rows link to the detail page |
| Empty / error / skeleton states | — | — | Distinct from one another |

### `/candidates/new` and edit — `CandidateForm.tsx` (658 lines)
| Section | Field | Validation (`lib/candidates/validation.ts`) |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Paste candidate details** | Free-text blurb | placeholder shows a realistic example |
| | **Structure with AI** | `POST /api/candidates/ai-action` → proposals, `saved:false` |
| | Diff table | Field / Yours (kept) / AI suggestion |
| **Profile** | Name (`#name`) | **Required**, ≤200 chars |
| | Email (`#email`) | ≤320 chars, pattern `^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$`, **lowercased on save** |
| | Phone (`#phone`) | ≤40 chars, **≥6 digits after stripping non-digits** |
| | Current company (`#company`) | ≤200 |
| | Current role (`#role`) | ≤200 |
| | Location (`#location`) | ≤120 |
| | Total experience (`#experience`) | 0–60 |
| | Expected salary (`#salary`) | ≥0 |
| | Notice period (`#notice`) | 0–365 days |
| | Source (`#source`) | one of career_page, resume_upload, email, referral, job_board, agency_database, manual |
| | Skills | ≤40 entries, ≤60 chars each, case-insensitive dedupe |
| | Education | `RepeatableEditor` — normalised, never rejected |
| | Employment history | `RepeatableEditor` — normalised, never rejected |
| **Duplicate warning banner** | Attention-toned `<h2>` | Lists suspected duplicates with "View the other record" |

**Contactability rule:** a candidate must have an **email or a phone**. Enforced
in validation (`"Add an email address or a phone number."`) **and** by the DB
CHECK `candidates_contactable`.

### `/candidates/[id]` — detail
Tabs/cards: **Profile**, **Resumes** (`ResumesCard`), **Application history**
(`ApplicationHistory`), **Communications** (`CommunicationCard`), **Timeline**,
`CandidateActions` (edit / archive), and a link to `/candidates/[id]/activity`
("What's happened with {name}?").

## 11.3 Complete User Flow — create with a duplicate warning

```
Step 1  Recruiter clicks Add candidate
Step 2  (Optional) pastes a blurb, clicks Structure with AI
        → POST /api/candidates/ai-action  (Viewer denied)
        → structureCandidateText() returns proposals, saved:false
        → diff table: Field | Yours (kept) | AI suggestion
Step 3  Fills/accepts fields, clicks Save
Step 4  POST /api/candidates {name, email, phone, …}
Step 5  BACKEND: requireRole(["owner","admin","recruiter"])
        parseCandidatePayload(body,"create")
          - name required
          - email pattern + lowercase
          - phone ≥6 digits
          - MUST have email OR phone
          - skills deduped/capped
          - education & employment_history NORMALISED, not rejected
            (refusing the whole save because one row is half-filled would lose
             the other six)
Step 6  findDuplicateCandidates({email, phone}) runs BEFORE the insert
Step 7  If duplicates found AND acknowledge_duplicates is not true:
          → HTTP 409 {error:"This candidate may already exist.",
                      code:"duplicate_suspected", duplicates:[…]}
          → NOTHING IS CREATED YET
Step 8  UI shows the warning banner with each suspected match and what collided
Step 9  Recruiter re-submits with acknowledge_duplicates: true
Step 10 INSERT candidates (organization_id from the SESSION)
        DB trigger trg_candidates_normalize_contact fills
        email_normalized / phone_normalized
        DB trigger trg_candidates_mark_matches_stale invalidates match scores
Step 11 Best-effort INSERT into candidate_duplicates for later review
        (a failure here does NOT undo the candidate)
Step 12 logActivity "candidate.created" (+ "candidate.duplicate_flagged")
Step 13 RESPONSE 201 {data, duplicates}
```

**A suspected duplicate never blocks creation.** The same person legitimately
re-applies, and blocking would strand a real candidate.

## 11.4 API Flow

| API | Method | Auth | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `/api/candidates` | GET | any member | Filters validated by `filtersFromSearchParams()`; paginated |
| `/api/candidates` | POST | owner/admin/recruiter | **409 `duplicate_suspected`** on first pass; `acknowledge_duplicates:true` to proceed |
| `/api/candidates/[id]` | GET | any member | Returns the candidate **plus its duplicates**; 404 cross-tenant |
| `/api/candidates/[id]` | PATCH | owner/admin/recruiter | Translates the `candidates_contactable` CHECK into a readable message |
| `/api/candidates/[id]` | DELETE | **owner/admin** | **ARCHIVES**. 404 "Candidate not found, or already archived." |
| `/api/candidates/lookup` | GET | **any member** | `?q=` typeahead by name/email/phone |
| `/api/candidates/search` | POST | **any member incl. Viewer** | Two separated steps: AI parses the sentence into filters, then a **deterministic** query runs. AI never selects rows. |
| `/api/candidates/ai-action` | POST | owner/admin/recruiter | Structures a blurb; `saved:false` |
| `/api/candidates/[id]/resumes` | GET/POST | GET all roles / POST owner/admin/recruiter | See M09 |
| `/api/candidates/[id]/communication-preferences` | PUT | owner/admin/recruiter | See M21 |

**Natural-language search is deliberately two-phase.** `parseCandidateSearch()`
turns the sentence into structured filters; the database query is then ordinary
and deterministic. That separation is what makes the results reproducible and
keeps a Viewer's search read-only.

## 11.5 Database Flow

```
USER ACTION  Save candidate
 → API       POST /api/candidates
 → BACKEND   requireRole → parse → findDuplicateCandidates → (409 or continue)
 → DATABASE  INSERT public.candidates
               CHECK candidates_contactable (email OR phone)
               CHECK total_experience_years 0..60
               CHECK notice_period_days 0..365
               trigger trg_candidates_normalize_contact
               trigger trg_candidates_touch_updated_at
               trigger trg_candidates_mark_matches_stale
             INSERT public.candidate_duplicates (best effort)
             INSERT public.activity_events
 → RESPONSE  201 {data, duplicates}
 → UI        detail page + duplicate banner if any
```

Note `candidates."current_role"` is **quoted in DDL** because `current_role` is a
reserved word in PostgreSQL. PostgREST and TypeScript refer to it unquoted.

`trg_candidates_guard_delete` (`guard_candidate_delete_with_resumes()`) blocks a
hard delete when resumes exist.

| Test Case ID | Feature | Test Scenario | Steps | Expected Result | Actual | Status | Priority |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| TC-CAND-001 | Create | Name + email | Add candidate → name + email → Save | 201, detail page opens | | | Critical |
| TC-CAND-002 | Create | Name + phone only | No email | Accepted | | | Critical |
| TC-CAND-003 | Create | **Neither email nor phone** | Name only | "Add an email address or a phone number." | | | Critical |
| TC-CAND-004 | Create | Missing name | Email only | "A candidate name is required." | | | Critical |
| TC-CAND-005 | Create | 201-char name | Paste | "Name must be 200 characters or fewer." | | | High |
| TC-CAND-006 | Create | Invalid email | `rahul@` | "Enter a valid email address." | | | High |
| TC-CAND-007 | Create | Email is lowercased | `Rahul@Example.COM` | Stored `rahul@example.com` | | | High |
| TC-CAND-008 | Create | Phone with 5 digits | `12345` | "Enter a valid phone number." | | | High |
| TC-CAND-009 | Create | Phone with formatting | `+91 (98765) 43210` | Accepted; `phone_normalized` = last 10 digits | | | High |

| TC-CAND-015 | Create | Invalid source | `POST {"source":"psychic"}` | "Select a valid intake source." | | | Medium |
| TC-CAND-016 | Create | Half-filled education row | Leave one education row partly blank | **Save succeeds**; the row is normalised, the other rows survive | | | High |
| TC-CAND-017 | **Dedupe** | Same email | Create a candidate with an existing address | **409** `duplicate_suspected` with the matching record listed. **Nothing created yet** | | | Critical |
| TC-CAND-018 | Dedupe | Same phone, different format | Existing `9876543210`, enter `+91 98765 43210` | 409, matched on phone | | | Critical |
| TC-CAND-019 | Dedupe | Acknowledge and proceed | Re-submit with `acknowledge_duplicates: true` | 201 created; a `candidate_duplicates` row is recorded for review | | | Critical |
| TC-CAND-020 | Dedupe | Warning is visible | Trigger a duplicate in the UI | Attention-toned banner with "View the other record" | | | High |
| TC-CAND-021 | Dedupe | Duplicates on the detail page | Open a flagged candidate | Duplicates are listed | | | High |
| TC-CAND-022 | Dedupe | Duplicate insert failure doesn't roll back | Simulate a failure inserting `candidate_duplicates` | The **candidate still exists** | | | Medium |
| TC-CAND-023 | Edit | Partial update | Change location only | Everything else untouched | | | Critical |
| TC-CAND-024 | Edit | **Clear both contacts** | PATCH `{"email":null,"phone":null}` | "A candidate needs an email address or a phone number." | | | Critical |
| TC-CAND-025 | Edit | Clear one contact | PATCH `{"email":null}` with a phone on file | Allowed | | | High |
| TC-CAND-026 | Edit | DB CHECK is the backstop | Direct PostgREST update clearing both | Refused by `candidates_contactable`, translated to a readable message via the API | | | Critical |
| TC-CAND-027 | Edit | Viewer denied | Viewer PATCHes | 403 | | | Critical |
| TC-CAND-028 | Archive | Owner archives | Detail → archive | `archived_at` set; disappears from the default list | | | Critical |
| TC-CAND-029 | Archive | Recruiter denied | Recruiter DELETEs | 403 | | | Critical |
| TC-CAND-030 | Archive | Double archive | Twice | 404 "Candidate not found, or already archived." | | | High |
| TC-CAND-031 | Archive | Applications survive | Archive someone with applications | Applications still open and render the name | | | Critical |
| TC-CAND-032 | Search | Structured filters | Skills=Java, Location=Gurgaon, exp 4–7 | Only matching candidates | | | High |
| TC-CAND-033 | Search | Clear filters | Click Clear | All candidates return | | | Medium |
| TC-CAND-034 | NL search | Happy path | "Java candidates in Gurgaon with 4-7 years and less than 45 days notice" | Parsed into filters; matching results | | | Critical |
| TC-CAND-035 | NL search | **Deterministic** | Run the same query twice | Identical result sets | | | Critical |
| TC-CAND-036 | NL search | AI unavailable | Unset the key | Friendly message; **structured filters still work** | | | Critical |
| TC-CAND-037 | NL search | Nonsense query | "purple monkey dishwasher" | Empty or unfiltered results — **never an error page** | | | High |

| TC-CAND-039 | NL search | **Viewer can search** | As viewer | Search runs; results are read-only | | | High |
| TC-CAND-040 | NL search | Recruiter scope | As recruiter | Confirm whether results are org-wide or own-scope, and that it is consistent | | | High |

| TC-CAND-042 | AI structure | Empty paste | Click with an empty box | "Paste some candidate details to structure." | | | Medium |
| TC-CAND-043 | AI structure | Viewer denied | Viewer | 403 | | | High |
| TC-CAND-044 | Lookup | Typeahead | Type 2–3 chars into a candidate picker | Suggestions by name/email/phone | | | High |
| TC-CAND-045 | Tenant | Cross-tenant read | Org A requests org B's candidate | **404**, never 403 | | | Critical |
| TC-CAND-046 | Tenant | Dedupe does not leak | Create a candidate whose email exists in **org B** | **No duplicate warning** — dedupe is org-scoped | | | Critical |
| TC-CAND-047 | Tenant | Lookup does not leak | Search for an org B candidate's name | No results | | | Critical |
| TC-CAND-048 | Audit | Field names only | Edit a candidate, then read the audit log | The event records **which fields changed, not the values** — the log must not become a second copy of personal data | | | High |

## 11.7 UI/UX Checklist — M08

- [ ] Duplicate warning is impossible to miss and offers a link to the other record
- [ ] The AI diff table clearly marks which column is yours and which is the AI's
- [ ] Skill chips add on Enter and remove on click
- [ ] Repeatable education/employment rows can be added and removed
- [ ] The NL search field signals AI with an icon, not a label
- [ ] NL search shows a loading state and never blocks the structured filters
- [ ] Long skill lists wrap rather than overflowing
- [ ] Archived candidates are visually distinguished if shown
- [ ] Detail page tabs keep their state on refresh (or reset predictably)

## 11.8 Edge Cases — M08

| ID | Edge case | Expected |
| --- | --- | --- |
| EC-CAND-01 | Name in a non-Latin script | Stored and displayed correctly |
| EC-CAND-02 | Email exactly 320 chars | Accepted |
| EC-CAND-03 | Phone with letters `98765ABCDE` | Rejected (<6 digits after stripping) |
| EC-CAND-04 | Experience 0 | Accepted (fresher) |
| EC-CAND-05 | Notice 0 | Accepted (immediate joiner) |
| EC-CAND-06 | Three candidates sharing one phone | All created; each new one warns |
| EC-CAND-07 | Candidate with no skills | Allowed; match scoring degrades gracefully |
| EC-CAND-08 | Editing a candidate invalidates their match scores | `trg_candidates_mark_matches_stale` sets `is_stale` |
| EC-CAND-09 | Hard delete attempted while resumes exist | Blocked by `trg_candidates_guard_delete` |

## 11.9 Known Risks — M08

1. `candidates.email_normalized` has **no unique index**, so one address can
   legitimately belong to two records. Intake treats that as `email_ambiguous`.
2. The duplicate check is a two-round-trip protocol. If a client ignores the 409
   and always sends `acknowledge_duplicates: true`, the warning is bypassed
   entirely — test that the UI never does this by default.
3. Skill cap differs from jobs (40 vs 25).

---

# 12. MODULE M09 — Resumes & Resume AI Parsing

## 12.1 Purpose

Turn a PDF/Word file into structured candidate fields **without ever letting the
AI overwrite trusted data**. Upload → extract → parse → review a field-by-field
diff → apply only what the recruiter accepted.

**Who uses it:** Owner/Admin/Recruiter upload, parse and review; Viewer can list
and download.

## 12.2 The enforcement point

`lib/resumes/review.ts` — `applyReviewDecisions()` accepts **explicit per-field
decisions** and returns **only** the fields the recruiter chose to accept. A field
absent from the decisions is never written, so *the default outcome of doing
nothing is that nothing changes.*

Reviewable fields (`REVIEWABLE_FIELDS`): name, email, phone, location,
current_company, current_role, total_experience_years, expected_salary,
notice_period_days, skills.

## 12.3 Upload policy (`lib/resumes/uploadPolicy.ts`)

- Allowed: **`.pdf`, `.doc`, `.docx` only**.
- Rejection message, in one place so client and server agree:
  **"Only PDF and Word documents (.doc, .docx) are supported"**
- **Extension first, MIME as fallback.** Browsers disagree about `.doc`
  (`application/msword`, `application/octet-stream`, or nothing at all), and
  rejecting a real Word document because the browser mislabelled it would be an
  unexplainable failure.
- Max **10 MB**, matching the bucket's `file_size_limit`.
- The extractor (`extract.ts`) can also read `.txt`, but the *upload* policy is
  narrower on purpose.
- `MAX_EXTRACTED_CHARACTERS = 40,000`; `MIN_USEFUL_CHARACTERS = 120` — below that
  the UI must say the file yielded almost no text (a scanned image PDF) rather
  than blaming the AI.

## 12.4 UI Components

| UI Component | Purpose | User Action | Expected Result |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `ResumesCard` on `/candidates/[id]` | List | — | "Uploaded resumes"; each row shows parse status |
| `/candidates/[id]/resume` + `ResumeUploader` | Upload | Choose a file | `accept` = `RESUME_UPLOAD_ACCEPT` |
| **Parse** action | Run AI | Click | `POST /api/resumes/[id]/parse` |
| **Review parsed resume** | Open the diff | Click | → `/candidates/[id]/resume/review` |
| `ReviewForm` diff table | Decide | Per field: keep yours / take the AI's | Only accepted fields are written |
| "Also found in the resume" | Extra data | — | Fields with no current value |
| "No change needed" / "Nothing to review" | Empty states | — | Shown when the parse proposes nothing new |
| **Download** | Retrieve the file | Click | `GET /api/resumes/[id]/download` → short-lived signed URL |

## 12.5 Complete User Flow

```
Step 1  Recruiter opens /candidates/{id}/resume
Step 2  Chooses a PDF, uploads
Step 3  POST /api/candidates/{id}/resumes  (multipart)
        requireRole(["owner","admin","recruiter"])
        404 if the candidate is not this org's
        400 "Upload a file." / "Choose a resume file to upload."
        400 "That file is larger than 10 MB."
        400 unsupported type → RESUME_UPLOAD_REJECTION
        → uploads to the PRIVATE `resumes` bucket at
          <organization_id>/<candidate_id>/<uuid>-<filename>
        → INSERT resumes (file_url, file_name, file_type, file_size_bytes,
          file_hash SHA-256, parse_status 'pending')
Step 4  Recruiter clicks Parse
Step 5  POST /api/resumes/{id}/parse
        - extractResumeText() by file kind (unpdf / mammoth / word-extractor)
        - very little text recovered → 422 with an honest reason
        - parseResume() → schema-validated structured output
        - UPSERT resume_parse_results (UNIQUE on resume_id — re-parsing replaces)
        - resumes.parse_status / parsed_at / extracted_characters updated
        - logAiCall for the audit trail
Step 6  Recruiter opens Review changes
Step 7  buildFieldComparisons(candidate, parsed) → a diff per field
Step 8  Recruiter decides per field, clicks Apply
Step 9  POST /api/resumes/{id}/review {decisions}
        409 "This resume hasn't been parsed yet." if no parse result
        applyReviewDecisions() returns ONLY accepted fields
        UPDATE candidates with exactly those
        400 if the update would clear both contacts
        resume_parse_results.applied_fields / reviewed_by / reviewed_at recorded
Step 10 Candidate profile now shows the accepted values only
```

**File hash purpose:** the cost model forbids re-parsing an unchanged file, so
`file_hash` makes that a lookup rather than a re-upload.

## 12.6 API Flow

| API | Method | Auth | Errors |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `/api/candidates/[id]/resumes` | GET | **all four roles** | 404 candidate |
| `/api/candidates/[id]/resumes` | POST | owner/admin/recruiter | 400 no file / >10 MB / unsupported type; 404 candidate; 400 "Could not save that resume." |
| `/api/resumes/[id]/parse` | POST | owner/admin/recruiter | 404 resume; **422** when too little text was recovered; AI codes |
| `/api/resumes/[id]/review` | POST | owner/admin/recruiter | 404 resume/candidate; **409 not parsed yet**; 400 contactability; 400 "Could not apply those changes." |
| `/api/resumes/[id]/download` | GET | any member | 404 resume; error if the signed URL cannot be created |

## 12.7 Database Flow

```
resumes (id, organization_id, candidate_id, file_url, file_name, file_type,
         file_size_bytes, file_hash, parse_status, parse_error,
         extracted_characters, uploaded_by, uploaded_at, parsed_at)
   │ 1:1 (UNIQUE resume_id)
   ▼
resume_parse_results (id, organization_id, resume_id, raw_json jsonb,
         confidence numeric(3,2) 0..1, applied_fields text[],
         reviewed_by, reviewed_at, created_at)
```

`confidence` is **advisory only** — it never gates anything on its own.

Storage RLS on `storage.objects` authorises on the **first path segment**
(`organization_id`): SELECT for any member, INSERT for owner/admin/recruiter,
DELETE for owner/admin only.

## 12.9 UI/UX Checklist — M09

- [ ] Upload shows progress and a clear success/failure per file
- [ ] Parse status is visible on every resume row (pending / parsed / failed)
- [ ] The diff table's two columns are unmistakably labelled
- [ ] Per-field controls default to **keep yours**
- [ ] Empty states differ: "Nothing to review", "No change needed", "Nothing to decide"
- [ ] A low-text extraction explains the file, not the AI
- [ ] Download opens in a new tab and does not leak the storage path into the URL bar permanently
- [ ] Viewer sees the list and Download but no Upload/Parse/Apply

## 12.10 Known Risks — M09

1. A resume parse can propose a **name** change. Accepting it renames the person
   — verify the diff makes that obvious.
2. `confidence` is stored but advisory; make sure the UI does not present it as
   a gate.
3. Re-parsing replaces the previous result, destroying the earlier proposal.
   `applied_fields` is the only record of what was accepted before.

# 13. MODULE M10 — Applications Management

## 13.1 Purpose

An application is one candidate against one job. It is the row every later module
hangs data off: matches, screening calls, reports, evaluations, interviews,
submissions, onboarding.

**Who uses it:** Owner/Admin/Recruiter create, move and annotate; Owner/Admin
archive; Viewer reads. A **Recruiter's list shows their own plus unassigned**.

## 13.2 The stage model (`lib/applications/stages.ts`)

**Board stages, in order:**
`applied → shortlisted → ai_screening_call → phone_interview → video_interview →
written_assessment → director_round → hired`

**Terminal exits, reachable from ANY stage (not board columns):** `rejected`,
`withdrawn`.

**Phases** (derived, never stored — `lib/applications/phase.ts`):

| Phase | Stages |
| --- | --- |
| screening | applied, shortlisted, ai_screening_call |
| interviewing | phone_interview, video_interview |
| final | written_assessment, director_round |
| closed | hired, rejected, withdrawn |

**Transition rule (`canTransition`)** — deliberately permissive about
**direction**: recruiters legitimately move applications backwards. What it
forbids is **resurrecting a finished application**: from `hired`, `rejected` or
`withdrawn`, nothing is allowed, with the message *"This application is already
{stage}. Create a new application to consider this candidate again."* Reopening a
hire or rejection silently would corrupt the funnel that analytics reports on.

`DEFAULT_APPLICATION_STAGE = "applied"` is a typed constant referenced by every
creation path — because the Supabase client has no generated row types here, a
string literal that stopped being valid would keep compiling and fail at runtime
(exactly what happened when migration 0021 renamed `new` → `applied`).

`rejected_at_stage` is stamped by `trg_applications_stamp_rejected_stage` — a
candidate turned down after a director round and one turned down on their CV are
not the same outcome.

**Priority** (`low` | `normal` | `high`) is recruiter-set, **not** derived from
stage or SLA. `normal` is deliberately a neutral chip: a board where every card
wears a colour communicates nothing.

## 13.3 The candidate-field refusal (`lib/applications/validation.ts`)

`name`, `email`, `phone` (plus `email_normalized`, `phone_normalized`, `candidate`,
`candidate_name`, `candidate_email`, `candidate_phone`) are **rejected outright**
by `PATCH /api/applications/:id` with HTTP **422** and the message:

> "Candidate name, email and phone are edited on the candidate's own page, not here."

The check uses `field in payload`, so **`{"email": null}` counts** — that is an
attempt to erase an address, not an absence of intent. One candidate can hold five
applications; letting any application edit their phone number means five screens
racing to own one fact.

## 13.4 UI Components

### `/applications` — list
Filters (`ApplicationFilters`): stage, job, candidate, recruiter, archived.
Sorted by **stage order**, not `updated_at` — a recruiter reading the list is
asking "where is everyone?", and answering with "whoever I touched last" makes
them rebuild the pipeline in their head. `StageBadge` per row.

### `/applications/new` — `NewApplicationForm`
"Link a candidate to a job": a `CandidatePicker` (placeholder "Name or email"),
a job select, optional stage/source/recruiter. Empty state links to "Add some on the job".

### `/applications/[id]` — detail (539 lines)
| Card / Component | Purpose |
| --- | --- |
| `StageStepper` | Current stage in board context |
| `ApplicationStageSection` | Move stage (dropdown limited by the job's **enabled** stages) |
| `NextActionBanner` | What to do next (`lib/evaluation/nextAction.ts`) |
| `ApplicationEditForm` | Priority, source, assigned recruiter |
| Notes | placeholder "Add a note — what happened, what's next…" |
| Timeline | Stage history + notes + activity |
| **Match** card | "No match calculated" → link to `/match` |
| **AI screening call** card | "Start a call", "Attempts", "What the candidate hears first" |
| **Screening report** card | "No report yet" / "What the candidate said" / "Regenerate" |
| `EvaluationPanel` + `EvaluationEntryForm` | "Score (1-10)", "Outcome", "Summary" |
| `ScheduleInterview` | "Date & time", "Prepare for this interview" |
| **Interview brief** card | "Suggested questions" |
| **Client submission** card | "Draft a submission" / "Submit again" / "Already submitted" |
| `SendMessagePanel` | Manual candidate message (M21) |
| `CommunicationLog` | What has been sent |
| `ApplicationHeaderActions` | Archive etc. |

## 13.5 Complete User Flow — move a stage (and everything it sets off)

```
USER ACTION   Recruiter picks "Shortlisted" in the stage control
→ FRONTEND    PATCH /api/applications/{id} {stage:"shortlisted"}
→ BACKEND     requireRole(["owner","admin","recruiter"])
              rejectCandidateFields(body) → 422 if name/email/phone present
              load the current row → 404 if not this org's
              isApplicationStage(stage) → 400 "Invalid stage."
              canTransition(from,to) → 409 with the terminal-stage reason
→ DATABASE    UPDATE applications SET stage
                trigger trg_applications_stage_history
                  → closes the open application_stage_history row (exited_at)
                  → opens a new one (entered_at, changed_by)
                trigger trg_applications_stamp_rejected_stage (if → rejected)
                trigger trg_applications_create_onboarding (if → hired)
                trigger trg_applications_touch_updated_at
→ AUDIT       logActivityBatch: application.stage_changed
              (+ application.recruiter_assigned when that changed)
→ NOTIFY      notify("assigned_to_application") — ONLY when the assignee
              actually changed AND it is not the actor assigning to themselves
→ CANDIDATE   trySendForEvent(EVENT_FOR_STAGE[stage])   ← BEFORE automations
              (so the candidate hears about the stage a PERSON moved them to,
               not one an automation moved them to a second later)
              No entry for phone/video interview: those messages state a TIME,
              and a stage move has none — they fire from scheduling instead.
→ AUTOMATION  dispatch(trigger:"application_stage_changed")
              AWAITED (a serverless freeze would strand a half-run automation
              with its claim row written) but WRAPPED — a failing automation
              must never fail the manual action
→ RESPONSE    {data: application}
→ UI          router.refresh(); stepper, timeline and banner all update
```

## 13.6 API Flow

| API | Method | Auth | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `/api/applications` | GET | any member | `?stage ?job_id ?candidate_id ?recruiter_id ?archived`. Recruiter sees own + unassigned |
| `/api/applications` | POST | owner/admin/recruiter | 400 "Select a candidate." / "Select a job." / invalid stage / invalid source / invalid recruiter; **409 "This candidate has already been applied to that job."**; 404 candidate or job not found |
| `/api/applications/[id]` | GET | any member | Returns stage history + notes |
| `/api/applications/[id]` | PATCH | owner/admin/recruiter | **422 candidate fields**; 400 invalid stage/priority/source/score; **409 terminal transition**; 404 |
| `/api/applications/[id]` | DELETE | **owner/admin** | ARCHIVES |
| `/api/applications/[id]/notes` | POST | owner/admin/recruiter | 400 "Write something before saving the note." / length cap; 404 |
| `/api/applications/[id]/ai-action` | POST | **any member** | One-paragraph summary; facts gathered server-side; `saved:false` |

**Uniqueness:** `applications` has `UNIQUE (candidate_id, job_id)`. Without it a
double submission creates two pipelines for one person and the funnel counts them
twice.

## 13.8 UI/UX Checklist — M10

- [ ] The stage stepper shows position without stray numbering artefacts
- [ ] The move dropdown lists only stages the job actually has, plus the two exits
- [ ] `NextActionBanner` gives one clear next step
- [ ] Notes appear immediately after saving (`router.refresh()`)
- [ ] The timeline reads chronologically and distinguishes stage moves from notes
- [ ] Cards for unavailable features say why ("No match calculated", "No report yet")
- [ ] A terminal application makes it visually obvious that no moves are possible
- [ ] Priority chips: high = warning tone; normal/low = neutral
- [ ] Long candidate/job names truncate

## 13.9 Known Risks — M10

1. **A very long detail page.** Twelve cards on one route; check that a slow card
   (AI, screening) does not block the rest.
2. The dispatch is **awaited**, so a slow automation makes the stage change feel
   slow. Confirm the UI shows a busy state.
3. `EVENT_FOR_STAGE` has no phone/video entries by design — a tester expecting a
   message on those moves will think it is broken. It fires from scheduling instead.
