# 14. MODULE M11 — AI Matching

## 14.1 Purpose

Score how well a candidate fits a job, split into a **deterministic** half built
from checkable facts and an optional **semantic** half from the AI — and make sure
the number and the explanation can never contradict each other.

## 14.2 How the score is built

**CODE owns the number.** The model cannot state a score at all, so it cannot
state one that disagrees with the facts beside it.

### Deterministic component weights (`COMPONENT_WEIGHTS`, overridable per job)

| Component | Weight |
| --- | --- |
| skills | 40 |
| experience | 20 |
| salary | 15 |
| location | 15 |
| notice | 10 |

**Missing data is SKIPPED, not scored zero**, and the remaining weights are
renormalised. Scoring a blank field as zero would punish an incomplete profile as
though it were a bad fit — a different and much more damaging claim.

- Skills: literal, case-insensitive overlap. Equivalence judgement is the AI's job.
- Location: loose substring either way — "Gurgaon" matches "Gurgaon, Haryana";
  genuinely different cities do not match.
- Notice thresholds: `COMFORTABLE = 30`, `ACCEPTABLE = 60`, `LONG = 90` days.

### Combination (`lib/matching/score.ts`)

`SEMANTIC_WEIGHT = 0.3`, `DETERMINISTIC_WEIGHT = 0.7` (a job may override).
The semantic minority share is deliberate: the deterministic half rests on
recorded facts, the semantic half on judgement.

Semantic score = `roleSimilarity*0.6 + seniorityFit*0.4`, lifted by confident
skill equivalences (capped at 1 — the model cannot claim to cover more skills
than were missing).

**When AI is unavailable**, `semantic` is `null`, the deterministic score **is**
the overall score, and `ai_used = false` so the UI says so rather than implying a
semantic read happened.

### The three labelled lists
`strong_matches`, `gaps`, `needs_verification` — each entry records whether it was
**checked by code** or **assessed by AI**, so a recruiter can tell a fact from a
judgement.

## 14.3 Staleness

`application_matches.is_stale` is set by database triggers — **not** recalculated
on every page view (the cost model forbids that):

- `trg_candidates_mark_matches_stale` — the candidate's profile changed
- `trg_jobs_mark_matches_stale` — the job's requirements changed

`trg_matches_sync_application_score` keeps `applications.match_score` in step.

`UNIQUE (application_id)` — one current match per application; recalculation replaces it.

## 14.4 UI + API Flow

`/applications/[id]/match` → `MatchView`.

| API | Method | Auth | Behaviour |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `/api/applications/[id]/match` | GET | any member | Calculates on demand when absent or stale — **but a Viewer never triggers a calculation**; they see what exists. Returns `{data, calculated}` |
| `/api/applications/[id]/match` | POST | owner/admin/recruiter | Forces a recalculation. 404 when "not found" appears in the error, else 400 |

## 14.5 Test Cases

| Test Case ID | Feature | Test Scenario | Steps | Expected Result | Actual | Status | Priority |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| TC-MATCH-001 | Calculate | First view | Open `/applications/{id}/match` as a recruiter | A score is calculated and shown with the three lists | | | Critical |
| TC-MATCH-002 | Calculate | **Viewer does not trigger** | Open the same page as a Viewer on an application with no match | Shows "no match yet" — **does not create one** | | | Critical |
| TC-MATCH-003 | Determinism | Same inputs, same score | Recalculate twice with nothing changed and AI off | Identical deterministic score | | | Critical |
| TC-MATCH-004 | Weights | Skills dominate | Candidate matching all required skills but nothing else | Score reflects the 40-point weight | | | High |
| TC-MATCH-005 | **Missing data** | Blank salary | Candidate with no expected salary | Salary is **skipped and weights renormalised** — the score is not depressed as if it were a bad fit | | | Critical |
| TC-MATCH-006 | Missing data | Candidate with nothing but a name | Score is computed from whatever exists, with honest lists | | | High |
| TC-MATCH-007 | Skills | Case-insensitive | Job wants "Java", candidate has "java" | Matched | | | High |
| TC-MATCH-008 | Location | Substring match | Job "Gurgaon", candidate "Gurgaon, Haryana" | Matched | | | High |
| TC-MATCH-009 | Location | Different cities | Job "Pune", candidate "Chennai" | Not matched | | | High |
| TC-MATCH-010 | Notice | Comfortable | 15 days | Scores at the comfortable tier | | | Medium |
| TC-MATCH-011 | Notice | Long | 120 days | Scores at the long tier | | | Medium |
| TC-MATCH-012 | **AI off** | No key | Unset `OPENAI_API_KEY`, calculate | Deterministic score stands alone; **`ai_used = false` and the UI says AI was not consulted** | | | Critical |
| TC-MATCH-013 | AI error | Provider down | Point at a dead host | `ai_error` recorded; the match is still valid and labelled deterministic-only | | | Critical |
| TC-MATCH-014 | **Consistency** | Lists vs score | Compare `strong_matches` / `gaps` with the number | Internally consistent — a score of 90 with three gaps and no strengths is a bug | | | Critical |
| TC-MATCH-015 | Provenance | Fact vs judgement | Read each list entry | Each says whether code checked it or AI assessed it | | | High |
| TC-MATCH-016 | Staleness | **Candidate edit** | Change the candidate's skills | `is_stale` becomes true; the UI offers a recalculation | | | Critical |
| TC-MATCH-017 | Staleness | **Job edit** | Change the job's required skills | `is_stale` becomes true | | | Critical |
| TC-MATCH-018 | Staleness | Not recalculated on every view | Open the match page 5 times with nothing changed | Only **one** calculation happens | | | High |
| TC-MATCH-019 | Sync | `applications.match_score` | After a calculation | The application row's score matches `application_matches.overall_score` | | | High |
| TC-MATCH-020 | Uniqueness | Recalculate | Force recalculation | Still exactly one `application_matches` row | | | High |
| TC-MATCH-021 | Bounds | Score range | Any calculation | `0 ≤ overall_score ≤ 100` (DB CHECK) | | | High |
| TC-MATCH-022 | Perms | Recruiter can recalculate | POST as recruiter | Allowed | | | High |
| TC-MATCH-023 | Perms | Viewer cannot | POST as viewer | 403 | | | Critical |
| TC-MATCH-024 | Tenant | Cross-tenant | GET org B's application match | 404 | | | Critical |
| TC-MATCH-025 | Weights | Per-job override | Set custom weights in the Resume Score stage config | The calculation uses them | | | High |

## 14.6 UI/UX Checklist — M11

- [ ] The score is the largest element; the lists explain it
- [ ] "AI not used" is stated plainly when it was not
- [ ] Stale matches are visibly marked with a way to recalculate
- [ ] Each finding shows its provenance (checked vs assessed)
- [ ] The recalculate button shows a busy state and cannot be double-fired

## 14.7 Known Risks — M11

1. **`GET` can write.** A recruiter's page view triggers a calculation, which
   costs an AI call. Confirm the staleness guard is doing its job.
2. Location matching is substring-based, so "Delhi" matches "New Delhi" **and**
   any string containing "delhi". Test a deliberately confusing pair.
3. Weight overrides live in the job's stage config; a mis-set weight silently
   changes every score for that job.

---

# 15. MODULE M12 — AI Screening Calls (Bolna)

## 15.1 Purpose

Place an automated outbound voice call to a candidate, ask the job's screening
questions, and bring back a transcript. **Everything a real person hears is
regulated**, so this module is designed to fail closed at every step.

**Who uses it:** Owner/Admin/Recruiter trigger; Viewer reads. A Viewer must
**never** be able to make the product telephone someone.

## 15.2 The consent disclosure — non-negotiable

`buildCallScript()` **always** emits the consent disclosure as the **first
segment**. There is no parameter to disable it, and a unit test asserts it cannot
be removed. It states three things: who is calling, that the call is automated,
and that it may be recorded — then makes continuing an explicit, informed choice.

Segment kinds, in order: `consent` → `greeting` → `briefing` → `question`… → `closing`.

The job's stage prompt lands as a **`briefing`, after consent and after the
greeting**. It **supplements** the script; it can never replace or precede the
disclosure. A recruiter-authored prompt that could displace it would be a
compliance hole with a text box in front of it.

Caps: `MAX_QUESTIONS = 12` (beyond that a call stops being a screen),
`MAX_INSTRUCTIONS_LENGTH = 2000` (so one job cannot turn a screen into a monologue).

**Recording someone without telling them is unlawful in many jurisdictions.**
Treat any failure here as **Critical**, not cosmetic.

## 15.3 Retry policy (`lib/screening/retry.ts`)

| Setting | Default | Bound |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `maxAttempts` | **3** | `MAX_ALLOWED_ATTEMPTS = 5` |
| `delayMinutes` | **120** | `MIN_ALLOWED_DELAY_MINUTES = 15` |

**Retryable:** `no_answer`, `busy`, `failed`.
**Terminal (never auto-retried):** `completed`, `answered`, `cancelled`,
**`callback_requested`**.

`callback_requested` is the important one: the candidate asked to be called at a
different time. Dialling them again on our schedule ignores what they said. A
human decides when.

The attempt cap does double duty — a **cost cap** (the Bolna call is the highest
single-event cost) and **decency** (three unanswered calls is persistence; ten is
harassment, and a runaway automation that dialled someone repeatedly would be a
serious harm).

## 15.4 UI Components

| UI Component | Location | Purpose |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **AI screening call** card | `/applications/[id]` | Status, attempts |
| **Start a call** button | `/applications/[id]/screening-call` (`StartCallPanel`) | Explicit human trigger |
| "What the candidate hears first" | same | **Shows the consent disclosure before you dial** |
| "Attempts" counter | same | Against the configured cap |
| "Call transcript" | same | After the webhook lands |
| `/screening-calls` list | global | All calls with `CallStatusBadge` |
| `/settings/screening` (`ScreeningForm`) | settings | Max attempts, retry delay, language, recording |

## 15.5 Complete User Flow

```
Step 1  Recruiter opens /applications/{id}/screening-call
Step 2  The panel SHOWS the consent disclosure text ("What the candidate hears
        first") before any dialling is possible
Step 3  Recruiter clicks Start a call
Step 4  POST /api/applications/{id}/screening-call
        requireRole(["owner","admin","recruiter"])   ← Viewer can never dial
Step 5  startScreeningCall():
          - Bolna integration must be CONNECTED (fails closed if not)
          - retry policy read from organization_settings.screening_settings
          - attempt cap enforced
          - buildCallScript() with the job's questions + rendered stage prompt
          - the provider is called with a metadata.screening_call_id = OUR id
Step 6  DATABASE INSERT screening_calls
          status 'queued', attempt_number, triggered_by,
          consent_confirmed FALSE
          trigger trg_screening_calls_consent (stamp_screening_consent)
          trigger trg_screening_calls_tenant_integrity
Step 7  RESPONSE 201 {data:{id}}
...call happens...
Step 8  Bolna POSTs /api/webhooks/bolna
          NO SESSION. Three constraints:
            1. HMAC-SHA256 over the RAW BYTES, constant-time compare.
               No BOLNA_WEBHOOK_SECRET → 503, every request rejected.
               Bad/missing signature → 401 (deliberately vague).
            2. TENANCY COMES FROM OUR ROW. screening_calls is looked up by the
               id WE generated; organization_id is read from that row.
               A forged organization_id in the payload is IGNORED.
            3. It can only advance ONE KNOWN CALL. It cannot create rows and
               cannot touch anything outside screening_calls.
          Unknown call id → 200 {received:true, matched:false}
            (200 so the provider stops retrying an event we will never accept)
          Update failure → 500 so the provider RETRIES
            (losing a transcript silently is worse)
Step 9  Recorded: status, ended_at, transcript, recording_url,
        duration_seconds, failure_reason (truncated to 500 chars)
        consent_confirmed set ONLY if the candidate heard the disclosure and
        did not decline (isConsentRefusal())
Step 10 Notification + activity event; automations on `screening_call_completed`
        (a SERVICE-mode trigger, since there is no session)
```

## 15.6 API Flow

| API | Method | Auth | Errors |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `/api/applications/[id]/screening-call` | GET | any member | Lists every attempt |
| `/api/applications/[id]/screening-call` | POST | owner/admin/recruiter | Errors carry a `code`; integration-disconnected, attempt-cap and no-phone all surface distinctly |
| `/api/screening-calls` | GET | any member | 400 invalid status filter |
| `/api/webhooks/bolna` | POST | **HMAC only** | 503 not configured; 401 invalid signature; 400 invalid JSON / missing call reference; 503 no service-role client; 200 unmatched; 500 write failure (to force a retry) |

## 15.7 Database Flow

`screening_calls (id, organization_id, application_id, provider CHECK IN ('bolna'),
status, attempt_number ≥1, provider_call_id, scheduled_for, started_at, ended_at,
duration_seconds ≥0, recording_url, transcript, language, **consent_confirmed
NOT NULL DEFAULT false**, consent_confirmed_at, failure_reason, triggered_by,
CHECK ended_at >= started_at)`

## 15.8 Test Cases

| Test Case ID | Feature | Test Scenario | Steps | Expected Result | Actual | Status | Priority |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| TC-CALL-001 | **Consent** | Disclosure is shown first | Open the screening-call panel | "What the candidate hears first" shows the disclosure **before** any Start button is usable | | | Critical |
| TC-CALL-002 | **Consent** | Disclosure cannot be removed | Try to configure a stage prompt that replaces the opening | The disclosure still comes first; the prompt lands as a `briefing` after the greeting | | | Critical |
| TC-CALL-003 | Consent | Disclosure names all three facts | Read it | Who is calling, that it is automated, that it may be recorded | | | Critical |
| TC-CALL-004 | Consent | Not stamped until confirmed | Start a call, before any webhook | `consent_confirmed = false` | | | Critical |
| TC-CALL-005 | Consent | Refusal | Webhook with a refusal `consent_answer` | `consent_confirmed` stays **false** | | | Critical |
| TC-CALL-006 | Trigger | Happy path | Bolna connected, candidate has a phone → Start a call | 201; a `queued` row appears | | | Critical |
| TC-CALL-007 | Trigger | **Bolna disconnected** | Disconnect Bolna, Start a call | Refused with a clear reason. **No call is placed** | | | Critical |
| TC-CALL-008 | Trigger | No phone number | Candidate with email only | Refused with a distinct reason | | | Critical |
| TC-CALL-009 | Trigger | **Viewer denied** | As viewer, POST | **403** — a Viewer must never make the product telephone anyone | | | Critical |
| TC-CALL-010 | Trigger | Recruiter allowed | As recruiter | Allowed | | | High |
| TC-CALL-011 | Trigger | Cross-tenant | POST to org B's application | 404 | | | Critical |
| TC-CALL-012 | **Retry** | Cap respected | Set max attempts to 3, fail 3 calls, try a 4th | Refused; attempt count stops at 3 | | | Critical |
| TC-CALL-013 | Retry | Delay respected | Set 120 min, retry immediately | Refused until the delay has elapsed | | | Critical |
| TC-CALL-014 | Retry | **callback_requested never auto-retried** | Set that status, wait past the delay | **No automatic retry** | | | Critical |
| TC-CALL-015 | Retry | Terminal statuses | `completed`, `answered`, `cancelled` | No retry | | | High |
| TC-CALL-016 | Retry | Retryable statuses | `no_answer`, `busy`, `failed` | Retry allowed after the delay | | | High |
| TC-CALL-017 | Settings | Attempts clamped to 5 | Set 10 in `/settings/screening` | Clamped to 5 | | | High |
| TC-CALL-018 | Settings | Delay floored at 15 | Set 1 minute | Clamped to 15 | | | High |
| TC-CALL-019 | Script | Question cap | Job with 20 screening questions | Only 12 asked | | | Medium |
| TC-CALL-020 | Script | Instruction cap | 3,000-char stage prompt | Truncated at 2,000 | | | Medium |
| TC-CALL-021 | Script | Tokens resolved at run time | Prompt contains `{{candidate.name}}` | Resolved **for this candidate only**; the stored template still holds the token | | | Critical |
| TC-CALL-022 | **Webhook** | No secret configured | Unset `BOLNA_WEBHOOK_SECRET`, POST anything | **503**, every request rejected | | | Critical |
| TC-CALL-023 | Webhook | Missing signature | POST with no signature header | **401** | | | Critical |
| TC-CALL-024 | Webhook | Wrong signature | POST with a bad HMAC | **401**, vague message | | | Critical |
| TC-CALL-025 | Webhook | **Tampered body** | Sign a body, then alter one byte | 401 — the HMAC covers the **raw bytes** | | | Critical |
| TC-CALL-026 | Webhook | **Forged organization_id** | Valid signature, payload claims another org | Ignored — tenancy comes from **our** `screening_calls` row | | | Critical |
| TC-CALL-027 | Webhook | Unknown call id | Valid signature, id we never issued | **200** `{received:true, matched:false}` — the provider must stop retrying | | | High |
| TC-CALL-028 | Webhook | Missing call reference | No `metadata.screening_call_id` | 400 "Missing call reference." | | | High |
| TC-CALL-029 | Webhook | Invalid JSON | Signed garbage | 400 "Invalid JSON." | | | Medium |
| TC-CALL-030 | Webhook | Write failure retries | Simulate a DB failure | **500**, so the provider retries — a silently lost transcript is worse | | | High |
| TC-CALL-031 | Webhook | Transcript recorded | Valid completed payload | Transcript, recording URL, duration and status all stored | | | Critical |
| TC-CALL-032 | Webhook | Long failure reason | 2,000-char `error` | Truncated to 500 chars | | | Low |
| TC-CALL-033 | Webhook | Cannot create rows | Signed payload for a non-existent call | Nothing is inserted | | | Critical |
| TC-CALL-034 | List | `/screening-calls` | Open the page | Every call with a status badge | | | High |
| TC-CALL-035 | List | Invalid status filter | `?status=nonsense` | 400 "Invalid status filter." | | | Medium |
| TC-CALL-036 | Constraint | `ended_at >= started_at` | Try to write an earlier end | DB CHECK refuses | | | Medium |
| TC-CALL-037 | Dashboard | Failed calls tile | Create a failed call | The `failedCalls` tile turns red | | | High |
| TC-CALL-038 | Automation | Service-mode trigger | Active rule on `screening_call_completed` | Fires from the webhook (no session) via the **service-role** client | | | High |

## 15.9 Known Risks — M12

1. The webhook is the **only unauthenticated endpoint** in the product. It is
   well-constrained, but it deserves the most adversarial testing you can give it.
2. `screening_call_completed` runs in **service** mode, and `ACTION_MODES` marks
   `start_screening_call`, `generate_screening_report` and `calculate_match` as
   **session-only**. A rule pairing them is refused at activation — verify that
   refusal is legible.
3. Bolna is a real-money, real-person integration. Never test against production
   credentials with a real phone number.

---

# 16. MODULE M13 — AI Screening Report

## 16.1 Purpose

Turn a call transcript into structured facts a recruiter can act on — interest
level, expected CTC, notice period, location acceptance, availability — while
keeping **what the AI originally said** permanently distinguishable from **what a
human corrected**.

## 16.2 The two rules that define this module

### 1. Eligibility — consent is checked FIRST

`checkReportEligibility(call)` refuses in three ways:

| Code | When | Message |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `no_consent` | `consent_confirmed = false` | "No consent was recorded on this call, so its transcript can't be used. The candidate must be told the call is recorded and agree to continue." |
| `not_completed` | the call did not complete | — |
| `no_transcript` | no transcript text | — |

Consent is checked **before** anything else, deliberately: without confirmation the
recording is not ours to process, regardless of how good the transcript is. The
database enforces the same rule via `trg_screening_reports_preconditions`
(`enforce_screening_report_preconditions()`), so this is the friendly-message half.

### 2. The AI's original words are frozen forever

`screening_reports` carries **two parallel sets** of columns:

| Current (editable) | Original AI (never updated after insert) |
| --- | --- |
| `summary_text` | `ai_summary_text` |
| `interest_level` | `ai_interest_level` |
| `expected_ctc` | `ai_expected_ctc` |
| `notice_period_days` | `ai_notice_period_days` |
| `location_accepted` | `ai_location_accepted` |
| `availability_notes` | `ai_availability_notes` |

Plus `uncertain_fields[]` (fields the model itself flagged, surfaced so a
recruiter checks them first) and `corrected_fields[]` (fields a human changed).

`trg_screening_reports_protect_ai` (`protect_ai_screening_original()`) **refuses
any update to an `ai_*` column**. The API translates that into
*"The original AI extraction cannot be edited."*

`UNIQUE (screening_call_id)` — one report per call; regenerating replaces it.

## 16.3 UI Components

`/applications/[id]/screening-report` → `ReportEditor` (316 lines).
Cards on the application detail page: "Screening report", "No report yet",
"What the candidate said", **Regenerate**.

## 16.4 Complete User Flow

```
Step 1  A call completes with consent and a transcript
Step 2  Recruiter opens /applications/{id}/screening-report
Step 3  Clicks Generate (or Regenerate)
Step 4  POST /api/applications/{id}/screening-report
        requireRole(["owner","admin","recruiter"])
          — generating costs an AI call, so a Viewer cannot spend it
Step 5  checkReportEligibility(latest completed call)
        → refuses with a code + reason if consent/completion/transcript missing
Step 6  generateScreeningSummary() → schema-validated extraction
Step 7  INSERT screening_reports with BOTH the current values and the frozen ai_*
        copies, plus uncertain_fields[]
Step 8  RESPONSE 201 {data:{id}}
Step 9  Recruiter reads the report; uncertain fields are highlighted first
Step 10 Recruiter corrects a value, clicks Save
Step 11 POST /api/screening-reports/{id}/review
        - updates only the CURRENT columns
        - records the field in corrected_fields[]
        - sets reviewed_by / reviewed_at
        - ANY attempt to write an ai_* column → 400
          "The original AI extraction cannot be edited."
Step 12 The report now shows: what the AI said, what a human changed, and by whom
```

## 16.5 API Flow

| API | Method | Auth | Errors |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `/api/applications/[id]/screening-report` | GET | **all four roles** | — |
| `/api/applications/[id]/screening-report` | POST | owner/admin/recruiter | Eligibility failures returned as `{error, code}` with a mapped status |
| `/api/screening-reports/[id]/review` | POST | owner/admin/recruiter | 404 report; **400 "The original AI extraction cannot be edited."**; 400 "Could not save that review." |

## 16.6 Test Cases

| Test Case ID | Feature | Test Scenario | Steps | Expected Result | Actual | Status | Priority |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| TC-REP-001 | Generate | Happy path | Completed call, consent true, transcript present | Report generated with all six fields | | | Critical |
| TC-REP-002 | **Eligibility** | **No consent** | Set `consent_confirmed = false`, generate | Refused with `no_consent` and the exact message about telling the candidate | | | Critical |
| TC-REP-003 | Eligibility | Not completed | Call still `queued` | Refused with `not_completed` | | | Critical |
| TC-REP-004 | Eligibility | No transcript | Completed but transcript null | Refused with `no_transcript` | | | Critical |
| TC-REP-005 | Eligibility | **DB enforces it too** | Insert a report via PostgREST for a non-consented call | **Refused by `trg_screening_reports_preconditions`** | | | Critical |
| TC-REP-006 | **Freeze** | `ai_*` immutable | `POST /api/screening-reports/{id}/review {"ai_summary_text":"changed"}` | **400** "The original AI extraction cannot be edited." | | | Critical |
| TC-REP-007 | Freeze | DB trigger enforces it | Direct PostgREST update of `ai_interest_level` | Refused by `trg_screening_reports_protect_ai` | | | Critical |
| TC-REP-008 | Correction | Human edit recorded | Change `expected_ctc`, save | Current value changes; `ai_expected_ctc` unchanged; `corrected_fields` contains `expected_ctc` | | | Critical |
| TC-REP-009 | Correction | Reviewer recorded | Save a correction | `reviewed_by` / `reviewed_at` set | | | High |
| TC-REP-010 | Correction | Both versions visible | Open a corrected report | The UI shows what the AI said **and** what a human changed | | | Critical |
| TC-REP-011 | Uncertainty | Flagged fields surfaced | Generate on an ambiguous transcript | `uncertain_fields` are highlighted for review first | | | High |
| TC-REP-012 | Regenerate | Replaces | Regenerate | Still exactly one report (UNIQUE on `screening_call_id`) | | | High |
| TC-REP-013 | Regenerate | **Corrections are lost** | Correct a field, then regenerate | Confirm the UI warns before discarding human corrections | | | High |
| TC-REP-014 | Perms | **Viewer can read** | As viewer, GET | Allowed | | | High |
| TC-REP-015 | Perms | Viewer cannot generate | As viewer, POST | 403 | | | Critical |
| TC-REP-016 | Perms | Viewer cannot review | As viewer, POST review | 403 | | | Critical |
| TC-REP-017 | AI | Not configured | Unset the key | Friendly message; **the transcript is still readable** | | | Critical |
| TC-REP-018 | AI | Invalid output | Force bad JSON | `invalid_output`; **no report written** | | | Critical |
| TC-REP-019 | Bounds | CTC negative | Try `expected_ctc = -1` | DB CHECK refuses | | | Medium |
| TC-REP-020 | Bounds | Notice > 365 | Try 400 | DB CHECK refuses | | | Medium |
| TC-REP-021 | Enum | Interest level | Values limited to high/medium/low/unclear | Invalid rejected | | | Medium |
| TC-REP-022 | Enum | Location acceptance | accepted/rejected/unclear | Invalid rejected | | | Medium |
| TC-REP-023 | Tenant | Cross-tenant | Review org B's report | 404 | | | Critical |
| TC-REP-024 | Pipeline | Review flag on the board | A report awaiting review | The pipeline card shows "screening report to review" | | | High |

## 16.7 Known Risks — M13

1. **Regenerating discards human corrections** (the row is replaced). Verify the
   UI warns first — this is the single most likely data-loss path in the module.
2. `uncertain_fields` is model-supplied; it is advisory and should not gate.
3. `not_completed` / `no_transcript` messages are shorter than the consent one —
   confirm they are still actionable.

---

# 17. MODULE M14 — Structured Evaluations & Next Action

## 17.1 Purpose

One uniform view of *how the candidate did* at every judged stage, merged from
four different underlying sources, plus a **suggested** next step that a human
must click.

## 17.2 The read model — five sections, four sources

| Section | Source(s) |
| --- | --- |
| AI Screening Call | `screening_calls` + `screening_reports` (M12/M13) |
| Phone Interview | `interviews` (mode `phone`) + `interview_feedback` **and** `application_evaluations` (for calls held off-system) |
| Video Interview | `interviews` (mode `video`) + `interview_feedback` **and** `application_evaluations` |
| Written Assessment | `application_evaluations` only |
| Director Round | `application_evaluations` only |

**Merging, not copying, is the whole point.** A second log would be invisible to
the screening-report screen and the interview scheduler, so a call recorded in one
place would be missing from the other.

**Scores are scaled to 1–10 for display.** `interview_feedback` stores **1–5**,
`application_evaluations` stores **1–10**, and screening reports had no score at
all until migration 0022. One column of badges must mean one thing, so the scale
is normalised for display and the native value is kept alongside.

`MANUAL_EVALUATION_STAGES` (stages that accept a manually logged entry):
`phone_interview`, `video_interview`, `written_assessment`, `director_round`.
**`ai_screening_call` is not manually loggable** — it comes from the call.

## 17.3 Verdict and thresholds (`lib/evaluation/verdict.ts`)

`EVALUATION_STATUSES = pass | fail | needs_review`, with tones
success / error / **warning**. `ROUND_SCORE_MAX = 10`, `RESUME_SCORE_MAX = 100`.
`MAX_LIST_ITEMS = 4` highlights.

## 17.4 Next Action (`lib/evaluation/nextAction.ts`) — a suggestion, never an act

> Nothing here moves a stage, rejects anyone, or writes a row. It renders as a
> labelled chip with a button the recruiter must click, and clicking goes through
> the same confirmation the move-to-stage control already uses.

| Kind | When | Behaviour |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `stop` | the last round failed | suggests ending it |
| `proceed` | passed, and the job has a further stage | target = the next stage in the **effective** list |
| `hire` | passed the last configured round | suggests Hired |
| `awaiting` | nothing judged yet | no button |
| `none` | already hired/rejected/withdrawn | no button |

`visibleStages` is the application's **effective** stage list
(`lib/applications/effectiveStages.ts`), so a job that switched Video Interview
off never gets "Proceed to Video Interview" suggested.

## 17.5 UI Components

`EvaluationPanel` (375 lines) + `EvaluationEntryForm` on `/applications/[id]`;
`EvaluationVerdict` and `NextActionBanner` components.
Fields: **Score (1-10)**, **Outcome** (pass/fail/pending), **Summary**
(placeholder "What happened, and what you concluded."), occurrence date.

## 17.6 API Flow

| API | Method | Auth | Errors |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `/api/applications/[id]/evaluations` | POST | owner/admin/recruiter | 400 validation; 404 application; **refused for a stage this application's job does not have**; 400 "Could not save this entry." |
| `/api/applications/[id]/evaluations/[entryId]` | PATCH | owner/admin/recruiter | Only rows in `application_evaluations` are editable — **a screening report or interview feedback is not**; 404 "That entry could not be found." |

## 17.7 Database Flow

`application_evaluations (id, organization_id, application_id,
stage_key CHECK IN ('phone_interview','video_interview','written_assessment','director_round'),
occurred_at, score CHECK 1..10, outcome evaluation_outcome DEFAULT 'pending',
summary, logged_by, created_at, updated_at)`

`occurred_at` is **when the thing happened**, which is not when it was logged — a
recruiter writing up Friday's call on Monday needs the list ordered by the call.

Triggers: `trg_application_evaluations_tenant`, `trg_application_evaluations_touch`.

An `evaluations_complete` automation trigger fires when the last outstanding
evaluation is logged.

## 17.8 Test Cases

| Test Case ID | Feature | Test Scenario | Steps | Expected Result | Actual | Status | Priority |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| TC-EVAL-001 | Log | Phone interview entry | Log score 8, outcome pass, summary | Row appears in the Phone Interview section | | | Critical |
| TC-EVAL-002 | Log | All four manual stages | Log one each | All appear in the right sections | | | High |
| TC-EVAL-003 | Log | **Stage not on this job** | Log a Written Assessment on a job that disabled it | **Refused** with a clear reason | | | Critical |
| TC-EVAL-004 | Log | AI screening not manually loggable | Try to log an `ai_screening_call` entry | Refused | | | High |
| TC-EVAL-005 | Log | Score out of range | Score 11, then 0 | DB CHECK 1..10 refuses both | | | High |
| TC-EVAL-006 | Log | Score optional | Log with outcome only | Accepted (`score` is nullable) | | | Medium |
| TC-EVAL-007 | Log | Invalid outcome | `{"outcome":"maybe"}` | Rejected | | | High |
| TC-EVAL-008 | Log | Backdated entry | Set `occurred_at` to last Friday | Listed by **occurrence**, not by creation | | | High |
| TC-EVAL-009 | Log | Viewer denied | Viewer POSTs | 403 | | | Critical |
| TC-EVAL-010 | Edit | Correct an entry | PATCH an `application_evaluations` row | Saved; `updated_at` touched | | | High |
| TC-EVAL-011 | Edit | **Cannot edit a screening report through here** | PATCH with a `screening_reports` id | 404 "That entry could not be found." | | | Critical |
| TC-EVAL-012 | Edit | Cannot edit interview feedback through here | PATCH with an `interview_feedback` id | 404 | | | Critical |
| TC-EVAL-013 | **Merge** | Interview feedback appears | Submit feedback on a phone interview | It shows in the Phone Interview section **without** being logged manually | | | Critical |
| TC-EVAL-014 | Merge | Screening report appears | Generate a report | It shows in the AI Screening Call section | | | Critical |
| TC-EVAL-015 | **Scaling** | 1–5 becomes 1–10 | Submit interview feedback with rating 4 | Displayed as 8/10 (scaled) with the native value preserved | | | Critical |
| TC-EVAL-016 | Scaling | 1–10 unchanged | Log an evaluation of 8 | Displayed as 8/10 | | | High |
| TC-EVAL-017 | Next action | `awaiting` | Fresh application, nothing judged | Chip says awaiting; **no button** | | | High |
| TC-EVAL-018 | Next action | `proceed` | Pass a round with a further stage configured | "Proceed to {next}" targeting the **next effective** stage | | | Critical |
| TC-EVAL-019 | Next action | **Skips disabled stages** | Disable Video Interview, pass Phone | Suggests **Written Assessment**, not Video | | | Critical |
| TC-EVAL-020 | Next action | `hire` | Pass the last configured round | Suggests Hired | | | Critical |
| TC-EVAL-021 | Next action | `stop` | Fail a round | Suggests ending it | | | High |
| TC-EVAL-022 | Next action | `none` | Already hired | No suggestion, no button | | | High |
| TC-EVAL-023 | **Next action never acts** | Load an application with a `proceed` suggestion and do nothing | **The stage does not move by itself**; no row is written | | | Critical |
| TC-EVAL-024 | Next action | Clicking confirms | Click the button | Goes through the same confirmation as the normal stage control | | | High |
| TC-EVAL-025 | Automation | `evaluations_complete` | Log the last outstanding evaluation with an active rule | The rule fires once | | | High |
| TC-EVAL-026 | Tenant | Cross-tenant | POST to org B's application | 404 | | | Critical |

## 17.9 Known Risks — M14

1. The 1–5 → 1–10 scaling is display-only. Anyone reading the database directly
   will see two scales; make sure exports and analytics agree with the screen.
2. Only `application_evaluations` rows are editable. A wrong interview rating must
   be corrected in the interview feedback, not here — confirm the UI makes that
   obvious rather than showing an inert edit control.

# 18. MODULE M15 — Pipeline Board & SLA

## 18.1 Purpose

The Kanban view of every active application, coloured by how long each has been
sitting still. Answers "where is everyone, and who is stuck?".

**Who uses it:** Owner/Admin/Recruiter can move cards and ask the pipeline AI;
Owner/Admin can edit SLA targets; Viewer reads. A **Recruiter sees their own plus
unassigned**.

## 18.2 Design decisions the tester should know

- **Buttons/dropdowns, not drag-and-drop.** Deliberate: a dropdown is
  keyboard-accessible, works on a phone, and cannot fire from a mis-drag. Moving
  someone through a hiring pipeline should be deliberate.
- **Terminal outcomes are exit counts, not columns.** Otherwise the board fills
  with finished work and stops being a work queue.
- **SLA accent is a left rule**, not a filled background — readable, and it keeps
  the flat card style.

## 18.3 SLA model (`lib/pipeline/sla.ts`)

Default target days per stage:

| Stage | Default target |
| --- | --- |
| applied | 2 |
| shortlisted | 3 |
| ai_screening_call | 3 |
| phone_interview | 5 |
| video_interview | 7 |
| written_assessment | 7 |
| director_round | 7 |
| hired / rejected / withdrawn | not tracked |

`AT_RISK_THRESHOLD = 0.75` — "at risk" once 75% of the target has elapsed.

Statuses: `ok` | `at_risk` | `breached` | `not_tracked`.

**An absent `pipeline_sla_config` row means "use the default", never "no SLA"** —
a board with no aging at all would defeat the point of the module. Terminal stages
return `not_tracked`: showing a hired candidate as "12 days overdue" would be
noise that trains people to ignore the indicator.

## 18.4 UI Components

| UI Component | Purpose | User Action | Expected Result |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `<h1>` | Job name or "Pipeline" | — | — |
| Card count line | "{n} applications on the board" | — | Recruiters also see "· yours and unassigned" |
| `SlaSettings` | Stage targets | Click (owner/admin) | Editable target-days rows |
| Job filter buttons | Scope the board | Click | `/pipeline/{jobId}`; "All jobs" resets. **First 12 jobs only** |
| `AskPipelineAI` | Prioritisation help | Ask | owner/admin/recruiter only |
| `PipelineBoard` columns | Stages | — | One column per board stage |
| Card | One application | Click the name | → `/applications/{id}` |
| Card: match tag | `{n}% match` | — | Only when a score exists |
| Card: SLA label | Aging | — | Coloured by `slaColor(status)` |
| Card: **"screening report to review"** | Attention signal | Click | → the report page |
| Card: "Move to…" select | Stage move | Choose | `PATCH /api/applications/{id}` |
| Exit counts | rejected / withdrawn | — | Counts, not columns |
| `EmptyState` / `ErrorState` / `SkeletonRows` | States | — | Distinct |

## 18.5 Complete User Flow

```
Step 1  Recruiter opens /pipeline
Step 2  getBoard({organizationId, viewerRole, viewerId, jobId})
        - RLS + role scoping (recruiter → own + unassigned)
        - per-card SLA assessed from days-in-stage vs the configured target
Step 3  Board renders; overdue cards carry a coloured left rule
Step 4  Recruiter picks "Move to…" → Shortlisted on a card
Step 5  PATCH /api/applications/{id} {stage}
        (the SAME endpoint the detail page uses — one rule, one code path)
Step 6  Everything in §13.5 happens: history, audit, notify, candidate message,
        automations
Step 7  router.refresh(); the card moves columns
```

## 18.6 API Flow

| API | Method | Auth | Errors |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `/api/pipeline/sla` | GET | any member | — |
| `/api/pipeline/sla` | PUT | **owner/admin** | 400 validation; 400 "Could not save those targets." |
| `/api/pipeline/ai-action` | POST | owner/admin/**recruiter** | 400 "Could not read the pipeline." |
| Card moves reuse `/api/applications/[id]` PATCH | | | |

`pipeline_sla_config (organization_id, stage, target_days CHECK 0..365,
UNIQUE(organization_id, stage))`.

## 18.7 Test Cases

| Test Case ID | Feature | Test Scenario | Steps | Expected Result | Actual | Status | Priority |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| TC-PIPE-001 | Load | Board renders | Open `/pipeline` | One column per board stage; cards in the right columns | | | Critical |
| TC-PIPE-002 | Load | Card count | Read the subtitle | Matches the number of cards on the board | | | High |
| TC-PIPE-003 | Load | Empty board | Fresh org | Empty state, not a broken grid | | | High |
| TC-PIPE-004 | Load | Error state | Break the query | "Couldn't load the pipeline." | | | High |
| TC-PIPE-005 | Scope | **Recruiter sees own + unassigned** | As a recruiter | Subtitle says "· yours and unassigned"; other recruiters' cards absent | | | Critical |
| TC-PIPE-006 | Scope | Owner sees everything | As owner | All cards | | | Critical |
| TC-PIPE-007 | Scope | Viewer read-only | As viewer | Cards visible; **no "Move to…" select** | | | Critical |
| TC-PIPE-008 | Move | Happy path | Move a card | Card changes column; stage history recorded | | | Critical |
| TC-PIPE-009 | Move | **Terminal exits offered** | Any active card | Rejected and Withdrawn in the dropdown | | | Critical |
| TC-PIPE-010 | Move | Terminal card has no options | A rejected card | `availableTransitions` returns `[]` — no move control | | | Critical |
| TC-PIPE-011 | Move | Busy state | Move on a slow connection | Select is `disabled`; a second change does nothing | | | High |
| TC-PIPE-012 | Move | Failure surfaces | Force a 409 | `FormError` shows the reason; the card does not move | | | High |
| TC-PIPE-013 | Move | Side effects fire | Move with an active automation and template | Both fire — the board uses the same endpoint as the detail page | | | Critical |
| TC-PIPE-014 | **SLA** | Within target | Card 1 day into a 3-day stage | `ok`, **no accent** | | | Critical |
| TC-PIPE-015 | SLA | At risk | 2.5 days into a 3-day stage (≥75%) | `at_risk` with a warning accent | | | Critical |
| TC-PIPE-016 | SLA | Breached | 5 days into a 3-day stage | `breached` with an error accent, "{n} days overdue" | | | Critical |
| TC-PIPE-017 | SLA | **Terminal not tracked** | A hired card | `not_tracked` — **never** "12 days overdue" | | | Critical |
| TC-PIPE-018 | SLA | **Absent config uses defaults** | Org with no `pipeline_sla_config` rows | Defaults apply (applied 2, shortlisted 3, …) — **not** "no SLA" | | | Critical |
| TC-PIPE-019 | SLA | Configured overrides default | Set applied = 10 | A 5-day-old applied card is `ok` | | | High |
| TC-PIPE-020 | SLA | Target 0 | Set 0 for a stage | Everything in it is immediately breached (0..365 is allowed) | | | Medium |
| TC-PIPE-021 | SLA | Target 366 | Try to set it | Rejected (CHECK 0..365) | | | Medium |
| TC-PIPE-022 | SLA | **Owner/Admin only** | As recruiter, open SLA settings | Read-only or hidden; `PUT` → 403 | | | Critical |
| TC-PIPE-023 | SLA | Timezone | Org in a different zone | Days-in-stage uses the **org** timezone | | | High |
| TC-PIPE-024 | Filter | By job | Click a job button | Only that job's cards; URL is `/pipeline/{jobId}` | | | High |
| TC-PIPE-025 | Filter | **Only 12 jobs shown** | Org with 20 jobs | 12 buttons; verify the other 8 are reachable another way | | | Medium |
| TC-PIPE-026 | Filter | All jobs | Click "All jobs" | Full board | | | Medium |
| TC-PIPE-027 | Attention | Screening report flag | Application with an unreviewed report | The card shows "screening report to review" linking to it | | | High |
| TC-PIPE-028 | Match tag | Shown only when scored | Card with no match | **No** match tag (not "0%") | | | High |
| TC-PIPE-029 | Ask AI | Happy path | Recruiter asks a prioritisation question | An answer, nothing written | | | High |
| TC-PIPE-030 | Ask AI | **Viewer cannot** | As viewer | The panel is absent; `POST` → 403 | | | Critical |
| TC-PIPE-031 | Ask AI | AI off | Unset the key | Friendly message; **the board still works** | | | Critical |
| TC-PIPE-032 | A11y | Keyboard | Tab to a move select and change it with the keyboard | Works; `aria-label` reads "Move {name} to another stage" | | | High |
| TC-PIPE-033 | Responsive | Mobile | 375px | Columns scroll horizontally; cards remain readable | | | High |
| TC-PIPE-034 | Perf | 500 cards | Large org | Renders without freezing | | | Medium |

## 18.8 Known Risks — M15

1. **Only the first 12 jobs** get a filter button. On a larger org the rest are
   unreachable from this page.
2. A card move is a full page refresh; on a big board that is a visible pause.
3. `AT_RISK_THRESHOLD` is a fraction of the target, so a 2-day target flips to
   at-risk after 1.5 days — check that reads sensibly for short stages.

# 19. MODULE M16 — Interviews & Feedback

## 19.1 Purpose

Schedule a human interview, optionally push a Google Calendar invite, and collect
**structured** feedback (a rating and a recommendation) rather than a note nobody
can aggregate.

## 19.2 Vocabulary (`lib/interviews/feedback.ts`)

| Set | Values |
| --- | --- |
| `INTERVIEW_MODES` | `video`, `phone`, `onsite` |
| `INTERVIEW_STATUSES` | `scheduled`, `completed`, `cancelled`, `no_show` |
| `RECOMMENDATIONS` | `strong_yes`, `yes`, `no`, `strong_no` |
| `FEEDBACK_DUE_HOURS` | **24** |

Duration: `duration_minutes` DEFAULT 60, DB CHECK `> 0 and <= 480`.
Feedback rating: **integer 1–5** (DB CHECK), and `UNIQUE (interview_id, submitted_by)`
— one submission per person per interview.

## 19.3 Feedback validation

> Both a rating **and** a recommendation are required. An interview that produced
> neither is not feedback — it is a note, and there is a notes field for that.

- rating: must be an **integer** 1–5 → *"Give a rating from 1 to 5."*
- recommendation: must be in the list → *"Choose a recommendation."*
- notes: optional, trimmed, truncated at **5,000** characters.

## 19.4 Feedback-overdue logic (`feedbackReminderState`)

Returns `due: false` with a **distinct reason** for every non-due case, because
chasing someone for feedback on a cancelled interview is worse than not chasing:

| Reason | When |
| --- | --- |
| `feedback_submitted` | already filed |
| `cancelled` | status is `cancelled` **or `no_show`** (a no-show has nothing to give feedback on) |
| `not_finished` | `now < scheduled_at + duration`, or an unparseable date |
| `too_soon` | finished, but less than `FEEDBACK_DUE_HOURS` ago |
| `due: true` | finished ≥24h ago with no feedback; carries `hoursOverdue` |

## 19.5 Google Calendar — the two guarantees

1. **`not_connected` is NOT an error.** It is the ordinary state when nobody has
   connected Calendar, and the caller must **carry on and save the interview**.
2. **A revoked grant also degrades to `not_connected`**, not `failed` — a revoked
   token is a connection problem, not a scheduling one.

A calendar problem comes back as a **`calendarMessage` alongside a successful
201**. Scheduling never fails because of the calendar.

`interviews.calendar_sync_status` defaults to `not_attempted` with a null
`calendar_event_id`, and that combination **must never read as a failure**.

> **Important caveat, stated in the code itself:** `lib/integrations/calendar/oauth.ts`
> is "written to Google's documented contract but **has never run against
> Google**, because no Google Cloud project exists for this product." Treat the
> live OAuth path as unverified.

## 19.6 UI Components

`/interviews` (list + `InterviewBadges`), `/interviews/[id]` (detail + `FeedbackForm`),
`ScheduleInterview` on the application page.

| UI Component | Purpose | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Date & time** | Schedule | Invalid → "That date isn't valid." |
| Mode select | video/phone/onsite | — |
| Interviewer select | Assign | Must be an active member |
| Duration | Minutes | 1–480 |
| Location / Meeting URL / Notes | Context | Location is for onsite |
| **Your feedback** card | Submit | Rating 1–5, recommendation, notes |
| **All feedback** card | Panel view | Several people may file on one interview |
| Cancel + reason | placeholder "e.g. Candidate withdrew, interviewer unavailable" | A cancelled interview is part of the record |

## 19.7 Complete User Flow

```
SCHEDULE
Step 1  Recruiter opens an application → Schedule interview
Step 2  Picks date/time, mode, interviewer, duration
Step 3  POST /api/interviews {application_id, …}
        requireRole(["owner","admin","recruiter"])
        400 "Choose an application."
        parseSchedulePayload() validates the rest
        404 when the error says "not found", else 400
Step 4  INSERT interviews (calendar_sync_status 'not_attempted')
        triggers: trg_interviews_tenant_integrity, trg_interviews_touch
Step 5  Calendar attempted IF connected:
          connected     → event created, calendar_event_id stored
          not_connected → NOT an error; interview saved, sync stays not_attempted
          revoked       → ALSO not_connected
          real failure  → calendar_error recorded, interview still saved
Step 6  RESPONSE 201 {data, calendarMessage?}
Step 7  Candidate message fires from HERE (phone/video interview scheduled),
        because this is where a TIME exists — a stage move has none

FEEDBACK
Step 8  Interviewer opens /interviews/{id}
Step 9  Rating 1–5 + recommendation + optional notes → Submit
Step 10 POST /api/interviews/{id}/feedback
        404 interview
        403 "Only the assigned interviewer can submit feedback for this one."
        409 "This interview was cancelled, so there's nothing to give feedback on."
        400 parseFeedback errors
Step 11 INSERT interview_feedback (UNIQUE interview_id + submitted_by)
Step 12 DB TRIGGER trg_feedback_completes_interview
        → the interview is marked completed, so status and feedback can never
          disagree
Step 13 Automation trigger `interview_completed` fires
Step 14 The entry appears in the application's Evaluation panel, scaled to 1–10
```

## 19.8 API Flow

| API | Method | Auth | Errors |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `/api/interviews` | GET | any member | 400 invalid status filter; 400 "Could not load interviews." |
| `/api/interviews` | POST | owner/admin/recruiter | 400 "Choose an application."; schedule validation; 404/400 |
| `/api/interviews/[id]` | GET | any member | 404 |
| `/api/interviews/[id]` | PATCH | owner/admin/recruiter | 400 "Invalid status." / "That date isn't valid." / "That interviewer is not a member of this team." / "No valid fields provided."; 404 |
| `/api/interviews/[id]/feedback` | POST | owner/admin/recruiter | 404; **403 not the assigned interviewer**; **409 cancelled**; 400 validation |

| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| TC-INT-001 | Schedule | Happy path | Schedule a video interview for tomorrow | 201; appears in `/interviews` and on the application | | | Critical |
| TC-INT-002 | Schedule | **Calendar not connected** | Calendar disconnected, schedule | **Interview is saved**; `calendar_sync_status = not_attempted`; **this does not look like a failure** | | | Critical |
| TC-INT-003 | Schedule | **Calendar revoked** | Revoke the Google grant, schedule | Degrades to `not_connected`, **not** `failed`; interview saved | | | Critical |
| TC-INT-004 | Schedule | Calendar connected | Connect, schedule | Event created; `calendar_event_id` stored | | | High |
| TC-INT-005 | Schedule | Calendar error | Force a provider failure | `calendarMessage` returned **alongside a 201**; interview saved | | | Critical |
| TC-INT-006 | Schedule | No application | POST without one | "Choose an application." | | | High |
| TC-INT-007 | Schedule | Invalid date | `scheduled_at = "tomorrow"` | "That date isn't valid." | | | High |
| TC-INT-008 | Schedule | Past date | Yesterday | Document the behaviour (there is no explicit future-date rule in the code) | | | Medium |
| TC-INT-009 | Schedule | Duration 0 | Set 0 | DB CHECK `> 0` refuses | | | High |
| TC-INT-010 | Schedule | Duration 481 | Set 481 | DB CHECK `<= 480` refuses | | | High |
| TC-INT-011 | Schedule | Cross-tenant interviewer | Org B's user id | "That interviewer is not a member of this team." | | | Critical |
| TC-INT-012 | Schedule | Viewer denied | Viewer POSTs | 403 | | | Critical |
| TC-INT-013 | Reschedule | Change the time | PATCH `scheduled_at` | Updated; calendar re-synced if connected | | | High |
| TC-INT-014 | Reassign | Change interviewer | PATCH `interviewer_id` | Updated | | | High |
| TC-INT-015 | Cancel | With a reason | Set status cancelled + reason | Recorded; the interview stays in the record | | | High |
| TC-INT-016 | Cancel | Invalid status | `{"status":"postponed"}` | 400 "Invalid status." | | | Medium |
| TC-INT-017 | **Feedback** | Happy path | Rating 4, recommendation `yes`, notes | 201; **the interview becomes `completed` via the trigger** | | | Critical |
| TC-INT-018 | Feedback | **Status/feedback cannot disagree** | Submit feedback on a `scheduled` interview | The trigger marks it completed automatically | | | Critical |
| TC-INT-019 | Feedback | Missing rating | Recommendation only | "Give a rating from 1 to 5." | | | Critical |
| TC-INT-020 | Feedback | Missing recommendation | Rating only | "Choose a recommendation." | | | Critical |
| TC-INT-021 | Feedback | Rating 0 / 6 | Try both | Rejected both ways | | | High |
| TC-INT-022 | Feedback | Non-integer rating | 3.5 | Rejected (`Number.isInteger`) | | | High |
| TC-INT-023 | Feedback | Invalid recommendation | `"maybe"` | Rejected | | | High |
| TC-INT-024 | Feedback | 6,000-char notes | Paste | Truncated at 5,000, not rejected | | | Low |
| TC-INT-025 | Feedback | **Wrong interviewer** | Someone who is not the assigned interviewer submits | **403** "Only the assigned interviewer can submit feedback for this one." | | | Critical |
| TC-INT-026 | Feedback | **Cancelled interview** | Submit on a cancelled one | **409** "This interview was cancelled, so there's nothing to give feedback on." | | | Critical |
| TC-INT-027 | Feedback | Duplicate submission | The same person submits twice | Blocked by `UNIQUE (interview_id, submitted_by)` | | | High |
| TC-INT-028 | Feedback | Panel | Two different interviewers file on one interview | Both stored; "All feedback" shows both | | | High |
| TC-INT-029 | Feedback | Viewer denied | Viewer POSTs | 403 | | | Critical |
| TC-INT-030 | Feedback | **Appears in Evaluations** | File a rating of 4 on a phone interview | Shows in the Phone Interview section scaled to **8/10** | | | Critical |
| TC-INT-031 | Overdue | Not finished | Interview still in the future | `not_finished`, **no reminder** | | | High |
| TC-INT-032 | Overdue | Too soon | Finished 2 hours ago | `too_soon`, no reminder | | | High |
| TC-INT-033 | Overdue | Due | Finished 25 hours ago, no feedback | `due: true`, `hoursOverdue = 1` | | | Critical |
| TC-INT-034 | Overdue | **Cancelled not chased** | Cancelled interview, 3 days old | `cancelled`, **no reminder** | | | Critical |
| TC-INT-035 | Overdue | **No-show not chased** | `no_show`, 3 days old | `cancelled` reason, no reminder | | | Critical |
| TC-INT-036 | Overdue | Feedback filed | Filed after 30 hours | `feedback_submitted`, no further reminder | | | High |
| TC-INT-037 | Candidate msg | **Fires from scheduling** | Active `video_interview_scheduled` template, schedule one | The candidate message goes out **with a time in it** | | | Critical |
| TC-INT-038 | Dashboard | Interviews today | Schedule one for today in the org timezone | The `interviewsToday` tile increments | | | High |
| TC-INT-039 | Automation | `interview_completed` | Active rule, submit feedback | The rule fires once | | | High |
| TC-INT-040 | Tenant | Cross-tenant | GET/PATCH org B's interview | 404 | | | Critical |

## 19.10 Known Risks — M16

1. **The Google OAuth exchange has never run against Google.** The code says so
   explicitly. Anything in the live Calendar path is unverified — plan for it.
2. There appears to be **no past-date guard** on `scheduled_at`. Confirm and file
   if a past interview can be scheduled.
3. Only the **assigned interviewer** may submit feedback — if the field is left
   blank, check who (if anyone) can file.

# 20. MODULE M17 — Clients & Client Submission

> **Agency mode only.** For an in-house organization (`agency_mode = false`)
> the Clients nav item, the client filters and the submission card are all
> hidden. Do not file "Clients is missing" as a bug on an in-house workspace.

## 20.1 Purpose

The companies an agency recruits **for**. Tracks the relationship, the account
manager, the expected feedback turnaround, and the record of exactly what was
sent about a candidate.

## 20.2 UI Components

### `/clients` and `/clients/[id]`
"Add a client" (`NewClientForm`), the client list, the detail page with
**Submissions**, `ClientActivityPanel`, and an AI activity summary.

`clients (id, organization_id, name UNIQUE per org, contacts jsonb [{name,email,phone,role}],
feedback_sla_days DEFAULT 3 CHECK 0..90, account_manager_id, notes, archived_at)`

**Name is unique per organization** — a duplicate would split a client's history
in two and quietly break their metrics.

### `/applications/[id]/submission` — `SubmissionPanel`
| UI Component | Purpose | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Draft a submission** / **Submit again** | Generate text | `POST` — returns text only, **writes and sends nothing** |
| Summary textarea | Edit | placeholder "Write the introduction yourself, or draft it with AI above." |
| **Already submitted** notice | Guard | 409 on re-draft without acknowledgement |
| **Send** | The explicit human act | `PUT` — records verbatim and opens the feedback clock |

## 20.3 The propose/send split — the core rule

- **`POST`** drafts. *"This endpoint only ever returns text. It writes nothing and
  sends nothing."* Facts are gathered server-side, so the draft cannot be steered
  by the caller.
- **`PUT`** sends. It records **what was actually sent, verbatim**, in
  `client_feedback_events.submission_text`. If it is ever disputed, the record must
  be what was sent, not what we would generate today.
- **Actual email delivery is Module 15's job.** Until then `PUT` **records** the
  submission rather than transmitting it. Do not report "no email arrived" as a
  submission bug without checking the message log.

## 20.4 Complete User Flow

```
Step 1  Recruiter opens /applications/{id}/submission
Step 2  Clicks Draft a submission
Step 3  POST /api/applications/{id}/submission
        requireRole(["owner","admin","recruiter"])
        404 "Application not found."
        409 if already submitted and not acknowledged
        generateClientSubmission() with server-gathered facts
        → returns TEXT ONLY
Step 4  Recruiter edits the text freely
Step 5  Clicks Send
Step 6  PUT /api/applications/{id}/submission {summary}
        400 "There's nothing to send — write or generate a summary first."
        409 "This job has no client attached, so there's nobody to submit to."
Step 7  INSERT client_feedback_events
          client_id, application_id, requested_at = now(),
          submission_text (VERBATIM), submitted_by
          CHECK feedback_response_after_request
Step 8  The feedback clock starts, measured against clients.feedback_sla_days
Step 9  When the client responds, outcome is recorded:
          interview | reject | hold | offer | other, plus response_notes
Step 10 Turnaround feeds Analytics' "Average Client Feedback Time"
```

## 20.5 API Flow

| API | Method | Auth | Errors |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `/api/clients` | GET | **any member incl. Viewer** | 400 "Could not load clients." |
| `/api/clients` | POST | owner/admin/recruiter | 409 "A client with that name already exists."; 400 |
| `/api/clients/[id]` | GET | any member | 404 |
| `/api/clients/[id]` | PATCH | owner/admin/recruiter | 400 "That account manager is not a member of this team."; **409 duplicate name (23505)**; 404 |
| `/api/clients/[id]` | DELETE | **owner/admin** | **ARCHIVES** — a real delete would orphan jobs (`jobs.client_id` is ON DELETE SET NULL); 404 "Client not found, or already archived." |
| `/api/clients/[id]/ai-action` | POST | any member | Internal activity summary; `saved:false` |
| `/api/applications/[id]/submission` | GET | any member | — |
| `/api/applications/[id]/submission` | POST | owner/admin/recruiter | 404; 409 already submitted |
| `/api/applications/[id]/submission` | PUT | owner/admin/recruiter | 400 empty summary; **409 no client attached**; 400 "Could not record that submission." |

## 20.6 Test Cases

| Test Case ID | Feature | Test Scenario | Steps | Expected Result | Actual | Status | Priority |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| TC-CLI-001 | Mode | **In-house hides Clients** | Set `agency_mode = false` | No Clients nav item, no client filters, no submission card | | | Critical |
| TC-CLI-002 | Mode | Agency shows Clients | `agency_mode = true` | All of the above appear | | | Critical |
| TC-CLI-003 | Create | Happy path | Add a client with contacts | 201; appears in the list | | | Critical |
| TC-CLI-004 | Create | **Duplicate name** | Add the same name twice | **409** "A client with that name already exists." | | | Critical |
| TC-CLI-005 | Create | Same name, different org | Create it in org B | Allowed (uniqueness is per organization) | | | High |
| TC-CLI-006 | Create | Empty name | Submit blank | Rejected (DB CHECK `length(btrim(name)) > 0`) | | | High |
| TC-CLI-007 | Create | SLA 91 days | Set 91 | Rejected (CHECK 0..90) | | | Medium |
| TC-CLI-008 | Create | SLA 0 | Set 0 | Allowed | | | Low |
| TC-CLI-009 | Create | Viewer denied | Viewer POSTs | 403 | | | Critical |

| TC-CLI-011 | Archive | Owner archives | Archive a client | `archived_at` set | | | High |

| TC-CLI-016 | Submission | Draft cannot be steered | Try to pass extra prompt text | Facts come from the server; caller input does not reach the prompt | | | Critical |

| TC-CLI-018 | Submission | Empty send | Send with a blank summary | 400 "There's nothing to send — write or generate a summary first." | | | High |
| TC-CLI-019 | Submission | **No client on the job** | Send for a job with no `client_id` | **409** "This job has no client attached, so there's nobody to submit to." | | | Critical |
| TC-CLI-020 | Submission | Already submitted | Draft again after sending | **409** with an acknowledgement path; the UI says "Already submitted" | | | High |
| TC-CLI-021 | Submission | Verbatim record | Send, then change the client's details | The stored `submission_text` is **unchanged** | | | Critical |
| TC-CLI-022 | Submission | Feedback clock | Send | `requested_at` set; turnaround measured against `feedback_sla_days` | | | High |
| TC-CLI-023 | Submission | Response recorded | Record outcome `interview` + notes | `responded_at` set; CHECK `responded_at >= requested_at` | | | High |
| TC-CLI-024 | Submission | Invalid outcome | `{"outcome":"ghosted"}` | Rejected (CHECK) | | | Medium |
| TC-CLI-025 | Submission | Viewer denied | Viewer POSTs/PUTs | 403 | | | Critical |
| TC-CLI-026 | AI summary | Client activity | Click on the client detail page | Summary appears; `saved:false` | | | Medium |
| TC-CLI-027 | AI summary | AI off | Unset the key | Friendly message; the page still works | | | High |
| TC-CLI-028 | Tenant | Cross-tenant client | GET org B's client | 404 | | | Critical |
| TC-CLI-029 | Analytics | Feedback time | Complete a submission cycle | It appears in Analytics' client feedback metric | | | High |

## 20.7 Known Risks — M17

1. **A submission does not actually email the client.** It records the text. If a
   tester expects a delivered email, they will file a false bug — and if a
   *recruiter* expects it, that is a genuine product risk.
2. `jobs.client_id` was a forward stub with no FK until Module 12; verify no
   orphan `client_id` values survive on old rows.
3. Contacts are free-form JSONB — no email validation on a client contact.

---

# 21. MODULE M18 — Automation Engine

## 21.1 Purpose

Rules that do work automatically: *when X happens, if Y is true, do Z.* Explicitly
**explainable and predictable** — conditions are evaluated by a pure function,
never by AI. An LLM may *draft* a rule; it never *runs* one.

**Who uses it:** **Owner/Admin only** create, edit, activate, delete, approve and
run the scheduler. Every role can **read** rules, the run history, and the
approval queue.

## 21.2 Trigger vocabulary (`lib/automations/catalog.ts`)

| Trigger | Label | Fires | Mode |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `application_stage_changed` | An application enters a stage | on stage change | session |
| `application_created` | An application is created | including by bulk intake | session |
| `screening_call_completed` | A screening call completes | **Bolna webhook** | **service** |
| `screening_report_created` | A screening report is generated | | session |
| `interview_completed` | An interview is completed | on feedback submission | session |
| `evaluations_complete` | Every evaluation is in | on the last one logged | session |
| `onboarding_document_uploaded` | A new hire uploads a document | | session |
| `onboarding_completed` | Onboarding is marked complete | | session |
| `time_elapsed_in_stage` | An application has sat too long | **the scheduler** | **service** |

## 21.3 Condition fields and operators

**Fields:** `stage`, `match_score`, `candidate_has_phone`, `candidate_has_email`,
`screening_consent_confirmed`, `job_has_screening_questions`, `days_in_stage`,
`interest_level`, `application_source`, `candidate_has_resume`, `days_since_applied`,
`assigned_recruiter_present`, `job_is_open`, `evaluation_outcome`.

**Operators:** `eq`, `neq`, `gt`, `gte`, `lt`, `lte`, `is_true`, `is_false` —
constrained per field by `FIELD_OPERATORS` (e.g. `stage` only accepts `eq`/`neq`).

## 21.4 Actions

| Action | Requires integration | Runs in |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `start_screening_call` | **bolna** | session only |
| `generate_screening_report` | — | session only |
| `calculate_match` | — | session only |
| `move_to_stage` | — | both |
| `notify_recruiter` | — (in-app needs nothing) | both |
| `add_note` | — | both |
| `send_candidate_email` | **email** | both |
| `assign_recruiter` | — | both |
| `call_n8n_webhook` | **n8n** | both |
| `send_templated_message` | **computed from the template's channels** | both |

**Three session-only actions.** `start_screening_call`, `generate_screening_report`
and `calculate_match` each build their own session-bound Supabase client
internally, so under the service role they would be denied by RLS. Activation
**refuses** a rule pairing them with a sessionless trigger — the same honesty rule,
one level finer.

**`send_templated_message` deliberately has no fixed integration** — an email-only
template needs `email`, a WhatsApp one needs `whatsapp`. Naming `email` would block
a WhatsApp-only organization from activating a rule that never touches email.

## 21.5 Guardrails

| Guardrail | Rule |
| --- | --- |
| **Kill switch** | `organizations.automations_enabled` is checked **before a single rule is loaded**. It does **not** pause the rules — their status is untouched, so switching back on restores exactly what was there. |
| **Draft by default** | `POST /api/automations` never reads `status` from the body. Activation is always a separate act. |
| **Consequential actions** | `start_screening_call`, `generate_screening_report`, `calculate_match`, `send_candidate_email`, `send_templated_message` — cost money or contact a person |
| **Approval MANDATORY** | any rule containing `send_candidate_email` — the wording lives in this codebase and nobody in the org ever read it |
| **Approval on by default, switchable off** | `send_templated_message` — an Owner/Admin wrote *and explicitly activated* that template, so the review already happened over the words themselves |
| **Daily cap** | optional; over it the run is **BLOCKED and the rule pauses itself** |
| **Integration health** | a missing integration is **BLOCKED**, not failed |
| **Dedupe** | `UNIQUE (automation_id, application_id, dedupe_key)`; the key is normally `<stage>:<stage_entered_at>` |
| **Optimistic concurrency** | `expected_version` vs the row's `version`; a mismatch → 409 |
| **Approval expiry** | `expires_at` defaults to now + 7 days — a proposal about a candidate goes stale |
| **Test runs are inert** | `POST /api/automations/[id]/test` **does not call `dispatch()` at all**, and writes no run record — a test must not consume the dedupe key |

## 21.6 Dispatch order (`lib/automations/engine.ts`)

```
1. Check the ORGANIZATION KILL SWITCH        (before any rule is loaded)
2. Load ACTIVE rules for this trigger        (drafts and paused never execute)
3. Build the evaluation context once
4. CLAIM THE RUN — insert the run row FIRST.
   The unique index makes concurrent triggers safe. A conflict means this
   occasion is already handled: stop. No call, no AI spend.
5. Evaluate conditions — not matching is a SKIP, not a failure
6. Check the DAILY CAP — over it is BLOCKED, and the rule pauses itself
7. Check integration health — missing is BLOCKED, not failed
8. If approval is required: PROPOSE and stop. Nothing is executed.
9. Otherwise execute actions, each recording its own outcome
```

**Claiming before evaluating means a skipped run also consumes the dedupe key.**
Deliberate: a rule that did not match on this stage-entry will not match on a
re-delivery either, and the run history then shows **every occasion the engine
considered** — which is what makes "why didn't my automation fire?" answerable.

## 21.7 The scheduler (`lib/automations/sweep.ts`)

- `vercel.json` runs `GET /api/automations/sweep` **hourly** (`0 * * * *`).
- **Safe to run every hour** because the dedupe key contains **no time**:
  `time_elapsed_in_stage:<stage>:<stage_entered_at>`. Every sweep computes the
  same string for the same stale application, so the first claims the run and
  every later one is rejected. **One action per stage-entry, not one per sweep.**
  This is also why there is no "last reminded at" column — that would need a
  read-then-write, and the thing being raced is whether a candidate is emailed twice.
- The scan is **bounded** and the bound is **reported, never silent**: `truncated`
  reaches the UI and says how many were left.
- **A sweep that finds nothing writes a row saying so** — that is how the UI tells
  "the scheduler ran and there was nothing to do" apart from "the scheduler has
  never run", two states that look identical from an empty history and mean
  completely different things.
- `maxDuration = 300` seconds.

### The two verbs

| Verb | Caller | Auth | Scope |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **GET** | Vercel Cron | `Authorization: Bearer $CRON_SECRET`, constant compare | **every organization**, service-role client |
| **POST** | "Run the scheduler now" button | `requireRole(["owner","admin"])` | the caller's organization only, RLS applied |

The verbs are split on purpose: guessing the caller from a header would mean an
unauthenticated POST could be mistaken for a cron, and the difference between the
two paths is *this organization* versus *every organization*.

`GET` returns **503** when `CRON_SECRET` is unset ("Scheduled sweeps aren't
configured on this deployment") and **503** when no service-role key is
configured. `POST` returns **409** when the kill switch is off.

## 21.8 UI Components

| Page | Components |
| --- | --- |
| `/automations` | Rule list, **New automation**, "This automation is live", "Recent runs", `SchedulerPanel` ("Run the scheduler now"), the kill switch, "You can't create automations" for non-admins |
| `/automations/new` | `TemplatePicker` ("Start from a template"), "Describe it instead" (AI drafting, placeholder "e.g. Start AI screening for anyone entering Screening with a match above 75% who has a phone number") |
| `/automations/[id]` | `AutomationForm` (1,067 lines): **When** / **If** / **Then**, "In plain English", **Guardrails**, `ActivationPanel` ("Review & activate"), "Run history" |
| `/automations/approvals` | `ApprovalCard` — "Waiting for a decision", "Decisions already made", placeholder "Optional note — why you decided this way" |

## 21.9 Complete User Flow — AI-drafted rule to live

```
Step 1  Admin opens /automations/new → Describe it instead
Step 2  Types "Start AI screening for anyone entering Screening with a match
        above 75% who has a phone number"
Step 3  POST /api/automations/ai-action
        requireRole(["owner","admin"])
        400 "Describe the workflow you want to automate." if empty
        → draftAutomationRule()
        → THIS ENDPOINT WRITES NOTHING. It returns a proposal.
Step 4  Admin reviews the When / If / Then and the plain-English summary
Step 5  Clicks Save
Step 6  POST /api/automations
        - ALWAYS creates a DRAFT (status is not read from the body)
        - name 1..120, unique per org → 409 on collision
        - conditions validated; 422 if actions cannot be resolved
        - required_integrations computed from the actions and stored
        - approvalIsMandatory() FORCES requires_approval on regardless of
          what the client sent
        - drafted_by_ai = true
Step 7  Admin opens the rule → Review & activate
Step 8  PATCH /api/automations/{id} {status:"active", expected_version:N}
        - 409 if the version moved (someone else edited it)
        - 409 if a REQUIRED INTEGRATION IS DISCONNECTED
        - 409 if a session-only action is paired with a sessionless trigger
Step 9  Rule is live. The next matching event dispatches it.
Step 10 With approval required, the run PROPOSES and stops:
        automation_approvals row, status pending, expires in 7 days
Step 11 Admin opens /automations/approvals, reads the proposal, decides
Step 12 PATCH /api/automations/approvals/{id} {decision:"approved"}
        → THIS IS WHAT ACTUALLY RUNS THE ACTIONS, under the approver's identity
        409 if it was already decided or has expired
```

## 21.10 API Flow

| API | Method | Auth | Errors |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `/api/automations` | GET | **any member** | 500 "Could not load automations." |
| `/api/automations` | POST | **owner/admin** | 400 template/name/conditions/daily-limit; **422** unresolvable actions; **409** duplicate name |
| `/api/automations/[id]` | GET | any member | 404 |
| `/api/automations/[id]` | PATCH | owner/admin | 400 many; 422 actions; **409 version conflict**; **409 activation blocked**; 409 duplicate name; 404 |
| `/api/automations/[id]` | DELETE | owner/admin | **A real delete** — a rule is configuration, not a record; 404 |
| `/api/automations/[id]/test` | POST | owner/admin | 400 "Choose an application to test against."; 404 automation/application. **Performs no live action and writes no run** |
| `/api/automations/ai-action` | POST | owner/admin | 400 empty description. **Writes nothing** |
| `/api/automations/runs` | GET | **any member incl. Viewer** | 500 |
| `/api/automations/approvals` | GET | **any member** | 400 unknown status filter; 500 |
| `/api/automations/approvals/[id]` | PATCH | **owner/admin** | 400 `'Send a decision of "approved" or "rejected".'`; **409** already decided/expired |
| `/api/automations/switch` | PATCH | owner/admin | 400 "Send { enabled: true } or { enabled: false }."; 404 |
| `/api/automations/sweep` | GET | **CRON_SECRET** | 503 unset / 401 wrong / 503 no service key |
| `/api/automations/sweep` | POST | owner/admin | **409 kill switch off** |
